    Protected Sub Page_Load(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As System.EventArgs) Handles Me.Load

        Try

            InitForm()

            TodayConvertedDate = DTHelper.GetConvertedClientDate(Today.Year, Today.Month, Today.Day, clsCookie.CalendarType)

            If Not IsPostBack Then

                If clsCookie.CalendarType.ToString.ToLower <> "gregorian" Then
                    FillMonth()
                    FillYear()
                End If

                setLanguage()
                'SetEmployeeHirarchy()
                'Set Rpt Type
                Dim strRptType As String = Request.QueryString("rt")
                If IsNothing(strRptType) = False AndAlso strRptType.Trim <> "" Then

                    hdRptType.Value = CInt(Request.QueryString("rt"))

                    If hdRptType.Value = RptType.AbsenteesGraph Then
                        ddlCriteria.SelectedIndex = 3
                        ddlCriteria.Enabled = False
                        'strErrorText = ReportHelper.FillCriteria(ddlCriteria, ddlFrom, ddlTo, CompanyID, m_strBranchList,clsCookie.Searchby, _
                        '    boEmployee, boDepartment, boSubDepartment, boBranch, boCategory, boGrade, boGroupLevel, boDesignation, boCity, boRegion, boDivision, boEmployeeGrade, _
                        '    boManagementLevel, boSubManagementLevel, boQualificationLevel, boSubLeavingReason, boLeavingReason, boDoctor, boShift, boDiagnosis, boMedicalPolicy, _
                        '    boMedicalPolicyMaster, , , , , m_strGradeList, m_strDepartmentList, m_strDivisionList)
                        ddlFrom.ClientEnabled = True
                        ddlTo.ClientEnabled = True
                    ElseIf hdRptType.Value = RptType.AttendanceSummaryDepartment Then
                        lbl_To.Visible = False
                        txtToDate.Visible = False
                        val_To.Visible = False
                        imgToDate.Visible = False
                        pnlInclude.Visible = True
                        chkAbsent.Visible = False
                        chkEarly.Visible = False
                        chklate.Visible = False
                        chkManual.Visible = False
                        chkDivisionGroupInclude.Visible = True
                        chkOffDay.Visible = True
                        lbl_LateComingOnly.Visible = False
                        lbl_EarlyGoingOnly.Visible = False
                        lbl_IncludeAbsent.Visible = False
                        lbl_ManualEnteriesOnly.Visible = False
                        lbl_DivisionGroupInclude.Visible = True
                        lbl_IncludeOffDay.Visible = True
                        lbl_From.Text = "Date"
                        'ddlCriteria.SelectedIndex = 3
                        'ddlCriteria.Enabled = False

                        ddlFrom.ClientEnabled = True
                        ddlTo.ClientEnabled = True
                        PnlShift.Visible = True
                        gvwDetail.Visible = True
                        lbl_Shifts.Visible = True
                        'ddlCriteria1.Enabled = True
                        Call DisplayShiftDetail()
                    ElseIf hdRptType.Value = RptType.AttendanceSummaryDivision Then
                        lbl_To.Visible = False
                        txtToDate.Visible = False
                        val_To.Visible = False
                        imgToDate.Visible = False
                        lbl_From.Text = "Date"
                        'ddlCriteria.SelectedIndex = 11
                        'ddlCriteria.Enabled = False

                        ddlFrom.ClientEnabled = True
                        ddlTo.ClientEnabled = True
                        PnlShift.Visible = True
                        gvwDetail.Visible = True
                        lbl_Shifts.Visible = True
                        Call DisplayShiftDetail()
                    ElseIf hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyAttendanceDateWise _
                        Or hdRptType.Value = RptType.EmployeeDailyAttendance _
                        Or hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyAttendanceFlagWise Then
                        chklate.Visible = True
                        chkEarly.Visible = True
                        chkAbsent.Visible = True
                        chkManual.Visible = True

                        lbl_LateComingOnly.Visible = True
                        lbl_EarlyGoingOnly.Visible = True
                        lbl_IncludeAbsent.Visible = True
                        lbl_ManualEnteriesOnly.Visible = True

                        lbl_ShiftTitle.Visible = True
                        ddlShift.Visible = True
                        lbl_To.Visible = True
                        txtToDate.Visible = True
                        val_To.Visible = True
                        imgToDate.Visible = True
                        'lbl_From.Text = "Date"
                    ElseIf hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyAttendance _
                        Or hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyAttendance_Revised Then
                        chklate.Visible = True
                        chkEarly.Visible = True
                        chkAbsent.Visible = True
                        chkManual.Visible = True

                        lbl_LateComingOnly.Visible = True

                        lbl_IncludeAbsent.Visible = True
                        lbl_ManualEnteriesOnly.Visible = True

                        lbl_ShiftTitle.Visible = True
                        ddlShift.Visible = True
                        lbl_To.Visible = False
                        txtToDate.Visible = False
                        val_To.Visible = False
                        imgToDate.Visible = False
                        lbl_From.Text = "Date"

                    ElseIf hdRptType.Value = RptType.AttendanceRegister Or _
                        hdRptType.Value = RptType.AttendanceSummaryDepartmentDivision Or _
                        hdRptType.Value = RptType.AbsenteesReport Or _
                        hdRptType.Value = RptType.AttendanceRegister_Revised Or _
                        hdRptType.Value = RptType.AttendanceRegister_RevisedNew Or _
                        hdRptType.Value = RptType.EmployeeAttendnace_Customized Or _
                        hdRptType.Value = RptType.AttendanceRegisterMusterRoll Or _
                        hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyAttendanceRegister Or _
                        hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyAttendanceRegister_Stylo Then
                        lbl_ShiftTitle.Visible = True
                        ddlShift.Visible = True
                    ElseIf hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyAbsentees Or _

                        lbl_From.Text = "From Date"
                        lbl_To.Text = "To Date"
                    ElseIf hdRptType.Value = RptType.MissingScheduleOnDate Then

                        imgToDate.Visible = False
                        lbl_Shifts.Visible = False

                        ddlTo.ClientEnabled = True
                        lbl_ShiftTitle.Visible = False
                        ddlShift.Visible = False
                    ElseIf hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyAttendanceSheet Or _
                        hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyAttendanceSheetEfro Or _

                        hdRptType.Value = RptType.OvertimeYearlySummaryDepartment Or _
                        hdRptType.Value = RptType.OvertimeYearlySummaryReason Then
                        lbl_year.Visible = True
                        lbl_month.Visible = False
                        ddlyear.Visible = True

                        ddlyear.SelectedValue = TodayConvertedDate.Year
                        ddlMonth.SelectedValue = TodayConvertedDate.Month
                        lbl_From.Visible = False

                        imgToDate.Visible = False
                        lbl_DateRange.Visible = False
                        lblDateFormat.Visible = False
                    ElseIf hdRptType.Value = RptType.MonthlyHeadCountDetail Then

                        ddlCriteria1.Items.Add(New ListItem("SubDepartment", "SubDepartment"))
                    ElseIf hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyHeadCountDetailReport Then
                        lbl_year.Visible = True

                        imgFromDate.Visible = False
                        imgToDate.Visible = False
                        lbl_DateRange.Visible = False
                        lblDateFormat.Visible = False
                        ddlCriteria.SelectedValue = "Branch"
                        'strErrorText = ReportHelper.FillCriteria(ddlCriteria, ddlFrom, ddlTo, CompanyID, m_strBranchList,clsCookie.Searchby, _
                        '    boEmployee, boDepartment, boSubDepartment, boBranch, boCategory, boGrade, boGroupLevel, boDesignation, boCity, boRegion, boDivision, boEmployeeGrade, _
                        '    boManagementLevel, boSubManagementLevel, boQualificationLevel, boSubLeavingReason, boLeavingReason, boDoctor, boShift, boDiagnosis, boMedicalPolicy, _
                        '    boMedicalPolicyMaster, clsCookie.IsLeaveApprover,clsCookie.EmployeeID)
                        ddlFrom.ClientEnabled = True
                        ddlTo.ClientVisible = False
                        lbl_To.Visible = False

                    ElseIf hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyAttendanceCustomized Then

    Protected Sub FillMonth()
        Dim dtMonth As New DataTable
        dtMonth = boMonthSetup.tblMonthSetup.GetDataByCalendarType(clsCookie.CalendarType)
        ddlMonth.DataTextField = "MonthTitle"
        ddlMonth.DataValueField = "MonthNo"
        ddlMonth.DataSource = dtMonth
        ddlMonth.DataBind()
    End Sub
    Protected Sub FillYear()
        Dim dtYear As New DataTable
        dtYear = boYearSetup.tblYearSetup.GetDatabyCalendarType(clsCookie.CalendarType)
        ddlyear.DataTextField = "YearNo"
        ddlyear.DataValueField = "YearNo"
        ddlyear.DataSource = dtYear
        ddlyear.DataBind()
    End Sub

    Private Sub InitForm()
        clientDateFormat = clsCookie.DateFormat.ToString()
        strErrorText = ""

        CompanyID = Convert.ToInt32(clsCookie.CompanyID)
        objCommonFunctions.CompanyID = CompanyID

        If clsCookie.IsAdmin <> True And clsCookie.IsBAdmin <> True Then
            hidIsLeaveApprover.Value = CInt(clsCookie.IsLeaveApprover)
        Else
            hidIsLeaveApprover.Value = 0
        End If

        ServerDateFormat = ConfigurationManager.AppSettings("ServerDateFormat")
        strClientDateFormat = clsCookie.DateFormat.ToString()
        strUserName = clsCookie.EmployeeCode 'clsCookie.UserName.ToString()

        m_strBranchList = clsCookie.Branchlist
        m_strDepartmentList = clsCookie.Departmentlist
        m_strDivisionList = clsCookie.Divisionlist
        m_strGradeList = clsCookie.Gradelist

        If clsCookie.EnablePayroll = "Y" Then
            IsPayroll = True
        Else
            IsPayroll = False
        End If
        blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled = Convert.ToBoolean(clsCookie.IsLateEarlyAccountEnabled)
        Dim boCompany As New BO_Company
        IsAttendanceFlag = boCompany.tblCompany.GetDatabyKey(CompanyID).Rows(0).Item("IsAttendanceFlag")
        blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = boCompany.tblCompany.GetDatabyKey(CompanyID).Rows(0).Item("IsGraceTimeExcludeAfterGraceInTime")
        IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = boCompany.tblCompany.GetDatabyKey(CompanyID).Rows(0).Item("IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductColumn")
        IsMiddleEast = boCompanyFlag.tblCompanyFlag.GetFlagValue_String("IsMiddleEast", "False", CompanyID)

        If boCompany.tblCompany.GetDatabyKey(CompanyID).Rows(0).Item("PayrollProcessStartType") = "C" Then
            IsSingleMonthPayroll = True
        Else
            IsSingleMonthPayroll = False
        End If

    End Sub
    Sub setLanguage()
        Dim bolanguage As New BO_Language
        Dim dt As DataTable = bolanguage.tblLanguage.GetDatabyKey(clsCookie.LanguageID)
        If dt.Rows.Count > 0 Then
            hidLanguageDirection.Value = dt.Rows(0)("Direction")
        End If
    End Sub
    Sub SetEmployeeHirarchy()
        Dim strEmployeeHirarchy As String = clsCookie.EmployeeID
        Dim strEmployeeID As String = clsCookie.EmployeeID
        boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetEmployeesByApproverIDs(CompanyID, strEmployeeID, strEmployeeHirarchy)

        If strEmployeeHirarchy.Trim = "" Then
            hidEmployeeHirarchy.Value = clsCookie.EmployeeID
        Else
            hidEmployeeHirarchy.Value = strEmployeeHirarchy & "," & clsCookie.EmployeeID
        End If
    End Sub
    Private Sub FillCriteria2(Optional ByVal pReviewRequired As Boolean = False)
        Try
            Dim dtTemp As DataTable
            If hdRptType.Value = RptType.AttendanceActivityDetail Then
                Dim bodevices As New BO_Devices
                ddlShift.Items.Clear()
                dtTemp = bodevices.tblDevices.GetDatabyCompany(CompanyID)
                ddlShift.Items.Add(New ListItem("All", 0))
                ddlShift.DataSource = dtTemp
                ddlShift.DataTextField = "DeviceIPAddress"
                ddlShift.DataValueField = "DeviceIPAddress"
                ddlShift.DataBind()
            Else
                Dim boshift As New BO_Shift()
                If pReviewRequired = True Then
                    FillReview()
                Else
                    dtTemp = boshift.tblShift.GetDataForCriteria(CompanyID)
                    ddlShift.Items.Clear()
                    If hdRptType.Value <> RptType.LabourHoursByAttendance Then
                        ddlShift.Items.Add(New ListItem("All", 0))
                    End If
                    ddlShift.DataSource = dtTemp
                    ddlShift.DataTextField = "shiftTitle"
                    ddlShift.DataValueField = "shiftID"
                    ddlShift.DataBind()
                End If
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
        End Try


        '    Try

    '        If pReviewRequired = True Then
    '            FillReview()
    '        Else
    '            Dim dtTemp As DataTable
    '            Dim boshift As New BO_Shift()
    '            dtTemp = boshift.tblShift.GetDataForCriteria(CompanyID)
    '            ddlShift.Items.Clear()
    '            If hdRptType.Value <> RptType.LabourHoursByAttendance Then
    '                ddlShift.Items.Add(New ListItem("All", 0))
    '            End If
    '            ddlShift.DataSource = dtTemp
    '            ddlShift.DataTextField = "shiftTitle"
    '            ddlShift.DataValueField = "shiftID"
    '            ddlShift.DataBind()
    '        End If

    '    Catch ex As Exception
    '        lblError.Visible = True
    '        EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
    '    End Try

    End Sub
    Private Sub FillReview()
        Try
            Dim dtTemp As DataTable
            Dim boReview As New BO_ReviewType
            dtTemp = boReview.tblReviewType.GetDatabyCompany(CompanyID)
            ddlShift.Items.Clear()
            If hdRptType.Value <> RptType.LabourHoursByAttendance Then
                ddlShift.Items.Add(New ListItem("- All -", 0))
            End If
            ddlShift.DataSource = dtTemp
            ddlShift.DataTextField = "ReviewTypeTitle"
            ddlShift.DataValueField = "ReviewTypeID"
            ddlShift.DataBind()

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
        End Try

    End Sub
    Private Sub FillCompany()
        Try
            'Company Criteria
            Dim boCompany As New BO_Company
            Dim dtCompany As New DataTable
            dtCompany = boCompany.tblCompany.GetDataForDropDown()
            ddlCompany.Items.Clear()
            ddlCompany.Items.Add(New ListItem("Select Company", -1))
            ddlCompany.Items.Add(New ListItem("All Company(s)", 0))
            ddlCompany.DataSource = dtCompany
            ddlCompany.DataTextField = "CompanyTitle"
            ddlCompany.DataValueField = "CompanyID"
            ddlCompany.DataBind()

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
        End Try
    End Sub
    Private Sub SetReportName_old()

        If IsNothing(hdRptType.Value) = True Then
            lblError.Visible = True
            strErrorText = "Invalid Access to Page"
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, strErrorText)
            Me.btnViewReport.Enabled = False
            Exit Sub
        Else
            strReportTitle = EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.GetOneValue("st_tblobjectMst", "IsNull(ObjectTitle,'') As ObjectTitle", "WHERE ObjectName = 'rptGroup1_FrTo.aspx?rt=" & CInt(hdRptType.Value) & "'")
        End If


        Select Case CInt(hdRptType.Value)

            Case RptType.DailyAttendance, RptType.AttendanceSummary, _
                 RptType.AttendanceTrend, RptType.DailyAbsentees, _
                 RptType.AbsenteesGraph, RptType.EmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail, _

                 RptType.OfficialVisit, RptType.VisitorReport, RptType.AbsenteesReport, _
                 RptType.AttendanceSummaryDivision, RptType.EmployeeWiseOTDetail, RptType.EarlyComingSummary, _
                 RptType.DailyAttendanceInOrOut, RptType.EmployeeInformationByConfirmation, _
                 RptType.MissingScheduleOnDate, RptType.DeviceLogActivities, RptType.EmployeeShiftScheduling, _
                 RptType.AttendanceSummaryDepartmentDivision, RptType.AttendanceSummaryForPayroll, RptType.ShiftSchedulingHours, _
                 RptType.DailyAttendanceSheet, RptType.EmployeeInventory, RptType.DailyAttendanceSheetEfro, RptType.LastWeekAttendance, _
                 RptType.AttendanceHistory, RptType.EmployeeDailyStatus, RptType.EmployeeAttendanceDetail, _
                 RptType.IrRegularitiesInOTAttendance, RptType.TodayActivityReport, RptType.ShortLeaveTransaction, _
                 RptType.OverTimeDetail, RptType.OverTimeSummary, RptType.LabourHoursByAttendance, RptType.ActualAttendanceCountByTransaction, _
                 RptType.ActualAttendanceCountByTransactionDepartmentWise, RptType.AttendanceInOutOnDateWise, RptType.AttendanceRegister_Revised, _
                 RptType.MonthEndSummary, RptType.BusinessPlanStrength, RptType.BusinessPlanStrengthSummary, _
                 RptType.DailyAttendanceCustomized, RptType.AttendanceSummaryEmployee_Customized, RptType.EmployeeInformationExcel, _
                 RptType.DailyAbsenteesAndLeave_Revised, RptType.EmployeePerformanceReport, RptType.EmployeeWarning, RptType.WorkerDetail, _
                 RptType.EmployeeLeavingReasonWiseReport, RptType.MonthlyStaffNumberReport, RptType.MonthlyLeaversByManagementLevels, _
                 RptType.MonthlyJoinnersByManagementLevels, RptType.MonthlyTransferPromotions, RptType.MonthlyData, RptType.MonthlyHeadCountDetail, _
                 RptType.Active_InActive_StaffCountReport, RptType.DailyHeadCountDetailReport, RptType.MonthlyApprovedOverTime, _
                 RptType.InOutDurationWiseReport, RptType.EmployeeTimeSpentMonthwise, RptType.AttendanceExceptionReport, RptType.CustomizedAttendnaceReport, _

    Private Sub SetReportName()

        If IsNothing(hdRptType.Value) = True Then
            lblError.Visible = True
            strErrorText = "Invalid Access to Page"
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, strErrorText)
            Me.btnViewReport.Enabled = False
            Exit Sub
        Else
            strReportTitle = EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.GetOneValue("st_tblobjectMst", "IsNull(ObjectTitle,'') As ObjectTitle", "WHERE ObjectName = 'rptGroup1_FrTo.aspx?rt=" & CInt(hdRptType.Value) & "'")
        End If


        Select Case CInt(hdRptType.Value)

            Case RptType.DailyAttendance, RptType.AttendanceSummary, _
                 RptType.AttendanceTrend, RptType.DailyAbsentees, _
                 RptType.AbsenteesGraph, RptType.EmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail, _
                 RptType.EmployeeInformation, RptType.AttendanceSummaryEmployee, _
                 RptType.MissingEntries, RptType.AttendanceSummaryDepartment, RptType.Activity, _
                 RptType.EmployeeInformationByJoining, RptType.EmployeeInformationByLeaving, _
                 RptType.EmployeePersonalInformation, RptType.NumberOfEmployees, RptType.AttendanceRegister, _
                 RptType.OfficialVisit, RptType.VisitorReport, RptType.AbsenteesReport, _
                 RptType.AttendanceSummaryDivision, RptType.EmployeeWiseOTDetail, RptType.EarlyComingSummary, _
                 RptType.DailyAttendanceInOrOut, RptType.EmployeeInformationByConfirmation, _
                 RptType.MissingScheduleOnDate, RptType.DeviceLogActivities, RptType.EmployeeShiftScheduling, _
                 RptType.AttendanceSummaryDepartmentDivision, RptType.AttendanceSummaryForPayroll, RptType.ShiftSchedulingHours, _
                 RptType.DailyAttendanceSheet, RptType.EmployeeInventory, RptType.DailyAttendanceSheetEfro, RptType.LastWeekAttendance, _
                 RptType.AttendanceHistory, RptType.EmployeeDailyStatus, RptType.EmployeeAttendanceDetail, _
                 RptType.IrRegularitiesInOTAttendance, RptType.TodayActivityReport, RptType.ShortLeaveTransaction, _
                 RptType.OverTimeDetail, RptType.OverTimeSummary, RptType.LabourHoursByAttendance, RptType.ActualAttendanceCountByTransaction, _
                 RptType.ActualAttendanceCountByTransactionDepartmentWise, RptType.AttendanceInOutOnDateWise, RptType.AttendanceRegister_Revised, _
                 RptType.MonthEndSummary, RptType.BusinessPlanStrength, RptType.BusinessPlanStrengthSummary, _
                 RptType.DailyAttendanceCustomized, RptType.AttendanceSummaryEmployee_Customized, RptType.EmployeeInformationExcel, _
                 RptType.DailyAbsenteesAndLeave_Revised, RptType.EmployeePerformanceReport, RptType.EmployeeWarning, RptType.WorkerDetail, _
                 RptType.EmployeeLeavingReasonWiseReport, RptType.MonthlyStaffNumberReport, RptType.MonthlyLeaversByManagementLevels, _
                 RptType.MonthlyJoinnersByManagementLevels, RptType.MonthlyTransferPromotions, RptType.MonthlyData, RptType.MonthlyHeadCountDetail, _
                 RptType.Active_InActive_StaffCountReport, RptType.DailyHeadCountDetailReport, RptType.MonthlyApprovedOverTime, _
                 RptType.InOutDurationWiseReport, RptType.EmployeeTimeSpentMonthwise, RptType.AttendanceExceptionReport, RptType.CustomizedAttendnaceReport, _
                 RptType.CustomizedAttendnaceReportWH, RptType.EmployeeLeaveSummary, RptType.AttendanceRegister_RevisedNew, RptType.DailyAttendance_Revised, _
                 RptType.EmployeeAttendnace_Customized, RptType.AttendanceAnalysis_MTD, RptType.AttendanceAnalysis_YTD, RptType.OverTimeMusterRoll, _

    Private Function PerformValidation() As Boolean

        Try
            PerformValidation = False

            If ddlCriteria.SelectedValue <> "All" And ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue <> "All" And ddlCriteria.SelectedValue = ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue Then
                Dim strCriteriaDiffMsg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_CriteriaDiffMsg")
                strErrorText = IIf(strCriteriaDiffMsg = "", "Criteria and Sub Criteria must be different", strCriteriaDiffMsg)
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, strErrorText)
                Return False

            ElseIf ddlCriteria.Visible = True And ddlCriteria.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower <> "all" And (ddlFrom.SelectedIndex = -1 Or ddlTo.SelectedIndex = -1) Then
                Dim strCriteriaSelectionMsg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_CriteriaSelectionMsg")
                strErrorText = IIf(strCriteriaSelectionMsg = "", "From and To criteria must be select.", strCriteriaSelectionMsg)
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, strErrorText)
                Return False

            ElseIf ddlCriteria1.Visible = True And ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower <> "all" And (ddlFrom1.SelectedIndex = -1 Or ddlTo1.SelectedIndex = -1) Then
                Dim strCriteriaSelectionMsg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_CriteriaSelectionMsg")
                strErrorText = IIf(strCriteriaSelectionMsg = "", "From and To sub criteria must be select.", strCriteriaSelectionMsg)
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, strErrorText)
                Return False

            End If
            PerformValidation = True
        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
        End Try

    End Function
    Protected Sub btnViewReport_Click(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As System.EventArgs)
        Dim strPopup As String = ""

        If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.AttendanceRegisterMusterRoll Or ddlReportViewer.SelectedValue.ToLower = "pdf" Then

            Try
                Me.Validate()
                If Me.IsValid Then
                    If PerformValidation() = False Then Exit Sub
                    If SetParamData() = True Then
                        If SetDataSource() = True Then  'Redirect
                            hidURL.Value = Server.UrlEncode(hdRptTitle.Value)
                            strPopup = "<script language='javascript'>window.open('frmReportViewer1.aspx?ID=" & hidURL.Value & "&Type=PDF','_blank');</script>"
                            ScriptManager.RegisterStartupScript(Me, Me.[GetType](), "Mykey", strPopup, False)
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            Catch ex As Exception
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            End Try

        ElseIf ddlReportViewer.SelectedValue.ToLower = "excel" Then

            Try
                Me.Validate()
                If Me.IsValid Then
                    If PerformValidation() = False Then Exit Sub
                    If SetParamData() = True Then
                        If SetDataSource() = True Then  'Redirect
                            hidURL.Value = Server.UrlEncode(hdRptTitle.Value)
                            strPopup = "<script language='javascript'>window.open('frmReportViewer1.aspx?ID=" & hidURL.Value & "&Type=EXCEL','_blank');</script>"
                            ScriptManager.RegisterStartupScript(Me, Me.[GetType](), "Mykey", strPopup, False)
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            Catch ex As Exception
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            End Try

        ElseIf ddlReportViewer.SelectedValue.ToLower = "excelrecord" Then

            Try
                Me.Validate()
                If Me.IsValid Then
                    If PerformValidation() = False Then Exit Sub
                    If SetParamData() = True Then
                        If SetDataSource() = True Then  'Redirect
                            hidURL.Value = Server.UrlEncode(hdRptTitle.Value)
                            strPopup = "<script language='javascript'>window.open('frmReportViewer1.aspx?ID=" & hidURL.Value & "&Type=ExcelRecord','_blank');</script>"
                            ScriptManager.RegisterStartupScript(Me, Me.[GetType](), "Mykey", strPopup, False)
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            Catch ex As Exception
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            End Try
        Else
            Try
                Me.Validate()
                If Me.IsValid Then
                    If PerformValidation() = False Then Exit Sub
                    If SetParamData() = True Then
                        If SetDataSource() = True Then
                            hidURL.Value = Server.UrlEncode(hdRptTitle.Value)
                            If hidURL.Value <> Nothing Then
                                strPopup = "<script language='javascript'>window.open('frmReportViewer.aspx?ID=" & hidURL.Value & "','_blank');</script>"
                                ScriptManager.RegisterStartupScript(Me, Me.[GetType](), "Mykey", strPopup, False)
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            Catch ex As Exception
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            End Try

        End If
    End Sub
    Private Function SetParamData() As Boolean

        SetParamData = False
        Try

            ''This following call to ParseDates() function also saves Dates (typed in by user in ClientDate format)
            ''These saved values will be retrieved later for sending to report parameters.
            'If objDateFormatSettings.ParseDates(strFromDate, strToDate) = True Then

            strFromDateClient = txtFromDate.Text
            strToDateClient = txtToDate.Text
            FromDateServer = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(strFromDateClient, strClientDateFormat)
            ToDateServer = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(strToDateClient, strClientDateFormat)

            strRptCriteria = ddlCriteria.SelectedValue
            If strRptCriteria.ToString.ToLower = "all" Then
                strRptFromCriteria = ""
                strRptToCriteria = ""
            Else
                If clsCookie.IsSimpleUser = True And strRptCriteria.ToString.ToLower = "employee" Then
                    strRptFromCriteria = clsCookie.EmployeeCode
                    strRptToCriteria = clsCookie.EmployeeCode
                Else
                    strRptFromCriteria = ddlFrom.SelectedItem.GetValue("RefNo")
                    strRptToCriteria = ddlTo.SelectedItem.GetValue("RefNo")
                End If
            End If

            objCommonFunctions.RptCriteria = strRptCriteria
            objCommonFunctions.RptFromCriteria = strRptFromCriteria
            objCommonFunctions.RptToCriteria = strRptToCriteria

            strRptCriteria1 = ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue

            If strRptCriteria1.ToString.ToLower = "all" Then
                strRptFromCriteria1 = ""
                strRptToCriteria1 = ""
            Else
                strRptFromCriteria1 = ddlFrom1.SelectedItem.GetValue("RefNo")
                strRptToCriteria1 = ddlTo1.SelectedItem.GetValue("RefNo")
            End If

            objCommonFunctions.RptCriteria1 = strRptCriteria1
            objCommonFunctions.RptFromCriteria1 = strRptFromCriteria1
            objCommonFunctions.RptToCriteria1 = strRptToCriteria1


            Return True

            'End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try
    End Function

    Private Function GetEarlyTimeSummary() As Boolean

        GetEarlyTimeSummary = False
        Try
            Dim dtEarlyComing As New DataTable

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()



            Dim ds As DataSet
            ' ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, False, strFilter, False, True, IsManualEntry)
            ds = ReportHelper.GetBeforeTimeByEmployee(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)

            Dim rptValues As String = " "

            If ds.Tables(1).Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceWithEarlyTime.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(ds)
                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(CompanyID)

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & _
                    " To " & strToDateClient)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", hdRptTitle.Value)
                Dim count As Integer = ds.Tables(1).Rows.Count - 1
                Dim TotalEarlyTime As String = ds.Tables(1).Rows(count)("TotalTime")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("TotalEarlyTime", TotalEarlyTime)



                'rptDoc.SetParameterValue("TotalAbsentees", 0)
                'rptDoc.SetParameterValue("TotalEmployees", boFinalSchedule.tblFinalSchedule.GetCount_New(strEmployees, _
                '    FromDateServer, ToDateServer))
                'rptDoc.SetParameterValue("TotalLeave", boFinalSchedule.tblFinalSchedule.GetLeaveCount_New(strEmployees, _
                '    FromDateServer, ToDateServer))

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim Ed As New EfroPage
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = Ed.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetArrivalExitInfo(ByVal InDate As Date, ByVal InTime As String, ByVal GraceTime As String, _
                ByVal OutDate As Date, ByVal OutTime As String, _
                ByVal schdInDate As Date, _
                ByVal schdOutDate As Date, ByVal PInTime As String, ByVal POutTime As String, _
                ByVal InTimeIsValid As Boolean, ByVal OutTimeIsValid As Boolean) As String()

        Dim strArrivalExitInfo(8) As String

        Dim strLate As String = ""
        Dim strLateBetween As String = ""
        Dim strEarly As String = ""
        Dim strOverTime As String = ""

        Dim strScheduleTimeDuration As String = ""
        Dim strTimeSpent As String = ""
        Dim strShortTime As String = ""
        Dim strExcessTime As String = ""

        Dim SL_ArrivalInfo As SortedList(Of clsCommonFunctions.ArrivalInfoType, String) = Nothing

        '**************************
        If InTimeIsValid = True Then  'User had In Entry

            SL_ArrivalInfo = objCommonFunctions.GetArrivalInfo(InDate, InTime, schdInDate, PInTime, GraceTime)


            'Get Arrival Time info w.r.t Scheduled or Actual Time
            '--------------------------------------------------------------------------

            Dim enArrivalInfo_Sched As clsCommonFunctions.ArrivalInfoType
            enArrivalInfo_Sched = SL_ArrivalInfo.Keys(0)

            'Get Arrival Time info w.r.t Grace Time Limit
            Dim enArrivalInfo_Grace As clsCommonFunctions.ArrivalInfoType
            enArrivalInfo_Grace = SL_ArrivalInfo.Keys(1)

            If enArrivalInfo_Sched = clsCommonFunctions.ArrivalInfoType.RightOnScheduledInTime Then
                strLate = ""
                strLateBetween = ""
            ElseIf enArrivalInfo_Sched = clsCommonFunctions.ArrivalInfoType.AfterScheduledInTime Then
                strLate = SL_ArrivalInfo.Item(enArrivalInfo_Sched)
                strLateBetween = ""
            End If

            If enArrivalInfo_Sched <> clsCommonFunctions.ArrivalInfoType.RightOnScheduledInTime Then

                If enArrivalInfo_Grace = clsCommonFunctions.ArrivalInfoType.BeforeGraceTimeLimit Then
                    strLateBetween = SL_ArrivalInfo.Item(enArrivalInfo_Grace)
                    strLate = ""
                ElseIf enArrivalInfo_Grace = clsCommonFunctions.ArrivalInfoType.AfterGraceTimeLimit Then
                    strLate = SL_ArrivalInfo.Item(enArrivalInfo_Grace)
                    strLateBetween = ""
                ElseIf enArrivalInfo_Grace = clsCommonFunctions.ArrivalInfoType.RightOnGraceTimeLimit Then
                    strLateBetween = GraceTime
                    strLate = ""
                End If

            End If

        End If

        'Get Exit info w.r.t Scheduled Out Time
        '--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        If OutTimeIsValid = True Then

            Dim SL_ExitInfo As SortedList(Of clsCommonFunctions.ExitInfoType, String)

            '**************************
            SL_ExitInfo = objCommonFunctions.GetExitInfo(OutDate, schdOutDate, OutTime, POutTime)

            Dim enExitInfo As clsCommonFunctions.ExitInfoType
            enExitInfo = SL_ExitInfo.Keys(0)

            If enExitInfo = clsCommonFunctions.ExitInfoType.EarlyGo Then
                strEarly = SL_ExitInfo.Item(enExitInfo)
            ElseIf enExitInfo = clsCommonFunctions.ExitInfoType.LateSitting Then
                strOverTime = SL_ExitInfo.Item(enExitInfo)
            End If



            'Get Short/Excess Time info w.r.t Scheduled Out Time
            '--------------------------------------------------------------------------

            Dim SL_ShortExcess As SortedList(Of clsCommonFunctions.TimesType, String)

            '**************************
            SL_ShortExcess = objCommonFunctions.GetShortTimeExcessTimeInfo(InDate, OutDate, schdInDate, schdOutDate, _
                                                                        InTime, PInTime, OutTime, POutTime)

            If SL_ShortExcess.ContainsKey(clsCommonFunctions.TimesType.ShortTime) = True Then
                strShortTime = SL_ShortExcess.Item(clsCommonFunctions.TimesType.ShortTime)
            End If

            If SL_ShortExcess.ContainsKey(clsCommonFunctions.TimesType.ExcessTime) = True Then
                strExcessTime = SL_ShortExcess.Item(clsCommonFunctions.TimesType.ExcessTime)
            End If

            If SL_ShortExcess.ContainsKey(clsCommonFunctions.TimesType.ScheduleTimeDuration) = True Then
                strScheduleTimeDuration = SL_ShortExcess.Item(clsCommonFunctions.TimesType.ScheduleTimeDuration)
            End If

            If SL_ShortExcess.ContainsKey(clsCommonFunctions.TimesType.TimeSpent) = True Then
                strTimeSpent = SL_ShortExcess.Item(clsCommonFunctions.TimesType.TimeSpent)
            End If


        End If


        'Finally Collect Results
        '--------------------------
        strArrivalExitInfo(0) = strLate
        strArrivalExitInfo(1) = strLateBetween
        strArrivalExitInfo(2) = strEarly     'Only w.r.t to OutTime of tblTransaction
        strArrivalExitInfo(3) = strOverTime  'Only w.r.t to OutTime of tblTransaction

        'Setting Text for Display
        '---------------------------------
        Dim strRemarks As String = "Present"
        Dim strLateText As String = "Present Late"
        Dim strEarlyText As String = "Early Gone"

        If strLate <> "" Then
            strRemarks = strLateText
        End If

        If strEarly <> "" Then

            If strRemarks.Trim <> "" Then

                If strLate <> "" Then
                    strRemarks = strRemarks.Trim.Substring(0, 9) & " / " & strEarlyText.Trim.Chars(0)
                Else
                    strRemarks = strRemarks.ToString() & " " & strEarlyText.Trim.ToString()
                End If
            Else
                strRemarks = strEarlyText
            End If

        End If
        strArrivalExitInfo(4) = strRemarks
        '------------------------------------

        strArrivalExitInfo(5) = strShortTime 'W.r.t to Scheduled Duration
        strArrivalExitInfo(6) = strExcessTime 'W.r.t to Scheduled Duration
        strArrivalExitInfo(7) = strScheduleTimeDuration 'Scheduled Duration
        strArrivalExitInfo(8) = strTimeSpent 'Time Spent w.r.t to Scheduled Duration

        Return strArrivalExitInfo

    End Function
    Private Function AttendanceActivityOld() As Boolean

        AttendanceActivityOld = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendanceActivity As New dsAttendance.AttendanceActivityDataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As DataTable

            dtAttendanceActivity.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendanceActivity)


            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            'Get Data
            dtData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceActivity(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)


            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Dim dv As DataView = dtData.DefaultView
                dv.Sort = "EmployeeName, ActivityDate"
                dtData = dv.ToTable
            End If


            For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1

                Dim dr As dsAttendance.AttendanceActivityRow = dtAttendanceActivity.NewAttendanceActivityRow


                dr.EmployeeID = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeName = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeName").ToString()

                dr.DepartmentID = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentID").ToString()
                dr.DepartmentTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentTitle").ToString()
                dr.DesignationID = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationID").ToString()
                dr.DesignationTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationTitle").ToString()
                dr.DesignationTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationTitle").ToString()

                dr.ActivityDate = CDate(dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityDate"))
                dr.ActivityTime = dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityTime").ToString()
                dr.WorkCode = dtData.Rows(i)("WorkCode").ToString()

                dtAttendanceActivity.Rows.Add(dr)
            Next


            If dtAttendanceActivity.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                    SetReportPath("rptAttendanceActivity_ar.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptAttendanceActivity.rpt")
                End If


                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendanceActivity.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("DateTitle", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & _
                    " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportCriteria", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function AttendanceActivity() As Boolean

        AttendanceActivity = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendanceActivity As New dsAttendance.AttendanceActivityDataTable

            'Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As New DataTable

            dtAttendanceActivity.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendanceActivity)


            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            Dim empCodeLength As Integer = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetRefNoLength(CompanyID)
            'Get Data

            If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.Activity Then
                dtData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceActivity(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)
            ElseIf CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.DeviceLogActivities Then
                dtData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceActivityByDeviceLog(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, empCodeLength)
            ElseIf CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeActivityReportCustomized Then
                dtData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceActivity_New(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)
            End If


            For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1

                Dim dr As dsAttendance.AttendanceActivityRow = dtAttendanceActivity.NewAttendanceActivityRow

                dr.EmployeeID = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeID").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeRefNo = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeName = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeName").ToString()

                dr.DepartmentID = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentID").ToString()
                dr.DepartmentTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentTitle").ToString()
                dr.DesignationID = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationID").ToString()
                dr.DesignationTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationTitle").ToString()
                dr.WorkCode = dtData.Rows(i)("WorkCode").ToString()

                dr.ActivityDate = CDate(dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityDate"))
                dr.InTime = dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityTime").ToString()

                Try
                    If dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeID") = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("EmployeeID") Then
                        If dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityDate").ToString = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("ActivityDate").ToString() Then
                            dr.OutTime = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("ActivityTime").ToString()
                            i = i + 1
                        Else
                            dr.OutTime = "00:00:00"
                        End If
                    Else
                        dr.OutTime = "00:00:00"
                    End If

                Catch ex As Exception
                    dr.OutTime = "00:00:00"
                End Try

                dtAttendanceActivity.Rows.Add(dr)
            Next


            If dtAttendanceActivity.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                    SetReportPath("rptAttendanceActivity_ar.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptAttendanceActivity.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendanceActivity.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("DateTitle", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & _
                    " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportCriteria", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetDailyAttendance() As Boolean

        GetDailyAttendance = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""

        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim strFilter As String = ""
            If chklate.Checked = True And chkEarly.Checked = True Then
                strFilter = "LateTime<>'0:00' And EarlyTime<>'0:00'"
            ElseIf chklate.Checked = True And chkEarly.Checked = False Then
                strFilter = "LateTime<>'0:00'"
            ElseIf chklate.Checked = False And chkEarly.Checked = True Then
                strFilter = "EarlyTime<>'0:00'"
            End If

            If chkAbsent.Checked = False Then
                If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
                strFilter = strFilter & " AttendanceStatus<>'A'"
            End If
            If ddlShift.SelectedValue <> 0 Then
                If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
                strFilter = strFilter & " ShiftID=" & ddlShift.SelectedValue
            End If


            Dim IsManualEntry As Boolean = False
            If chkManual.Checked = True Then
                IsManualEntry = True
            End If

            Dim ds As DataSet
            If RptType.DailyAttendanceDateWise <> hdRptType.Value And _
               RptType.EmployeeDailyAttendance <> hdRptType.Value Then ToDateServer = FromDateServer

            If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                If RptType.DailyAttendanceInOrOut = hdRptType.Value Or _
                   RptType.EmployeeDailyAttendance = hdRptType.Value Then
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, _
                        boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, False, strFilter, False, True, IsManualEntry, True)
                ElseIf RptType.DailyAttendance_Revised = hdRptType.Value Then
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceDetailFull(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, IsManualEntry, strFilter, IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn)
                Else
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, _
                        boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, False, strFilter, False, True, IsManualEntry, True, False, 0, 0, False, False, IsAttendanceFlag)
                End If
            Else
                If RptType.DailyAttendanceInOrOut = hdRptType.Value Or _
                   RptType.EmployeeDailyAttendance = hdRptType.Value Then
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, _
                        boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, False, strFilter, False, True, IsManualEntry, True)
                ElseIf RptType.DailyAttendance_Revised = hdRptType.Value Then
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceDetailFull(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, IsManualEntry, strFilter, IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn)
                Else
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, _
                        boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, False, strFilter, False, True, IsManualEntry, False, False, 0, 0, False, False, IsAttendanceFlag)
                End If
            End If

            Dim dt As DataTable
            dt = ds.Tables(0)
            Dim dt2 As DataTable
            dt2 = ds.Tables(1)

            If IsNothing(ds.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso ds.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                If RptType.DailyAttendanceInOrOut = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptDailyAttendanceDepartmentInOrOut.rpt")
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeDailyAttendance = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptDailyAttendanceCustomized1.rpt")
                Else
                    If IsAttendanceFlag = False Then
                        SetReportPath("rptDailyAttendanceDepartment.rpt")
                    Else
                        If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                            SetReportPath("rptDailyAttendanceDepartmentFlag_ar.rpt")
                        Else
                            SetReportPath("rptDailyAttendanceDepartmentFlag.rpt")
                        End If
                    End If
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(ds)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                If rdoInOnly.Checked = True Then
                    hdRptTitle.Value = hdRptTitle.Value & " (In Time)"
                Else
                    hdRptTitle.Value = hdRptTitle.Value & " (Out Time)"
                End If
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                'Report Specific Params
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strBeforeGrace1", "Within")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strBeforeGrace2", "Grace")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("AfterGrace1", "After")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strAfterGrace2", "Grace")

                SetParameterForDailyAttendanceInOrOut()

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetAttendanceTrend() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceTrend = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New dsAttendance.EmployeeAttendanceDataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As DataTable

            dtAttendance.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()


            dtData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceDetail(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Dim dv As DataView = dtData.DefaultView
                dv.Sort = "EmployeeName, ScheduleDate"
                dtData = dv.ToTable
            End If


            For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1
                Dim hoursSpent As Double = 0
                Dim timeSpent As String = "00:00:00"
                Dim strTime As String = ""
                Dim hasTranEntry As Boolean

                Dim dr As dsAttendance.EmployeeAttendanceRow = dtAttendance.NewEmployeeAttendanceRow()

                dr.EmployeeNo = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeName = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeName").ToString()
                dr.ShiftID = dtData.Rows(i)("ShiftCode").ToString()

                hasTranEntry = Not IsDBNull(dtData.Rows(i)("InDate"))
                hoursSpent = IIf(hasTranEntry, dtData.Rows(i)("Hours"), 0)


                'Transaction In/Out--Date/Time Info
                Dim InDate As Object = IIf(IsDBNull(dtData.Rows(i)("InDate")) = True, Nothing, dtData.Rows(i)("InDate"))
                Dim InTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(dtData.Rows(i)("InTime")) = True, "", dtData.Rows(i)("InTime"))
                Dim OutDate As Object = IIf(IsDBNull(dtData.Rows(i)("OutDate")) = True, Nothing, dtData.Rows(i)("OutDate"))
                Dim OutTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(dtData.Rows(i)("OutTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtData.Rows(i)("OutTime"))

                Dim InTimeIsValid As Boolean = IIf(IsDBNull(dtData.Rows(i)("InTimeIsValid")) = True, False, dtData.Rows(i)("InTimeIsValid"))
                Dim OutTimeIsValid As Boolean = IIf(IsDBNull(dtData.Rows(i)("OutTimeIsValid")) = True, False, dtData.Rows(i)("OutTimeIsValid"))


                'Proposed In/Out Dates, In/Out Times as per Schedule
                Dim schdInDate As Date = CDate(dtData.Rows(i)("ScheduleDate"))
                Dim schdOutDate As Date = CDate(dtData.Rows(i)("ScheduleOutDate"))
                Dim PInTime As String = dtData.Rows(i)("StartingTime").ToString
                Dim POutTime As String = dtData.Rows(i)("EndingTime").ToString

                dr.ProEstimateTime = POutTime   'Actual Scheduled Ending Time
                dr.ScheduledOutDate = CDate(dtData.Rows(i)("ScheduleOutDate"))

                'Grace Time
                Dim GraceTime As String = dtData.Rows(i)("GraceTime").ToString

                Dim strLate As String = ""
                Dim strLateBetween As String = ""
                Dim strEarly As String = ""
                Dim strOverTime As String = ""
                Dim strRemarks As String = " "

                hasTranEntry = Not IsNothing(InDate)
                hoursSpent = IIf(hasTranEntry = True, dtData.Rows(i)("Hours"), 0)

                Dim tsTimeSpan As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                If IsNothing(InDate) = False AndAlso InTimeIsValid = True AndAlso OutTimeIsValid = True Then
                    tsTimeSpan = ReportHelper.GetTimeBetween(CDate(InDate), InTime, CDate(OutDate), OutTime)
                End If


                dr.ActSpendTime = clsCommonFunctions.GetTimeSpanInHourMinuteSecond(tsTimeSpan)    'timeSpent

                If hasTranEntry Then
                    dr.OutDate = OutDate
                    dr.InTime = InTime
                    dr.OutTime = OutTime


                    Dim strArrivalExitInfo() As String = _
                        GetArrivalExitInfo(InDate, InTime, GraceTime, OutDate, OutTime, _
                            schdInDate, dr.ScheduledOutDate, PInTime, POutTime, InTimeIsValid, OutTimeIsValid)

                    strLate = strArrivalExitInfo(0)
                    strLateBetween = strArrivalExitInfo(1)
                    strEarly = strArrivalExitInfo(2)
                    strOverTime = strArrivalExitInfo(3)
                    strRemarks = strArrivalExitInfo(4)

                Else
                    dr.InTime = ""
                    dr.OutTime = ""
                End If

                dr.InDate = dtData.Rows(i)("ScheduleDate")

                dr.LateComeDuty = strLate
                dr.EarlyGoDuty = strEarly
                dr.Remarks = strRemarks

                '''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''''

                dr.Leave = IIf(dtData.Rows(i)("Leave").ToString() = "0", False, True)

                If dr.Leave Then
                    dr.LeaveDescription = dtData.Rows(i)("Leave").ToString()
                Else
                    If dtData.Rows(i)("StartingTime").ToString() = "00:00:00" And _
                        dtData.Rows(i)("EndingTime").ToString() = "00:00:00" Then
                        dr.LeaveDescription = "Off Day"
                        dr.LateComeDuty = "00:00:00"
                        dr.EarlyGoDuty = "00:00:00"
                    Else
                        If hasTranEntry Then
                            dr.LeaveDescription = "Present"
                        Else
                            dr.LeaveDescription = "Absent"
                        End If
                    End If
                End If

                dr.InDate = dtData.Rows(i)("ScheduleDate")
                If hasTranEntry Then
                    dr.OutDate = dtData.Rows(i)("OutDate")
                    dr.InTime = dtData.Rows(i)("InTime")
                    dr.OutTime = dtData.Rows(i)("OutTime")
                Else
                    dr.InTime = ""
                    dr.OutTime = ""
                End If


                dtAttendance.Rows.Add(dr)
            Next

            objCommonFunctions.SumAttendance()


            If IsNothing(dtAttendance) = False AndAlso dtAttendance.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                    SetReportPath("rptGraphicalAttendanceLine_ar.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptGraphicalAttendanceLine.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendance.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & _
                    " To " & strToDateClient)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", rptValues)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
            Dim intTotalDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")
            Dim ds As DataSet

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                If IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then
                    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , , True)
                    Else
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , , , , , , , , , , True)
                    End If
                Else
                    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , )
                    Else
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, )
                    End If
                End If

                If dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt1 = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt1.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

                If dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

            Next

            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)

            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_1.rpt")
                Else
                    If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail Then
                        If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt1.DefaultView.ToTable)
                            SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_ar.rpt")
                        Else
                            SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail.rpt")
                        End If
                    ElseIf CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeAttendanceDetail Then
                        SetReportPath("rptEmployeeAttendanceDetail.rpt")
                    ElseIf CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeAttendanceDetail_Engro Then
                        SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_Engro.rpt")
                    End If
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetEmployeeWiseOTDetail() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeWiseOTDetail = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim IsOverTime As Boolean
            If clsCookie.EnableOverTime = "Y" Then
                IsOverTime = True
            Else
                IsOverTime = False
            End If

            Dim ds As DataSet
            ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, False, "", False, True, False, False, IsOverTime, 0, 0, False, False, False, chkOTEmployee.Checked, chkNonOTEmployee.Checked)

            ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.RowFilter = "OverTime<>'0:00'"
            Dim dt As DataTable = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable()

            If IsNothing(dt) = False AndAlso dt.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                'ds.Tables.Remove(ds.Tables(1))
                'ds.Tables.Add(dt)
                rptDoc = New ReportDocument

                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseOTDetail.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(ds)

                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)

                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False

    Private Function GetDailyAbsentees() As Boolean

        GetDailyAbsentees = False

        Try

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim AutoEmails As New AutoEmails, dtData As DataTable = Nothing, _
                dsNew As New DataSet, boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule

            dtData = AutoEmails.GetDailyAbsenteesAndLeave(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, rdoAbsentees, rdoLeaves, ddlShift)
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dtData)

            Dim rptValues As String = " "

            If dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hdRptType.Value = RptType.DailyAbsenteesAndLeave_Revised Then
                    SetReportPath("rptDailyAbsenteesAndLeave_Revised.rpt")
                Else
                    If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                        SetReportPath("rptDailyAbsenteesAndLeave_ar.rpt")
                    Else
                        SetReportPath("rptDailyAbsenteesAndLeave.rpt")
                    End If

                End If
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(CompanyID)

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & _
                    " To " & strToDateClient)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", hdRptTitle.Value)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("TotalAbsentees", 0)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("TotalEmployees", boFinalSchedule.tblFinalSchedule.GetCount_New(strEmployees, _
                    FromDateServer, ToDateServer))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("TotalLeave", boFinalSchedule.tblFinalSchedule.GetLeaveCount_New(strEmployees, _
                    FromDateServer, ToDateServer))

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetAbsenteesReport() As Boolean

        GetAbsenteesReport = False
        Try

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
