            dtAbsents.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAbsents)

            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")


            Dim strFilter As String = ""
            'If rdoAbsentees.Checked = True Then
            '    If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
            '    strFilter = strFilter & " AttendanceStatus = 'A'"
            'ElseIf rdoLeaves.Checked = True Then
            '    If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
            '    strFilter = strFilter & " AttendanceStatus = 'L'"
            'Else
            '    If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
            '    strFilter = strFilter & " AttendanceStatus IN ('A','L')"
            'End If
            'If ddlShift.SelectedValue <> 0 Then
            '    If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
            '    strFilter = strFilter & " ShiftID=" & ddlShift.SelectedValue
            'End If


            strFilter = " AttendanceStatus = 'A'"

            Dim ds As DataSet
            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, False, strFilter, False, False, False)
                If dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt1 = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt1.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

                If dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

            Next

            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt1.DefaultView.ToTable)
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)

            Dim rptValues As String = " "

            If dsNew.Tables(1).Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptAbsenteesReport.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(CompanyID)

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & _
                    " To " & strToDateClient)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", hdRptTitle.Value)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    Private Function GetEmployeeInformation() As Boolean
        GetEmployeeInformation = False
        Try
            Dim dtEmployeeInfo As New dsAttendance.EmployeeInfoDataTable
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As DataTable = Nothing, IsLeaveEmployeeRequired As Boolean = False

            If ddlCriteria.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "left employees" Or _
                ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "left employees" Then
                IsLeaveEmployeeRequired = True
            Else
                IsLeaveEmployeeRequired = False
            End If

            dtEmployeeInfo.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtEmployeeInfo)
            If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeInformationByLeaving Then
                CreateEmployeeFilter(True)
            Else
                CreateEmployeeFilter()
            End If

            If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeInformation Or CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeePersonalInformation Then
                dtData = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetEmployeeInfo(strEmployees, CompanyID, IsLeaveEmployeeRequired)
            ElseIf CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeInformationByJoining Then
                dtData = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetEmployeeInfo_DateWise(strEmployees, CompanyID, "JOINING", FromDateServer, ToDateServer)
            ElseIf CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeInformationByLeaving Then
                dtData = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetEmployeeInfo_DateWise(strEmployees, CompanyID, "LEAVING", FromDateServer, ToDateServer)
            End If

            'dtData = dtEmployeeInfo.Copy

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Dim dv As DataView = dtData.DefaultView
                dv.Sort = "EmployeeName"
                dtData = dv.ToTable
            End If

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1
                Dim dr As dsAttendance.EmployeeInfoRow = dtEmployeeInfo.NewEmployeeInfoRow()

                dr.EmployeeRefNo = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeName = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeName").ToString()
                dr.FatherHusbandName = dtData.Rows(i)("FatherHusbandName").ToString()
                dr.DepartmentID = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentID").ToString()
                dr.DepartmentTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentTitle").ToString()
                dr.DesignationID = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationID").ToString()
                dr.DesignationTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationTitle").ToString()
                dr.BranchTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("BranchTitle").ToString()
                dr.QualificationID = dtData.Rows(i)("QualificationID").ToString()
                dr.QualificationTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("QualificationTitle").ToString()

                Dim strBirtDate As String = Convert.ToDateTime(dtData.Rows(i)("BirthDate")).ToString(strClientDateFormat)
                dr.BirthDate = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(strBirtDate, strClientDateFormat)

                Dim strAppointmentDate As String = Convert.ToDateTime(dtData.Rows(i)("AppointmentDate")).ToString(strClientDateFormat)
                dr.AppointmentDate = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(strAppointmentDate, strClientDateFormat)

                Dim strConfirmationDate As String = Convert.ToDateTime(dtData.Rows(i)("ConfirmationDate")).ToString(strClientDateFormat)
                dr.ConfirmationDate = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(strConfirmationDate, strClientDateFormat)

                dr.NewNIC = dtData.Rows(i)("NICNumber").ToString()
                dr.LeavingDate = dtData.Rows(i)("LeavingDate").ToString()
                dr.Address = dtData.Rows(i)("Address").ToString()
                dr.Telephone = dtData.Rows(i)("Telephone").ToString()
                dr.BloodGroup = dtData.Rows(i)("BloodGroup").ToString()
                If dtData.Columns.Contains("DivisionID") Then
                    dr.DivisionID = dtData.Rows(i)("DivisionID").ToString()
                    dr.DivisionTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DivisionTitle").ToString()
                End If

                dtEmployeeInfo.Rows.Add(dr)
            Next

            If IsNothing(dtEmployeeInfo) = False AndAlso dtEmployeeInfo.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeePersonalInformation Then
                    If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                        SetReportPath("Cr02EmpInformation_ar.rpt")
                    Else
                        SetReportPath("Cr02EmpInformation.rpt")
                    End If
                Else
                    If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                        SetReportPath("CrEmpInformation_ar.rpt")
                    Else
                        SetReportPath("CrEmpInformation.rpt")
                    End If

                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtEmployeeInfo.DefaultView.ToTable())


                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                If pnlDateCriteria.Visible = False Then
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Field1", "Branch")
                Else
                    If IsMiddleEast = True Then
                        rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Field1", "CPR/IQAMA")
                    Else
                        rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Field1", "New NIC")
                    End If
                End If

                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeInformationByLeaving Then
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Field2", "Leaving Date")
                Else
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Field2", "Birth Date")
                End If

                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeInformationByLeaving OrElse _
                   CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeInformationByJoining Then
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                End If
                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeInformation Then
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "")

                End If

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function

    Private Function GetConfirmedEmployeeInformation() As Boolean
        GetConfirmedEmployeeInformation = False
        Try
            Dim dtEmployeeInfo As New dsAttendance.EmployeeInfoDataTable
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As DataTable = Nothing

            dtEmployeeInfo.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtEmployeeInfo)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()



            FromDateServer = FromDateServer.AddMonths(-6)
            ToDateServer = ToDateServer.AddMonths(-6)


            If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeInformationByConfirmation Then
                dtData = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetConfirmedEmployeeInfo(strEmployees, CompanyID, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)
            End If

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Dim dv As DataView = dtData.DefaultView
                dv.Sort = "EmployeeName"
                dtData = dv.ToTable
            End If

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1
                Dim dr As dsAttendance.EmployeeInfoRow = dtEmployeeInfo.NewEmployeeInfoRow()

                dr.EmployeeRefNo = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeName = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeName").ToString()
                dr.FatherHusbandName = dtData.Rows(i)("FatherHusbandName").ToString()
                dr.DepartmentID = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentID").ToString()
                dr.DepartmentTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentTitle").ToString()
                dr.DesignationID = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationID").ToString()
                dr.DesignationTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationTitle").ToString()
                dr.BranchTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("BranchTitle").ToString()
                dr.CityID = dtData.Rows(i)("CityRefNo").ToString()
                dr.CityTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("CityTitle").ToString()
                dr.GroupID = dtData.Rows(i)("GradeRefNo").ToString()
                dr.GroupTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("GradeTitle").ToString()
                Dim strConfirmationDate As String
                Dim strBirtDate As String = Convert.ToDateTime(dtData.Rows(i)("BirthDate")).ToString(strClientDateFormat)
                dr.BirthDate = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(strBirtDate, strClientDateFormat)

                Dim strAppointmentDate As String = Convert.ToDateTime(dtData.Rows(i)("AppointmentDate")).ToString(strClientDateFormat)
                dr.AppointmentDate = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(strAppointmentDate, strClientDateFormat)
                If CBool(dtData.Rows(i)("HasConfirmed")) = True Then
                    strConfirmationDate = Convert.ToDateTime(dtData.Rows(i)("ConfirmationDate")).ToString(strClientDateFormat)
                    dr.ConfirmationDate = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(strConfirmationDate, strClientDateFormat)
                    dr.ConfirmationDue = "01/01/1900"
                ElseIf CBool(dtData.Rows(i)("HasConfirmed")) = False Then
                    strConfirmationDate = Convert.ToDateTime(dtData.Rows(i)("AppointmentDate")).ToString(strClientDateFormat)
                    dr.ConfirmationDue = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(strConfirmationDate, strClientDateFormat).AddMonths(6)
                    dr.ConfirmationDate = "01/01/1900"
                End If

                dr.NewNIC = dtData.Rows(i)("NICNumber").ToString()
                dr.LeavingDate = dtData.Rows(i)("LeavingDate").ToString()
                dr.Address = dtData.Rows(i)("Address").ToString()
                dr.Telephone = dtData.Rows(i)("Telephone").ToString()
                dr.BloodGroup = dtData.Rows(i)("BloodGroup").ToString()
                dtEmployeeInfo.Rows.Add(dr)
            Next

            If IsNothing(dtEmployeeInfo) = False AndAlso dtEmployeeInfo.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("ConfirmedEmpInformation.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtEmployeeInfo.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                If pnlDateCriteria.Visible = False Then
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Field1", "Branch")
                Else
                    If IsMiddleEast = True Then
                        rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Field1", "CPR/IQAMA")
                    Else
                        rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Field1", "New NIC")
                    End If
                End If
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("DateRange", "From " & Trim(txtFromDate.Text) & " To " & Trim(txtToDate.Text))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("TodayDate", Today.Date)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetNoOfEmployee() As Boolean
        GetNoOfEmployee = False
        Try
            Dim dtEmployeeInfo As New dsAttendance.EmployeeInfoDataTable
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As DataTable = Nothing

            dtEmployeeInfo.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtEmployeeInfo)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            dtData = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetEmployeeCount(strEmployees, CompanyID)

            'If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
            '    Dim dv As DataView = dtData.DefaultView
            '    dv.Sort = "EmployeeName"
            '    dtData = dv.ToTable
            'End If

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1
                Dim dr As dsAttendance.EmployeeInfoRow = dtEmployeeInfo.NewEmployeeInfoRow()
                dr.SrNo = i + 1
                dr.DepartmentTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentTitle").ToString()
                dr.BranchTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("BranchTitle").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeCategoryTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeCategoryTitle").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeCount = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeCount").ToString()
                dtEmployeeInfo.Rows.Add(dr)
            Next

            If IsNothing(dtEmployeeInfo) = False AndAlso dtEmployeeInfo.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                    SetReportPath("Cr03EmpInformation_ar.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("Cr03EmpInformation.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtEmployeeInfo.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Field1", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Field2", "")

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetAttendanceRegister_Revised() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceRegister_Revised = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New dsAttendance.ShiftScheduleRegDataTable
            Dim strServerDateFormat As String = ConfigurationManager.AppSettings("ServerDateFormat")

            Dim intTotDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)  '0 Based

            If intTotDays > 31 Then
                Dim strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_FromAndToDateDiffMsg")
                Throw New Exception(IIf(strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg = "", "Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 31 days", strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg))
            End If

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")


            Dim ds As New DataSet
            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.AttendanceRegister_RevisedNew Then
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceDetailFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, False, "", IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn)
                Else
                    If IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then
                        If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                            ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , , True)
                        Else
                            ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , , , , , , , , , , True)
                        End If
                    Else
                        If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                            ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , )
                        Else
                            ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, )
                        End If
                    End If
                End If

                If dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt1 = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt1.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

                If dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If
            Next

            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)

            If dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True And IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then
                    SetReportPath("crAttendanceRegister_3.rpt")
                ElseIf CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.AttendanceRegister_RevisedNew Then
                    SetReportPath("crAttendanceRegister_2.rpt")
                Else
                    If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                        dsNew.Tables.Add(dt1.DefaultView.ToTable)
                        SetReportPath("crAttendanceRegister_ar.rpt")
                    Else
                        SetReportPath("crAttendanceRegister.rpt")
                    End If
                End If

                'Report Params
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew)
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                'rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptValue", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptFromDate", txtFromDate.Text)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptToDate", ToDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat.ToString()))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptDates", "Dates From : " & FromDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat) & _
                                " To " & ToDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportDays", DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer) + 1)


                'Report Params
                Dim datTemp As Date = FromDateServer


                For i As Integer = 1 To 31
                    Try

                        'Dim dayName1 As String = datTemp.Day.ToString("dddd")
                        Dim dayName1 As String = datTemp.DayOfWeek.ToString

                        If dayName1.ToLower = "sunday" Then   'Indicate Sundays
                            rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, "*" & datTemp.Day.ToString)
                        Else
                            rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, datTemp.Day.ToString)
                        End If


                    Catch ex As Exception  'Month does not have this Date
                        rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, " ")
                    End Try

                    datTemp = datTemp.AddDays(1)

                Next

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetAttendanceSummaryEmployee_Revised() As Boolean
        Try

            GetAttendanceSummaryEmployee_Revised = False

            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee, EmpHelper As New EmployeeHelper
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable
            Dim intTotalDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)
            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dtData As New DataTable

            dtAttendance.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")

            Dim ds As DataSet
            If IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then
                If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                    For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , , True)
                        If dtData.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                            dtData = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                        Else
                            dtData.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                        End If
                    Next
                Else
                    For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , , , , , , , , , , True)
                        If dtData.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                            dtData = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                        Else
                            dtData.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                        End If
                    Next
                End If
            Else
                If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                    For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , )
                        If dtData.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                            dtData = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                        Else
                            dtData.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                        End If
                    Next
                Else
                    For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag)
                        If dtData.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                            dtData = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                        Else
                            dtData.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                        End If
                    Next
                End If
            End If

            dtAttendance = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable

            If dtAttendance.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                dtAttendance.DefaultView.Sort = "DepartmentRefNo, EmployeeRefNo Asc"
                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeLeaveSummary Then
                    Dim dtLeaveSummary As New DataTable
                    dtAttendance.DefaultView.RowFilter = "TotalLeave > 0 OR TotalAbsent > 0"
                    dtLeaveSummary = dtAttendance.DefaultView.ToTable
                    If dtLeaveSummary.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                        SetReportPath("rptEmployeeLeaveSummary.rpt")
                        rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtLeaveSummary.DefaultView.ToTable)
                    Else
                        lblError.Visible = True
                        Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                        EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                        Return False
                    End If
                Else
                    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                        SetReportPath("rptAttendanceSummaryEmp3.rpt")
                    Else
                        If blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled = True Then
                            SetReportPath("rptAttendanceSummaryEmp.rpt")
                        Else
                            If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceSummaryEmp2_ar.rpt")
                            Else
                                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceSummaryEmp2.rpt")
                            End If

                        End If
                    End If
                    rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendance.DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", rptValues)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim intIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled As Integer = IIf(blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled = True, 1, 0)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("IsLateEarlyAccEnabled", intIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled.ToString)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True
            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try



    End Function

    Private Function GetAttendanceSummaryDepartment() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceSummaryDepartment = False
        Try

            Dim clsEmail As New AutoEmails
            Dim dtAttendanceDept As New DataTable, strFilter As String = "", ds As DataSet

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            ds = ReadXMLFromHidden()
            strFilter = " ShiftID IN " & ShiftFilter(ds)

            dtAttendanceDept = clsEmail.GetAttendanceSummaryDepartment(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, strFilter, True, chkOffDay, _
                                strRptCriteria1, strRptFromCriteria1, strRptToCriteria1)

            If dtAttendanceDept.DefaultView.Count > 0 Then

                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.AbsentCountBranchWise_Stylo Then
                    SetReportPath("rptAbsenteesBranchWise.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptAttendanceSummaryDept.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendanceDept.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("DateTitle", "Date " & FromDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat.ToString()))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                If chkDivisionGroupInclude.Checked = True Then
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("GroupByDivision", "Yes")
                ElseIf chkDivisionGroupInclude.Checked = False Then
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("GroupByDivision", "No")
                End If

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try



    End Function
    Private Function GetAttendanceSummaryDivision() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceSummaryDivision = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendanceDivision As New dsAttendance.AttendanceSummaryDivisionDataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As New DataTable

            dtAttendanceDivision.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendanceDivision)
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            dtData = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetEmployeeInfo(strEmployees, CompanyID)
            dtData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable(True, New String() { _
                    "BranchID", "BranchTitle", "BranchRefNo", "DivisionID", "DivisionRefNo", "DivisionTitle"})


            Dim boShift As New BO_Shift
            Dim datTemp As Date = FromDateServer.Date
            Dim dtShift As DataTable = boShift.tblShift.GetDatabyCompany(CompanyID)
            Dim strFilter As String = "", ds As DataSet
            Dim intTotal As Integer = dtData.Rows.Count

            ds = ReadXMLFromHidden()
            strFilter = " ShiftID IN " & ShiftFilter(ds)
            If Right(strFilter, 4) <> "(-1)" Then
                dtShift.DefaultView.RowFilter = strFilter
                dtShift = dtShift.DefaultView.ToTable()
            Else
                Throw New Exception("Atleast One Shift Must Be Select.")
            End If

            Dim dtAttend As New DataTable("AttendanceDetail")
            Dim DCol1 = New DataColumn("EmployeeID", GetType(System.Int64))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol1)
            Dim DCol2 = New DataColumn("BranchID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol2)
            Dim DCol3 = New DataColumn("DivisionID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol3)
            Dim DCol4 = New DataColumn("ShiftID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol4)
            Dim DCol5 = New DataColumn("ScheduleDate", GetType(System.DateTime))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol5)
            Dim PKeysA() As DataColumn = {DCol1, DCol2, DCol3, DCol4, DCol5}
            dtAttend.PrimaryKey = PKeysA
            dtAttend.Merge(ReportHelper.GetAttendanceByDateDivision(strEmployees, datTemp, datTemp, CompanyID))
            Dim dtLeave As New DataTable
            dtLeave = ReportHelper.GetLeaveByDateDivision(strEmployees, datTemp, datTemp, CompanyID)
            dtAttend.Merge(dtLeave)

            dtAttend.DefaultView.RowFilter = strFilter
            dtAttend = dtAttend.DefaultView.ToTable

            Dim dtStrength As DataTable '= ReportHelper.GetDivisionStrength(CompanyID)

            ' Changes By Hafiz On 25/08/2010
            If strRptCriteria1.ToLower = "employee" Then
                dtStrength = ReportHelper.GetDivisionStrength(CompanyID)
            ElseIf strRptCriteria1.ToLower <> "all" Then
                dtStrength = ReportHelper.GetDepartmentStrength_New(CompanyID, strRptCriteria1, strRptFromCriteria1, strRptToCriteria1, strEmployees)
            Else
                dtStrength = ReportHelper.GetDivisionStrength(CompanyID)
            End If

            For j As Integer = 0 To dtAttend.Rows.Count - 1
                Dim drDestRows As DataRow = dtAttend.Rows(j)
                Dim schdInDate As Date = CDate(drDestRows("ScheduleDate"))
                Dim schdOutDate As Date
                If IsDBNull(drDestRows("ScheduleOutDate")) = True Then
                    schdOutDate = CDate(drDestRows("ScheduleDate"))
                Else
                    schdOutDate = CDate(drDestRows("ScheduleOutDate"))
                End If

                Dim ShiftID As Int32 = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("ShiftID")) = True, 0, drDestRows("ShiftID"))
                Dim GraceTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("GraceTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("GraceTime").ToString)
                Dim strStartingTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("StartingTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("StartingTime"))
                Dim strEndingTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("EndingTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("EndingTime"))


                Dim strAttendanceStatus As String
                Dim tsGraceTime As TimeSpan
                Dim tsDiff As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsTemp As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsSchdTimeSpan As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsTimeSpent As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsLateTime As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                If GraceTime.Length = 8 Then
                    tsGraceTime = New TimeSpan(GraceTime.Substring(0, 2), GraceTime.Substring(3, 2), GraceTime.Substring(6, 2))
                End If

                strAttendanceStatus = drDestRows("AttendanceStatus").ToString
                If strAttendanceStatus = "P" Then
                    Dim dtTranInDate As Date = CDate(drDestRows("InDate"))
                    Dim dtTranOutDate As Date = CDate(drDestRows("OutDate"))

                    Dim strInTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("InTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("InTime"))
                    Dim strOutTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("OutTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("OutTime"))

                    Dim InTimeIsValid As Boolean = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("InTimeIsValid")) = True, False, drDestRows("InTimeIsValid"))
                    Dim OutTimeIsValid As Boolean = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("OutTimeIsValid")) = True, False, drDestRows("OutTimeIsValid"))

                    'TS - Late Time
                    '-------------------------------------------------------
                    If InTimeIsValid = True Then
                        tsDiff = ReportHelper.GetTimeBetween(schdInDate, strStartingTime, dtTranInDate, strInTime)
                        If tsDiff.CompareTo(tsGraceTime) > 0 And ShiftID > 0 Then
                            dtAttend.Rows(j).Item("IsLate") = 1
                        End If
                    End If

                End If
            Next

            For i As Integer = 0 To intTotal - 1

                Dim drDept As DataRow = dtData.Rows(i)
                Dim intDeptID As Integer = CInt(drDept("DivisionID"))
                Dim intBranchID As Integer = CInt(drDept("BranchID"))


                Dim intShiftID As Integer = dtShift.Rows(0).Item("ShiftID")
                Dim strShiftTitle As String = dtShift.Rows(0).Item("ShiftTitle")
                Dim dr As dsAttendance.AttendanceSummaryDivisionRow = dtAttendanceDivision.NewAttendanceSummaryDivisionRow

                dr.DivisionID = drDept("DivisionRefNo")
                dr.DivisionTitle = drDept("DivisionTitle")
                dr.BranchID = intBranchID
                dr.BranchTitle = drDept("BranchTitle")
                dr.InDate = datTemp.Date

                Dim intStrengthSchedule As Integer '= ReportHelper.GetDivisionStrengthAsPerSchedule(CompanyID, intDeptID, intBranchID, datTemp, intShiftID)
                Dim intPresent As Integer '= ReportHelper.GetDivisionPresent(CompanyID, intDeptID, intBranchID, datTemp, intShiftID)
                Dim intOnLeave As Integer '= ReportHelper.GetDivisionLeavesAsPerSchedule(CompanyID, intDeptID, intBranchID, datTemp, intShiftID)
                Dim intAbsent As Integer '= intStrengthSchedule - intPresent - intOnLeave
                Dim intStrength As Integer
                Dim intLate As Integer
                Dim intHoliday As Integer
                intStrength = dtStrength.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID)
                intStrengthSchedule = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID)
                intPresent = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID & " And AttendanceStatus='P'")
                intOnLeave = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID & " And AttendanceStatus='L'")
                intAbsent = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID & " And AttendanceStatus='A'")
                intHoliday = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID & " And AttendanceStatus='H'")
                intLate = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID & " And IsLate=1")

                dr.Strength = intStrength
                dr.Present = intPresent
                dr.Absent = intAbsent
                dr.Late = intLate
                dr.Leave = intOnLeave
                dr.ScheduleStrength = intStrengthSchedule
                dr.ShiftID = intShiftID
                dr.ShiftTitle = strShiftTitle
                dr.Holiday = intHoliday
                dtAttendanceDivision.Rows.Add(dr)
                'Next
            Next

            dtAttendanceDivision.DefaultView.RowFilter = "ScheduleStrength > 0"

            If dtAttendanceDivision.DefaultView.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceSummaryDivision.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendanceDivision.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Division" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DivisionCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("DateTitle", "Date " & FromDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat.ToString())) '& " to " & ToDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat.ToString()))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", rptValues)
                'Me.ReportObject = rptDoc

                'objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, lblHeader.Text, rptDoc)
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)
                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception

            Return False
