    Private Function GetAttendanceSummaryDivisionWithShift() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceSummaryDivisionWithShift = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendanceDivision As New dsAttendance.AttendanceSummaryDivisionDataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As New DataTable

            dtAttendanceDivision.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendanceDivision)
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            dtData = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetEmployeeInfo(strEmployees, CompanyID)
            dtData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable(True, New String() { _
                    "BranchID", "BranchTitle", "BranchRefNo", "DivisionID", "DivisionRefNo", "DivisionTitle"})
            Dim boShift As New BO_Shift
            Dim dtShift As DataTable = boShift.tblShift.GetDatabyCompany(CompanyID)

            Dim intTotal As Integer = dtData.Rows.Count
            Dim strFilter As String = ""
            If ddlShift.SelectedValue <> 0 Then
                If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
                strFilter = strFilter & " ShiftID=" & ddlShift.SelectedValue
                dtShift.DefaultView.RowFilter = strFilter
                dtShift = dtShift.DefaultView.ToTable()
            End If
            Dim datTemp As Date = FromDateServer.Date


            Dim dtAttend As New DataTable("AttendanceDetail")
            Dim DCol1 = New DataColumn("EmployeeID", GetType(System.Int64))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol1)
            Dim DCol2 = New DataColumn("BranchID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol2)
            Dim DCol3 = New DataColumn("DivisionID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol3)
            Dim DCol4 = New DataColumn("ShiftID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol4)
            Dim DCol5 = New DataColumn("ScheduleDate", GetType(System.DateTime))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol5)
            Dim PKeysA() As DataColumn = {DCol1, DCol2, DCol3, DCol4, DCol5}
            dtAttend.PrimaryKey = PKeysA
            dtAttend.Merge(ReportHelper.GetAttendanceByDateDivision(strEmployees, datTemp, datTemp, CompanyID))
            Dim dtLeave As New DataTable
            dtLeave = ReportHelper.GetLeaveByDateDivision(strEmployees, datTemp, datTemp, CompanyID)
            dtAttend.Merge(dtLeave)

            Dim dtStrength As DataTable = ReportHelper.GetDivisionStrength(CompanyID)

            For j As Integer = 0 To dtAttend.Rows.Count - 1
                Dim drDestRows As DataRow = dtAttend.Rows(j)
                Dim schdInDate As Date = CDate(drDestRows("ScheduleDate"))
                Dim schdOutDate As Date
                If IsDBNull(drDestRows("ScheduleOutDate")) = True Then
                    schdOutDate = CDate(drDestRows("ScheduleDate"))
                Else
                    schdOutDate = CDate(drDestRows("ScheduleOutDate"))
                End If

                Dim ShiftID As Int32 = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("ShiftID")) = True, 0, drDestRows("ShiftID"))
                Dim GraceTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("GraceTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("GraceTime").ToString)
                Dim strStartingTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("StartingTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("StartingTime"))
                Dim strEndingTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("EndingTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("EndingTime"))


                Dim strAttendanceStatus As String
                Dim tsGraceTime As TimeSpan
                Dim tsDiff As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsTemp As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsSchdTimeSpan As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsTimeSpent As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsLateTime As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                If GraceTime.Length = 8 Then
                    tsGraceTime = New TimeSpan(GraceTime.Substring(0, 2), GraceTime.Substring(3, 2), GraceTime.Substring(6, 2))
                End If

                strAttendanceStatus = drDestRows("AttendanceStatus").ToString
                If strAttendanceStatus = "P" Then
                    Dim dtTranInDate As Date = CDate(drDestRows("InDate"))
                    Dim dtTranOutDate As Date = CDate(drDestRows("OutDate"))

                    Dim strInTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("InTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("InTime"))
                    Dim strOutTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("OutTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("OutTime"))

                    Dim InTimeIsValid As Boolean = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("InTimeIsValid")) = True, False, drDestRows("InTimeIsValid"))
                    Dim OutTimeIsValid As Boolean = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("OutTimeIsValid")) = True, False, drDestRows("OutTimeIsValid"))

                    'TS - Late Time
                    '-------------------------------------------------------
                    If InTimeIsValid = True Then
                        tsDiff = ReportHelper.GetTimeBetween(schdInDate, strStartingTime, dtTranInDate, strInTime)
                        If tsDiff.CompareTo(tsGraceTime) > 0 And ShiftID > 0 Then
                            dtAttend.Rows(j).Item("IsLate") = 1
                        End If
                    End If

                End If
            Next

            For i As Integer = 0 To intTotal - 1
                Dim drDept As DataRow = dtData.Rows(i)
                Dim intDeptID As Integer = CInt(drDept("DivisionID"))
                Dim intBranchID As Integer = CInt(drDept("BranchID"))
                For intshift As Int32 = 0 To dtShift.Rows.Count - 1
                    Dim intShiftID As Integer = dtShift.Rows(intshift).Item("ShiftID")
                    Dim strShiftTitle As String = dtShift.Rows(intshift).Item("ShiftTitle")
                    Dim dr As dsAttendance.AttendanceSummaryDivisionRow = dtAttendanceDivision.NewAttendanceSummaryDivisionRow

                    dr.DivisionID = drDept("DivisionRefNo")
                    dr.DivisionTitle = drDept("DivisionTitle")
                    dr.BranchID = intBranchID
                    dr.BranchTitle = drDept("BranchTitle")
                    dr.InDate = datTemp.Date

                    Dim intStrengthSchedule As Integer '= ReportHelper.GetDivisionStrengthAsPerSchedule(CompanyID, intDeptID, intBranchID, datTemp, intShiftID)
                    Dim intPresent As Integer '= ReportHelper.GetDivisionPresent(CompanyID, intDeptID, intBranchID, datTemp, intShiftID)
                    Dim intOnLeave As Integer '= ReportHelper.GetDivisionLeavesAsPerSchedule(CompanyID, intDeptID, intBranchID, datTemp, intShiftID)
                    Dim intAbsent As Integer '= intStrengthSchedule - intPresent - intOnLeave
                    Dim intStrength As Integer
                    Dim intLate As Integer
                    Dim intHoliday As Integer
                    intStrength = dtStrength.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID)
                    intStrengthSchedule = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID & " And ShiftID = " & intShiftID)
                    intPresent = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID & " And ShiftID = " & intShiftID & " And AttendanceStatus='P'")
                    intOnLeave = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID & " And ShiftID = " & intShiftID & " And AttendanceStatus='L'")
                    intAbsent = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID & " And ShiftID = " & intShiftID & " And AttendanceStatus='A'")
                    intHoliday = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID & " And ShiftID = " & intShiftID & " And AttendanceStatus='H'")
                    intLate = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DivisionID = " & intDeptID & " And ShiftID = " & intShiftID & " And IsLate=1")

                    dr.Strength = intStrength
                    dr.Present = intPresent
                    dr.Absent = intAbsent
                    dr.Late = intLate
                    dr.Leave = intOnLeave
                    dr.ScheduleStrength = intStrengthSchedule
                    dr.ShiftID = intShiftID
                    dr.ShiftTitle = strShiftTitle
                    dr.Holiday = intHoliday
                    dtAttendanceDivision.Rows.Add(dr)
                Next
            Next

            dtAttendanceDivision.DefaultView.RowFilter = "ScheduleStrength > 0"

            If dtAttendanceDivision.DefaultView.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceSummaryDivision.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendanceDivision.DefaultView.ToTable())
                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                'Dim rptValues As String = " "

                'If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                '    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                'ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Division" Then
                '    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DivisionCriteria()
                'ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                '    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                'End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("DateTitle", "Date " & FromDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat.ToString())) '& " to " & ToDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat.ToString()))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                'Me.ReportObject = rptDoc
                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                'objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, lblHeader.Text, rptDoc)
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)
                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try



    End Function
    Private Function GetMissingEntiresNew() As Boolean
        Dim dtAttendance2 As New DataTable

        GetMissingEntiresNew = False
        Try
            Dim dtMissing As New dsAttendance.MisingEntriesDataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As New DataTable


            dtMissing.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtMissing)
            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            'If strRptCriteria = "All" Then
            dtData = ReportHelper.GetMissingEntries(CompanyID, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, strEmployees)


            Dim strempid() As String = strEmployees.Split(",")
            Dim strFilter As String = ""
            If ddlShift.SelectedValue <> 0 Then
                If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
                strFilter = strFilter & " ShiftID=" & ddlShift.SelectedValue
            End If
            dtData.DefaultView.RowFilter = strFilter
            dtData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable()

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1
                Dim dr As dsAttendance.MisingEntriesRow = dtMissing.NewMisingEntriesRow()

                dr.EmployeeNo = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeNo")
                dr.EmployeeName = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeName")
                dr.DesignationTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationTitle")
                dr.DepartmentID = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentID")

                dr.DepartmentTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentTitle")
                dr.ShiftID = dtData.Rows(i)("ShiftID")
                dr.ShiftTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("ShiftTitle")


                dr.ScheduleInDate = dtData.Rows(i)("ScheduleInDate")
                dr.ScheduleOutDate = dtData.Rows(i)("ScheduleOutDate")
                dr.InTime = dtData.Rows(i)("InTime")
                dr.OutTime = dtData.Rows(i)("OutTime")

                dr.StartingTime = dtData.Rows(i)("StartingTime")
                dr.EndingTime = dtData.Rows(i)("EndingTime")

                dtMissing.Rows.Add(dr)

            Next



            If dtMissing.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                    SetReportPath("rptMissingEntries_ar.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptMissingEntries.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtMissing.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)


                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetMissingScheduleOnDate() As Boolean

        GetMissingScheduleOnDate = False
        Try
            Dim dtShift As New dsAttendance.ShiftScheduleRegDataTable
            Dim dtMaster As New dsMaster.EmployeeDataTable

            'Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As DataTable

            dtShift.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtShift)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            dtData = ReportHelper.GetListMissingShiftScheduleRegister(strEmployees, FromDateServer, CompanyID, strRptCriteria, strRptFromCriteria, strRptToCriteria, strClientDateFormat)

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1
                Dim dr As dsAttendance.ShiftScheduleRegRow = dtShift.NewShiftScheduleRegRow()

                dr.EmployeeID = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeName = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeName").ToString()
                If dtData.Columns.Contains("DesignationRefNo") Then
                    dr.DesignationID = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationRefNo").ToString()
                    dr.DesignationTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationTitle").ToString()
                Else
                    dr.DesignationID = "0"
                    dr.DesignationTitle = ""
                End If
                dr.BranchID = dtData.Rows(i)("BranchRefNo").ToString()
                dr.DepartmentID = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentRefNo").ToString()
                dr.DepartmentTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentTitle").ToString()
                dr.BranchTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("BranchTitle").ToString()

                dtShift.Rows.Add(dr)
            Next

            If IsNothing(dtShift) = False AndAlso dtShift.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                    SetReportPath("rptListMissingShiftScheduleEmployeeWiseGroup_ar.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptListMissingShiftScheduleEmployeeWiseGroup.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtShift.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptDates", strFromDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptValue", rptValues)


                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                For i As Integer = 0 To 30
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & (i + 1), (i + 1))
                Next

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)
                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Sub GetAvgrageInTimeOutTime(ByRef dt As DataTable, ByRef pInTime As String, ByRef pOutTime As String, ByVal pmonth As Integer)
        Dim datInDate As Date
        Dim strDate As String
        Dim InTimecount As Integer = 0
        Dim OutTimecount As Integer = 0
        Dim strTime As String
        Dim dblTotHr As Double
        Dim tsTotInTime As TimeSpan
        Dim tsTotOutTime As TimeSpan

        For i As Integer = 0 To dt.Rows.Count - 1
            pInTime = i + 1
            pOutTime = i + 2
            strDate = Convert.ToDateTime(dt.Rows(i)("InDate")).ToString(strClientDateFormat)
            strDate = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(strDate, strClientDateFormat)
            datInDate = Convert.ToDateTime(strDate)
            If datInDate.Month = pmonth Then
                If dt.Rows(i)("InTime") <> "" AndAlso dt.Rows(i)("InTime") <> "--" AndAlso dt.Rows(i)("InTimeIsValid") = True Then
                    InTimecount += 1
                    strTime = dt.Rows(i)("InTime")
                    Dim tsInTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(strTime.Substring(0, 2), strTime.Substring(3, 2), strTime.Substring(6, 2))
                    tsTotInTime = tsTotInTime.Add(tsInTime)
                End If
            End If
            If datInDate.Month = pmonth Then
                If dt.Rows(i)("OutTime") <> "" AndAlso dt.Rows(i)("OutTime") <> "--" AndAlso dt.Rows(i)("OutTimeIsValid") = True Then
                    OutTimecount += 1
                    strTime = dt.Rows(i)("OutTime")
                    Dim tsOutTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(strTime.Substring(0, 2), strTime.Substring(3, 2), strTime.Substring(6, 2))
                    tsTotOutTime = tsTotOutTime.Add(tsOutTime)
                End If
            End If
        Next

        If datInDate.Month = pmonth Then
            If tsTotInTime.TotalHours > 0.0 Then
                dblTotHr = tsTotInTime.TotalHours / InTimecount
                pInTime = ReportHelper.convertTotime(dblTotHr)
            Else
                pInTime = ""
            End If
            If tsTotOutTime.TotalHours > 0.0 Then
                dblTotHr = tsTotOutTime.TotalHours / OutTimecount
                pOutTime = ReportHelper.convertTotime(dblTotHr)

            Else
                pOutTime = ""
            End If
        Else
            pInTime = ""
            pOutTime = ""
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Function GetAttendanceSummaryDepartmentDivision() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceSummaryDepartmentDivision = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendanceDept As New dsAttendance.AttendanceSummaryDeptDataTable

            'dtAttendanceDept.Columns.Add(New DataColumn("MissingStrength", GetType(Integer)))

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As New DataTable

            dtAttendanceDept.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendanceDept)
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            dtData = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetEmployeeInfo(strEmployees, CompanyID)
            dtData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable(True, New String() { _
                    "BranchID", "BranchTitle", "BranchRefNo", "DepartmentID", "DepartmentRefNo", "DepartmentTitle", "DivisionID", "DivisionRefNo", "DivisionTitle"})
            Dim boShift As New BO_Shift
            Dim dtShift As DataTable = boShift.tblShift.GetDatabyCompany(CompanyID)

            Dim intTotal As Integer = dtData.Rows.Count
            Dim strFilter As String = ""
            If ddlShift.SelectedValue <> 0 Then
                If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
                strFilter = strFilter & " ShiftID=" & ddlShift.SelectedValue
                dtShift.DefaultView.RowFilter = strFilter
                dtShift = dtShift.DefaultView.ToTable()
            End If




            Dim dtAttend As New DataTable("AttendanceDetail")
            Dim DCol1 = New DataColumn("EmployeeID", GetType(System.Int64))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol1)
            Dim DCol2 = New DataColumn("BranchID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol2)
            Dim DCol3 = New DataColumn("DepartmentID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol3)
            Dim DCol4 = New DataColumn("ShiftID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol4)
            Dim DCol5 = New DataColumn("ScheduleDate", GetType(System.DateTime))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol5)
            Dim DCol6 = New DataColumn("DivisionID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol6)
            Dim PKeysA() As DataColumn = {DCol1, DCol2, DCol3, DCol4, DCol5, DCol6}
            dtAttend.PrimaryKey = PKeysA
            dtAttend.Merge(ReportHelper.GetAttendanceByDateDepartment(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID))


            For i As Integer = 0 To intTotal - 1
                Dim datTemp As Date = FromDateServer.Date
                Dim dr As dsAttendance.AttendanceSummaryDeptRow ' = dtAttendanceDept.NewAttendanceSummaryDeptRow
                Dim drDept As DataRow = dtData.Rows(i)
                Dim intDeptID As Integer = CInt(drDept("DepartmentID"))
                Dim intBranchID As Integer = CInt(drDept("BranchID"))
                Dim intDivisionID As Integer = CInt(drDept("DivisionID"))
                'For intshift As Int32 = 0 To dtShift.Rows.Count - 1
                'For intshift As Int32 = 0 To 0
                Dim intTotalDays As Int32 = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer) + 1
                For intDay As Int32 = 0 To DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)
                    Dim intStrengthSchedule As Integer '= ReportHelper.GetDepartmentStrengthAsPerSchedule(CompanyID, intDeptID, intBranchID, datTemp, intShiftID)
                    Dim intPresent As Integer '= ReportHelper.GetDepartmentPresent(CompanyID, intDeptID, intBranchID, datTemp, intShiftID)
                    Dim intOnLeave As Integer '= ReportHelper.GetDepartmentLeavesAsPerSchedule(CompanyID, intDeptID, intBranchID, datTemp, intShiftID)
                    Dim intAbsent As Integer '= intStrengthSchedule - intPresent - intOnLeave
                    Dim intStrength As Integer
                    Dim intShiftID As Integer, strShiftTitle As String
                    dr = dtAttendanceDept.NewAttendanceSummaryDeptRow

                    dr.DepartmentID = drDept("DepartmentRefNo")
                    dr.DepartmentTitle = drDept("DepartmentTitle")
                    dr.DivisionID = drDept("DivisionRefNo")
                    dr.DivisionTitle = drDept("DivisionTitle")
                    dr.BranchID = intBranchID
                    dr.DivisionID = intDivisionID
                    dr.BranchTitle = drDept("BranchTitle")
                    dr.InDate = datTemp.Date
                    intShiftID = 0 'dtShift.Rows(intshift).Item("ShiftID")
                    strShiftTitle = "" ' dtShift.Rows(intshift).Item("ShiftTitle")

                    intPresent = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "ScheduleDate = '#" & datTemp & "#' And BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DepartmentID = " & intDeptID & " And DivisionID = " & intDivisionID & " And AttendanceStatus='P'")

                    dr.Strength = intStrength
                    dr.Present = intPresent
                    dr.Absent = intAbsent
                    dr.Late = 0 'intLate
                    dr.Leave = intOnLeave
                    dr.ScheduleStrength = intStrengthSchedule
                    dr.ShiftID = intShiftID
                    dr.ShiftTitle = strShiftTitle
                    dtAttendanceDept.Rows.Add(dr)

                    datTemp = datTemp.AddDays(1)
                Next
                'Creating Avg Column
                dr = dtAttendanceDept.NewAttendanceSummaryDeptRow
                dr.DepartmentID = drDept("DepartmentRefNo")
                dr.DepartmentTitle = drDept("DepartmentTitle")
                dr.DivisionID = drDept("DivisionRefNo")
                dr.DivisionTitle = drDept("DivisionTitle")
                dr.BranchID = intBranchID
                dr.DivisionID = intDivisionID
                dr.BranchTitle = drDept("BranchTitle")
                dr.InDate = "12/31/9999"
                dr.Strength = 0
                dr.Present = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)/" & intTotalDays, "BranchID=" & intBranchID & " And DepartmentID = " & intDeptID & " And DivisionID = " & intDivisionID & " And AttendanceStatus='P'")
                dr.Absent = 0
                dr.Late = 0
                dr.Leave = 0
                dr.ScheduleStrength = 0
                dr.ShiftID = 0
                dr.ShiftTitle = ""
                dtAttendanceDept.Rows.Add(dr)
            Next

            If dtAttendanceDept.DefaultView.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceSummaryDeptDiv.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendanceDept.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                'Dim rptValues As String = " "

                'If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                '    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                'ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                '    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                'ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                '    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                'End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("DateTitle", "Date " & FromDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat.ToString())) '& " to " & ToDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat.ToString()))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                If chkDivisionGroupInclude.Checked = True Then
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("GroupByDivision", "Yes")
                ElseIf chkDivisionGroupInclude.Checked = False Then
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("GroupByDivision", "No")
                End If

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try



    End Function

    Private Function GetAttendanceSummaryForPayroll() As Boolean
        Dim FileName As String = ""

        CreateEmployeeFilter(blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled)
        FromDateServer = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(txtFromDate.Text, strClientDateFormat)
        ToDateServer = DTHelper.ConvertToServerFormat(txtToDate.Text, strClientDateFormat)
        Dim dtTemp As DataTable = ReportHelper.GetYearlyAttendance(CompanyID, ddlyear.SelectedValue, ddlMonth.SelectedValue, strEmployees, True)
        'FillEmployeeInfo(strEmployees, dtTemp)
        dtTemp.Columns.Remove("EmployeeID")
        dtTemp.Columns.Remove("DepartmentTitle")
        dtTemp.Columns.Remove("DesignationTitle")
        dtTemp.Columns.Remove("StopPayDays")

        FileName = "Attendance Summary"
        PreviewReport(dtTemp, FileName, vbTab)
        Return False
    End Function
    Public Sub PreviewReport(ByVal dt As System.Data.DataTable, ByVal FileName As String, ByVal delimiter As String)

        If dt.Rows.Count > 0 Then
            Dim strRptName As String = FileName '& "-" & ddlyear.SelectedValue & "-" & ddlMonth.SelectedValue
            Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
            Dim objCommonFunctions As New clsCommonFunctions
            objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, dt)
            Dim strEncode As String = Server.UrlEncode(hdRptTitle.Value)
            GeneratePopUp(strEncode)
        Else
            strErrorText = "No Record Found"
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, strErrorText)
        End If
    End Sub
    Private Sub GeneratePopUp(ByVal pstrEncode As String)
        Dim strPopup As String
        If pstrEncode <> Nothing Then
            strPopup = "window.open('frmDataViewer.aspx?ID=" & pstrEncode & "','_blank');"
            If strPopup <> "" Then
                ScriptManager.RegisterStartupScript(Me.Page, Me.Page.GetType(), "Pop", strPopup, True)
            End If
        End If
    End Sub
    Protected Sub rdoInOnly_CheckedChanged(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As System.EventArgs) Handles rdoInOnly.CheckedChanged

    End Sub
    Public Sub ShowReportPanel()
        Dim IsInOut As Boolean
        If RptType.DailyAttendanceInOrOut = hdRptType.Value Then
            pnlInOut.Visible = True
        Else
            pnlInOut.Visible = False
        End If
        IsInOut = pnlInOut.Visible
        pnlInclude.Visible = Not IsInOut
    End Sub
    Public Sub SetParameterForDailyAttendanceInOrOut()
        If RptType.DailyAttendanceInOrOut = hdRptType.Value Then
            Dim strTimingType As String
            If rdoInOnly.Checked = True Then
                strTimingType = "(In)"
            Else
                strTimingType = "(Out)"
            End If
            rptDoc.SetParameterValue("hrInOrOutType", strTimingType)
        End If
    End Sub
    Private Function GetShiftScheduleRegister() As Boolean

        GetShiftScheduleRegister = False
        Try
            Dim dtShift As New dsAttendance.ShiftScheduleRegDataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As DataTable, datFromDate As Date, datToDate As Date

            dtShift.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtShift)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            datFromDate = New Date(ddlyear.SelectedValue, ddlMonth.SelectedValue, 1)

            If clsCookie.CalendarType.ToString.ToLower <> "gregorian" Then
                datToDate = DTHelper.GetConvertedClientMonthEndDate(datFromDate.Year, datFromDate.Month, clsCookie.CalendarType)
                datFromDate = DTHelper.GetGregorianServerDate(datFromDate.Year, datFromDate.Month, datFromDate.Day, clsCookie.CalendarType)
                datToDate = DTHelper.GetGregorianServerDate(datToDate.Year, datToDate.Month, datToDate.Day, clsCookie.CalendarType)
            Else
                datToDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, -1, DateAdd(DateInterval.Month, 1, datFromDate))
            End If

            dtData = ReportHelper.GetShiftScheduleRegister(strEmployees, datFromDate, datToDate, intCompanyID)

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1
                Dim dr As dsAttendance.ShiftScheduleRegRow = dtShift.NewShiftScheduleRegRow()

                dr.EmployeeID = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeName = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeName").ToString()
                dr.DepartmentID = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentID").ToString()
                dr.DepartmentTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentTitle").ToString()
                dr.DesignationTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationTitle").ToString()
                dr.Day1 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col01").ToString())
                dr.Day2 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col02").ToString())
                dr.Day3 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col03").ToString())
                dr.Day4 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col04").ToString())
                dr.Day5 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col05").ToString())
                dr.Day6 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col06").ToString())
                dr.Day7 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col07").ToString())
                dr.Day8 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col08").ToString())
                dr.Day9 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col09").ToString())
                dr.Day10 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col10").ToString())
                dr.Day11 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col11").ToString())
                dr.Day12 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col12").ToString())
                dr.Day13 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col13").ToString())
                dr.Day14 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col14").ToString())
                dr.Day15 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col15").ToString())
                dr.Day16 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col16").ToString())
                dr.Day17 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col17").ToString())
                dr.Day18 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col18").ToString())
                dr.Day19 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col19").ToString())
                dr.Day20 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col20").ToString())
                dr.Day21 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col21").ToString())
                dr.Day22 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col22").ToString())
                dr.Day23 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col23").ToString())
                dr.Day24 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col24").ToString())
                dr.Day25 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col25").ToString())
                dr.Day26 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col26").ToString())
                dr.Day27 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col27").ToString())
                dr.Day28 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col28").ToString())
                dr.Day29 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col29").ToString())
                dr.Day30 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col30").ToString())
                dr.Day31 = LCase(dtData.Rows(i)("Col31").ToString())


                dtShift.Rows.Add(dr)
            Next

            If IsNothing(dtShift) = False AndAlso dtShift.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                    SetReportPath("rptShiftScheduleEmployeeWiseGroup_ar.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptShiftScheduleEmployeeWiseGroup.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtShift.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptDates", ddlyear.SelectedItem.Text + " - " + ddlMonth.SelectedItem.Text)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptValue", rptValues)


                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                For i As Integer = 0 To 30
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & (i + 1), (i + 1))
                Next

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)
                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetShiftScheduleHours() As Boolean

        GetShiftScheduleHours = False
        Try
            '-----Filter of Employees as Per User Rights---------
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtEmployeeMaster As DataTable = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetDatabyCompany(CompanyID)
            Dim boDepartment As New BO_Department
            Dim dtDeptMst As DataTable = boDepartment.tblDepartment.GetDatabyCompany(CompanyID)
            Dim dsTemp As New DataSet

            Dim dtData As DataTable
            dtData = ReportHelper.GetShiftScheduleHours(FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, True)

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtEmployeeMaster)
                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtDeptMst)
                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtData)

                Dim dcadd As DataColumn
                dsTemp.Relations.Add("MstDetail", dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables(2).Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                dsTemp.Relations.Add("MstDetailDept", dsTemp.Tables(1).Columns("DepartmentID"), dsTemp.Tables(2).Columns("DepartmentID"), False)

                dcadd = New DataColumn("EmployeeRefNo", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Parent(MstDetail).EmployeeRefNo,'')")
                dsTemp.Tables(2).Columns.Add(dcadd)

                dcadd = New DataColumn("EmployeeName", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Parent(MstDetail).EmployeeName,'')")
                dsTemp.Tables(2).Columns.Add(dcadd)

                dcadd = New DataColumn("DepartmentTitle", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Parent(MstDetailDept).DepartmentTitle,'')")
                dsTemp.Tables(2).Columns.Add(dcadd)


                SetReportPath("rptShiftScheduleHours.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsTemp.Tables(2).DefaultView.ToTable())

                'dsTemp.WriteXmlSchema("C:\abc1.xsd")

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                'If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                '    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                'ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                '    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                'ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                '    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                'End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("DateTitle", DTHelper.ConvertToClientDateFormat(FromDateServer, strClientDateFormat) + " - " + DTHelper.ConvertToClientDateFormat(ToDateServer, strClientDateFormat))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                'rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)


                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)
                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetEmployeeDailyStatus() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeDailyStatus = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""


        Try
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer, intMonth As Integer, intYear As Integer

            intMonth = Month(dtFrom)
            intYear = Year(dtFrom)

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtFinalData As New DataTable, dtTempData As New DataTable
            Dim dsTempData As New DataSet

            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblMain")
            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDetail")

            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeDailyStatusByDateCompany(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, intMonth, intYear, False)
            dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").Merge(dtTempData.DefaultView.ToTable)
            dtTempData.Dispose()

            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeDailyStatusByDateCompany(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, intMonth, intYear, True)
            dsTempData.Tables("tblDetail").Merge(dtTempData.DefaultView.ToTable)
            dtTempData.Dispose()

            dsTempData.Relations.Add("Main_Detail", dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTempData.Tables("tblDetail").Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
            dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").Columns.Add("LateCount", GetType(System.Int64), "IsNull(Max(Child(Main_Detail).LateCount),0)")
            dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").Columns.Add("NetTime", GetType(String), "IsNull(Max(Child(Main_Detail).NetTime),'')")

            dtFinalData = dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").DefaultView.ToTable

            If dtFinalData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptDailyStatusReport.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtFinalData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportDate", Format(FromDateServer, strClientDateFormat))


                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetAttendanceHistory() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceHistory = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""

        Try
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer, dtTo As Date = ToDateServer, dtDate As Date
            dtDate = dtFrom
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtFinalData As New DataTable
            Dim dsTempData As New DataSet, intLoopCount As Integer
            dsTempData.Tables.Add("dsDayWiseData")

            For intLoopCount = 1 To DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, dtFrom, dtTo) + 1

                Dim dtAttendanceData As New DataTable

                dtAttendanceData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceHistoryDayByDay(dtDate, CompanyID, strEmployees)
                dsTempData.Tables(0).Merge(dtAttendanceData.DefaultView.ToTable)
                dtDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, dtDate)
                dtAttendanceData.Dispose()

            Next

            dtFinalData = dsTempData.Tables("dsDayWiseData").DefaultView.ToTable

            If dtFinalData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceHistory.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtFinalData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", strFromDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "Print By: " & strUserName & " On Date " & Format(Today.Date, "dddd MM yyyy"))

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetLastWeekAttendance() As Boolean

        GetLastWeekAttendance = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""


        Try
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer, dtTo As Date = FromDateServer, strDateTitle(7) As String
            dtFrom = DateAdd("d", -6, dtTo)

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtFinalData As New DataTable
            Dim dsTempData As New DataSet, intLoopCount As Integer

            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblMain")
            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay1")
            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay2")
            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay3")
            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay4")
            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay5")
            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay6")
            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay7")

            For intLoopCount = 1 To 7

                Dim dtAttendanceData As New DataTable, dtMissingScheduleData As New DataTable

                dtAttendanceData = ReportHelper.GetLastWeekAttendanceByCompanyDayByDay(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, False)
                dsTempData.Tables(intLoopCount).Merge(dtAttendanceData.DefaultView.ToTable)

                dtMissingScheduleData = ReportHelper.GetLastWeekAttendanceByCompanyDayByDay(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, True)
                dsTempData.Tables(intLoopCount).Merge(dtMissingScheduleData.DefaultView.ToTable)

                strDateTitle(intLoopCount) = Format(dtFrom, "dddd dd MMMM")
                dtFrom = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, dtFrom)

                dtAttendanceData.Dispose()
                dtMissingScheduleData.Dispose()

            Next

            dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").Merge(dsTempData.Tables("tblDay1").DefaultView.ToTable(True, "EmployeeID", "EmployeeRefNo", "EmployeeName", "DesignationTitle", "DepartmentTitle"))


            For intLoopCount = 1 To 7

                dsTempData.Relations.Add("Main_Day" & intLoopCount & "", dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTempData.Tables("tblDay" & intLoopCount & "").Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").Columns.Add("Day" & intLoopCount & "", GetType(String), "IsNull(Max(Child(Main_Day" & intLoopCount & ").Attendance),'')")

            Next

            dtFinalData = dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").DefaultView.ToTable

            If dtFinalData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptLastWeekAttendance.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtFinalData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Print By: " & strUserName & " On Date " & Format(Today.Date, "dddd MM yyyy"))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("dtDay1", strDateTitle(1))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("dtDay2", strDateTitle(2))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("dtDay3", strDateTitle(3))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("dtDay4", strDateTitle(4))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("dtDay5", strDateTitle(5))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("dtDay6", strDateTitle(6))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("dtDay7", strDateTitle(7))

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
