    Private Function GetDailyAttendanceSheetEfro() As Boolean

        GetDailyAttendanceSheetEfro = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""


        Try
            Dim dc As DataColumn
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
            Dim dtPrevious As Date = DateAdd("d", -1, dtFrom)

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtBaseData As New DataTable, dtLastDayAttendance As New DataTable, _
                dtInFlag As New DataTable, dtOutFlag As New DataTable, dtFinalData As New DataTable
            Dim dsTemp As New DataSet

            If IsAttendanceFlag = False Then
                dtBaseData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetByDateCompany(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, False)
                dtLastDayAttendance = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetByDateCompany(dtPrevious, CompanyID, strEmployees, True)

                dtBaseData.TableName = "dtBaseData"
                dtLastDayAttendance.TableName = "dtLastDayAttendance"

                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtBaseData)
                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtLastDayAttendance)

                dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns.Add("InFlagTitle")
                dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns.Add("OutFlagTitle")

                dsTemp.Relations.Add("BaseData_LastDayAttendance", dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables("dtLastDayAttendance").Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                dc = New DataColumn("LastDayOut", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Max(Child(BaseData_LastDayAttendance).LastDayOut),'')")
                dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

            Else
                dtBaseData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetFlagWiseByDateCompany(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, False, False, False)
                dtInFlag = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetFlagWiseByDateCompany(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, False, True, False)
                dtOutFlag = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetFlagWiseByDateCompany(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, False, False, True)
                dtLastDayAttendance = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetFlagWiseByDateCompany(dtPrevious, CompanyID, strEmployees, True, False, False)

                dtBaseData.TableName = "dtBaseData"
                dtInFlag.TableName = "dtInFlag"
                dtOutFlag.TableName = "dtOutFlag"
                dtLastDayAttendance.TableName = "dtLastDayAttendance"

                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtBaseData)
                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtInFlag)
                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtOutFlag)
                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtLastDayAttendance)

                dsTemp.Relations.Add("BaseData_InFlag", dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables("dtInFlag").Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                dc = New DataColumn("InFlagTitle", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Max(Child(BaseData_InFlag).InFlagTitle),'')")
                dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                dsTemp.Relations.Add("BaseData_OutFlag", dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables("dtOutFlag").Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                dc = New DataColumn("OutFlagTitle", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Max(Child(BaseData_OutFlag).OutFlagTitle),'')")
                dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                dsTemp.Relations.Add("BaseData_LastDayAttendance", dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables("dtLastDayAttendance").Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                dc = New DataColumn("LastDayOut", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Max(Child(BaseData_LastDayAttendance).LastDayOut),'')")
                dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

            End If

            dtFinalData = dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").DefaultView.ToTable()

            If dtBaseData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptDailyAttendanceSheetEfro.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtFinalData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", strFromDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "Print By: " & strUserName & " On Date " & Format(Today.Date, "dddd MM yyyy"))

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetDailyAttendanceSheet() As Boolean

        GetDailyAttendanceSheet = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""


        Try
            Dim dc As DataColumn
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
            Dim dtPrevious As Date = DateAdd("d", -1, dtFrom)

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtBaseData As New DataTable, dtLastDayAttendance As New DataTable, _
                dtInFlag As New DataTable, dtOutFlag As New DataTable, dtFinalData As New DataTable
            Dim dsTemp As New DataSet

            If IsAttendanceFlag = False Then
                dtBaseData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetByDateCompany(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, False)
                dtLastDayAttendance = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetByDateCompany(dtPrevious, CompanyID, strEmployees, True)

                dtBaseData.TableName = "dtBaseData"
                dtLastDayAttendance.TableName = "dtLastDayAttendance"

                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtBaseData)
                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtLastDayAttendance)

                dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns.Add("InFlagTitle")
                dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns.Add("OutFlagTitle")

                dsTemp.Relations.Add("BaseData_LastDayAttendance", dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables("dtLastDayAttendance").Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                dc = New DataColumn("LastDayOut", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Max(Child(BaseData_LastDayAttendance).LastDayOut),'')")
                dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

            Else
                dtBaseData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetFlagWiseByDateCompany(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, False, False, False)
                dtInFlag = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetFlagWiseByDateCompany(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, False, True, False)
                dtOutFlag = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetFlagWiseByDateCompany(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees, False, False, True)
                dtLastDayAttendance = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSheetFlagWiseByDateCompany(dtPrevious, CompanyID, strEmployees, True, False, False)

                dtBaseData.TableName = "dtBaseData"
                dtInFlag.TableName = "dtInFlag"
                dtOutFlag.TableName = "dtOutFlag"
                dtLastDayAttendance.TableName = "dtLastDayAttendance"

                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtBaseData)
                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtInFlag)
                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtOutFlag)
                dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtLastDayAttendance)

                dsTemp.Relations.Add("BaseData_InFlag", dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables("dtInFlag").Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                dc = New DataColumn("InFlagTitle", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Max(Child(BaseData_InFlag).InFlagTitle),'')")
                dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                dsTemp.Relations.Add("BaseData_OutFlag", dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables("dtOutFlag").Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                dc = New DataColumn("OutFlagTitle", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Max(Child(BaseData_OutFlag).OutFlagTitle),'')")
                dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                dsTemp.Relations.Add("BaseData_LastDayAttendance", dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables("dtLastDayAttendance").Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                dc = New DataColumn("LastDayOut", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Max(Child(BaseData_LastDayAttendance).LastDayOut),'')")
                dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

            End If

            dtFinalData = dsTemp.Tables("dtBaseData").DefaultView.ToTable()

            If dtBaseData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptDailyAttendanceSheet.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtFinalData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", strFromDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetAttendanceInOutTimeOnDateWise() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceInOutTimeOnDateWise = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""


        Try
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer, dtLoopDate As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
            Dim strDateTitle(31) As String

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtFinalData As New DataTable
            Dim dsTempData As New DataSet, intLoopCount As Integer

            If DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, dtFrom, dtTo) + 1 < 32 Then
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblMain")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay1")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay2")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay3")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay4")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay5")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay6")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay7")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay8")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay9")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay10")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay11")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay12")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay13")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay14")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay15")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay16")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay17")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay18")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay19")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay20")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay21")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay22")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay23")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay24")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay25")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay26")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay27")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay28")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay29")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay30")
                dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDay31")


                For intLoopCount = 1 To DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, dtFrom, dtTo) + 1

                    Dim dtAttendanceData As New DataTable, dtMissingScheduleData As New DataTable

                    dtAttendanceData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceByCompanyDayByDay(dtLoopDate, CompanyID, strEmployees)
                    dsTempData.Tables(intLoopCount).Merge(dtAttendanceData.DefaultView.ToTable)

                    strDateTitle(intLoopCount) = Format(dtLoopDate, "dddd dd MMMM")
                    dtLoopDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, dtLoopDate)

                    dtAttendanceData.Dispose()

                Next

                If DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 30, dtFrom) > dtTo Then
                    Dim intDiffDate As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, dtTo, DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 30, dtFrom))
                    Dim intInnerLoopCount As Integer = 0
                    dtLoopDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, dtTo)

                    For intInnerLoopCount = intLoopCount To 31

                        Dim dtAttendanceData As New DataTable, dtMissingScheduleData As New DataTable

                        dtAttendanceData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceByCompanyDayByDayBlank(dtLoopDate, CompanyID, strEmployees)
                        dsTempData.Tables(intInnerLoopCount).Merge(dtAttendanceData.DefaultView.ToTable)

                        strDateTitle(intInnerLoopCount) = Format(dtLoopDate, "dddd dd MMMM")
                        dtLoopDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, dtLoopDate)

                        dtAttendanceData.Dispose()

                    Next

                End If

                dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").Merge(dsTempData.Tables("tblDay1").DefaultView.ToTable(True, "EmployeeID", "EmployeeRefNo", "EmployeeName", "DesignationTitle", "DepartmentTitle"))


                For intLoopCount = 1 To 31

                    dsTempData.Relations.Add("Main_Day" & intLoopCount & "", dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTempData.Tables("tblDay" & intLoopCount & "").Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                    dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").Columns.Add("Day" & intLoopCount & "", GetType(String), "IsNull(Max(Child(Main_Day" & intLoopCount & ").Status),'')")


                Next

                dtFinalData = dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").DefaultView.ToTable

                If dtFinalData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                    SetReportPath("rptAttendanceStatusDateWise.rpt")
                    rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtFinalData)

                    'Report Params
                    Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                    Dim rptValues As String = " "

                    If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                        rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                    ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                        rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                    ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                        rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                    End If

                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Print By: " & strUserName & " On Date " & Format(Today.Date, "dddd MM yyyy"))
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportDate", "From Date " & Format(dtFrom, "MMMM dd, yyyy") & " To " & Format(dtTo, "MMMM dd, yyyy"))

                    Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                    objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                    Return True

                Else
                    lblError.Visible = True
                    Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                    EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                    Return False
                End If
            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                strErrorText = "Date Criteria Must Be Select In 31 Days."
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, strErrorText)
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetIrRegularitiesInOTAttendance() As Boolean

        GetIrRegularitiesInOTAttendance = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""

        Try
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer, intLoop As Integer = 1, dtTempData As New DataTable
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtData As DataTable
            dtData = ReportHelper.GetIrRegularitiesInOTAttendanceByCompanyDate(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees)

            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                For intLoop = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1

                    If IsDBNull(dtData.Rows(intLoop)("OutTimeIsIrRegular")) = True Then
                        dtData.Rows(intLoop)("OutTimeIsIrRegular") = "N"
                        dtData.Rows(intLoop)("ActualOutTime") = "00:00:00"
                        dtData.Rows(intLoop)("PlanedOutTime") = "00:00:00"
                    End If
                    If IsDBNull(dtData.Rows(intLoop)("InTimeIsIrRegular")) = True Then
                        dtData.Rows(intLoop)("InTimeIsIrRegular") = "N"
                        dtData.Rows(intLoop)("PlanedInTime") = "00:00:00"
                        dtData.Rows(intLoop)("ActualInTime") = "00:00:00"
                    End If
                Next

                dtData.DefaultView.RowFilter = "(InTimeIsIrRegular = 'Y' OR OutTimeIsIrRegular = 'Y')"
                dtData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable

                SetReportPath("rptIrRegularOTAttendance.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "As On Date " & strFromDateClient)


                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetTodayActivityReport() As Boolean

        GetTodayActivityReport = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""

        Try

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtData As DataTable
            Dim dtTodayActivity As New dsAttendance.TodayActivityReportDataTable

            dtTodayActivity.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtTodayActivity)

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            dtData = ReportHelper.GetActivityLogByDateCompany(dtFrom, CompanyID, strEmployees)

            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Dim intWorkCode As Integer = 0, intEmployeeID As Integer = 0

                Dim dtTranInDateFull As DateTime
                Dim dtTranOutDateFull As DateTime
                Dim Seconds As Double, hours As Double

                intEmployeeID = dtData.Rows(0)("EmployeeID")
                For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 2
                    Dim dr As dsAttendance.TodayActivityReportRow = dtTodayActivity.NewTodayActivityReportRow

                    dr.EmployeeID = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeID")
                    dr.EmployeeRefNo = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo").ToString
                    dr.EmployeeName = UCase(dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeName").ToString)
                    dr.DepartmentTitle = UCase(dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentTitle").ToString)
                    dr.DesignationTitle = UCase(dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationTitle").ToString)
                    dr.DivisionTitle = UCase(dtData.Rows(i)("DivisionTitle").ToString)
                    dr.GroupTitle = UCase(dtData.Rows(i)("GroupTitle").ToString)
                    dr.BranchTitle = UCase(dtData.Rows(i)("BranchTitle").ToString)
                    dr.ActivityDate = dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityDate")
                    dr.ActivityTitle = UCase(dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityTitle"))
                    dr.ActivityTime = dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityTime")

                    If dtData.Rows(i)("CounterWorkCode") = 0 AndAlso _
                        UCase(dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityTitle")) = UCase(dtData.Rows(i + 1)("ActivityTitle")) Then
                        intWorkCode = Val(dtData.Rows(i)("WorkCode")) - 1
                    Else
                        intWorkCode = dtData.Rows(i)("CounterWorkCode")
                    End If

                    If intWorkCode <> 0 AndAlso dtData.Rows(i + 1)("WorkCode") = (intWorkCode) _
                        AndAlso dtData.Rows(i + 1)("EmployeeID") = intEmployeeID Then
                        If dr.ActivityTime > dtData.Rows(i + 1)("ActivityTime") Then
                            dr.ActivityTimeOut = dr.ActivityTime
                            dr.ActivityTime = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("ActivityTime")
                        Else
                            dr.ActivityTimeOut = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("ActivityTime")
                        End If
                        If i + 2 <= dtData.Rows.Count - 2 Then
                            intEmployeeID = dtData.Rows(i + 2)("EmployeeID")
                        Else
                            intEmployeeID = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("EmployeeID")
                        End If

                        dtTranInDateFull = New DateTime(dr.ActivityDate.Year, dr.ActivityDate.Month, dr.ActivityDate.Day, dr.ActivityTime.Substring(0, 2), dr.ActivityTime.Substring(3, 2), 0)
                        dtTranOutDateFull = New DateTime(dr.ActivityDate.Year, dr.ActivityDate.Month, dr.ActivityDate.Day, dr.ActivityTimeOut.Substring(0, 2), dr.ActivityTimeOut.Substring(3, 2), 0)
                        Seconds = DateDiff(DateInterval.Minute, dtTranInDateFull, dtTranOutDateFull)
                        hours = Math.Round(Seconds, 2)
                        dr.Hours = hours
                        i = i + 1
                    Else
                        dr.ActivityTimeOut = "00:00:00"
                        dr.Hours = 0
                        intEmployeeID = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("EmployeeID")
                    End If
                    dtTodayActivity.Rows.Add(dr)
                Next
            End If

            dtData.Clear()
            dtData.Dispose()
            dtData = dtTodayActivity.DefaultView.ToTable

            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptTodayActivity.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "As On Date " & strFromDateClient)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function

    Private Function GetEmployeeOverTimeByFlaging() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeOverTimeByFlaging = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = "", dtData As New DataTable
        Dim dtEmployees As DataTable, dtTempData As New DataTable
        Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer, dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
        Dim intCount As Integer, intEmployeeID As Int64, intInFlagID As Integer, _
        intOutFlagID As Integer, intOffFlagID As Integer

        Try


            If clsCookie.EnablePayroll.ToString.ToUpper = "Y" Then
                IsPayroll = ValidateMonthClose(ToDateServer.Month, ToDateServer.Year, "Payroll", CompanyID)
            End If

            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            dtEmployees = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeListByCompanyOverTimeSetup_New(CompanyID, strEmployees)

            If dtEmployees.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                For intCount = 0 To dtEmployees.Rows.Count - 1

                    intEmployeeID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("EmployeeID")
                    intInFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("InComingFlagID")
                    intOutFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("OutGoingFlagID")
                    intOffFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("OffDayFlagID")

                    If IsPayroll = True Then
                        If intInFlagID > 0 Or intOutFlagID > 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeDetailByCompany_New_Payroll(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeDetailByCompany_New_Payroll(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        ElseIf intInFlagID = -1 And intOutFlagID = -1 And intOffFlagID = 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeDetailByCompany_New_Payroll(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeDetailByCompany_New_Payroll(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        End If
                    Else
                        If intInFlagID > 0 Or intOutFlagID > 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeDetailByCompany_New(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeDetailByCompany_New(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        ElseIf intInFlagID = -1 And intOutFlagID = -1 And intOffFlagID = 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeDetailByCompany_New(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeDetailByCompany_New(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                    If dtTempData.Rows.Count > 0 Then dtData.Merge(dtTempData)
                Next
            End If

            dtTempData.Clear()
            dtTempData.Dispose()
            dtTempData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable

            If dtTempData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeOverTime_ar.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeOverTime.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtTempData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)
                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message & "-" & intEmployeeID)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetEmployeeOverTimeSummaryByFlaging() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeOverTimeSummaryByFlaging = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = "", dtData As New DataTable
        Dim dtEmployees As DataTable, dtTempData As New DataTable
        Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer, dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
        Dim intCount As Integer, intEmployeeID As Int64, intInFlagID As Integer, _
        intOutFlagID As Integer, intOffFlagID As Integer

        Try

            If clsCookie.EnablePayroll.ToString.ToUpper = "Y" Then
                IsPayroll = ValidateMonthClose(ToDateServer.Month, ToDateServer.Year, "Payroll", CompanyID)
            End If

            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            dtEmployees = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeListByCompanyOverTimeSetup_New(CompanyID, strEmployees)

            If dtEmployees.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                For intCount = 0 To dtEmployees.Rows.Count - 1

                    intEmployeeID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("EmployeeID")
                    intInFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("InComingFlagID")
                    intOutFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("OutGoingFlagID")
                    intOffFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("OffDayFlagID")

                    If IsPayroll = True Then
                        If intInFlagID > 0 Or intOutFlagID > 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeSummaryByCompany_New_Payroll(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeSummaryByCompany_New_Payroll(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        ElseIf intInFlagID = -1 And intOutFlagID = -1 And intOffFlagID = 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeSummaryByCompany_New_Payroll(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeSummaryByCompany_New_Payroll(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        End If
                    Else
                        If intInFlagID > 0 Or intOutFlagID > 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeSummaryByCompany_New(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeSummaryByCompany_New(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        ElseIf intInFlagID = -1 And intOutFlagID = -1 And intOffFlagID = 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeSummaryByCompany_New(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeOverTimeSummaryByCompany_New(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                    If dtTempData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                        dtData.Merge(dtTempData)
                    End If
                    dtTempData.Clear()
                    dtTempData.Dispose()
                Next
            End If

            dtTempData.Clear()
            dtTempData.Dispose()
            dtTempData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable

            If dtTempData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeOverTimeSummary_ar.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeOverTimeSummary.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtTempData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message & "-" & intEmployeeID)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetEmployeeOverTimeByFlagingCrossTab() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeOverTimeByFlagingCrossTab = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = "", dtData As New DataTable
        Dim dtEmployees As DataTable, dtTempData As New DataTable
        Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer, dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
        Dim intCount As Integer, intEmployeeID As Int64, intInFlagID As Integer, _
        intOutFlagID As Integer, intOffFlagID As Integer, SNo As Integer = 1

        Try


            Dim intTotDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)  '0 Based

            If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeOverTimeDateHourly Then
                If intTotDays > 31 Then
                    Dim strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_FromAndToDateDiffMsg")
                    Throw New Exception(IIf(strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg = "", "Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 31 days", strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg))
                End If
            Else
                If intTotDays > 7 Then
                    Dim strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_FromAndToDateDiffMsg")
                    Throw New Exception(IIf(strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg = "", "Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 7 days", strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg))
                End If
            End If
            

            If clsCookie.EnablePayroll.ToString.ToUpper = "Y" Then
                IsPayroll = ValidateMonthClose(ToDateServer.Month, ToDateServer.Year, "Payroll", CompanyID)
            End If

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "SubDepartment")

            If dtList.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                For Each dr As DataRow In dtList.Rows
                    dtEmployees = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeListByCompanyOverTimeSetup_New(CompanyID, dr("Employees"))
                    SNo = 1
                    If dtEmployees.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                        For intCount = 0 To dtEmployees.Rows.Count - 1

                            intEmployeeID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("EmployeeID")
                            intInFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("InComingFlagID")
                            intOutFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("OutGoingFlagID")
                            intOffFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("OffDayFlagID")

                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetOvertimeCrossTabReport(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID, SNo)
                            If dtData.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetOvertimeCrossTabReport(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID, SNo)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If

                            If dtTempData.Rows.Count > 0 Then dtData.Merge(dtTempData)
                            SNo = SNo + 1

                        Next
                    End If
                Next
            End If
            
            dtTempData.Clear()
            dtTempData.Dispose()
            dtTempData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable

            dtTempData.DefaultView.Sort = "SNo,EmployeeNo,Date ASC"
            dtData = dtTempData.DefaultView.ToTable

            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeOverTimeDateHourly Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeOverTimeCrossTab.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeOverTimeCrossTab_Weekly.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Period : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)
                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message & "-" & intEmployeeID)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Public Sub ExportToExcelfile(ByVal dt As System.Data.DataTable, ByVal FileName As String, ByVal delimiter As String)
        Dim sb As New System.Text.StringBuilder
        Response.Clear()
        Response.ContentType = "application/vnd.ms-excel" '"text/csv"
        'fileName = "abc"
        Response.AppendHeader("Content-Disposition", String.Format("attachment; filename={0}", FileName))

        Try
            Dim intColumn, intColumnValue As Integer
            Dim row As DataRow
            For intColumn = 0 To dt.Columns.Count - 1
                sb.Append(dt.Columns(intColumn).ColumnName)
                If intColumnValue <> dt.Columns.Count - 1 Then
                    sb.Append(delimiter)
                End If
            Next
            sb.Append(vbCrLf)
            For Each row In dt.Rows
                For intColumnValue = 0 To dt.Columns.Count - 1
                    sb.Append(StrConv(IIf(IsDBNull(row.Item(intColumnValue)), "", row.Item(intColumnValue)), VbStrConv.ProperCase))
                    If intColumnValue <> dt.Columns.Count - 1 Then
                        sb.Append(delimiter)
                    End If
                Next
                sb.Append(vbCrLf)
            Next

            Response.Write(sb.ToString)
            Response.End()

        Catch ex As Exception
            Throw
        Finally
            dt = Nothing
            sb = Nothing
        End Try
    End Sub
    Private Function GetMonthEndSummary() As Boolean

        GetMonthEndSummary = False

        Dim strReportPath As String = "", dtData As New DataTable
        Dim FileName As String = ""
        Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer, dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
        Dim FrMonth As Integer, FrYear As Integer, ToMonth As Integer, ToYear As Integer

        Try

            If IsSingleMonthPayroll = False Then
                dtFrom = DateAdd(DateInterval.Month, 1, dtFrom)
            End If

            FrMonth = dtFrom.Month
            FrYear = dtFrom.Year
            ToMonth = dtTo.Month
            ToYear = dtTo.Year

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            dtData = ReportHelper.GetMonthEndSummary(FrMonth, ToMonth, FrYear, ToYear, CompanyID, strEmployees)

            FileName = "MonthEndSummary"
            'PreviewReport(dtData, FileName, vbTab)
            ExportToExcelfile(dtData, FileName, vbTab)
            Return False

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try
    End Function

    Private Function GetDailyAttendanceCustomized() As Boolean

        GetDailyAttendanceCustomized = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""
        Dim boELeaveYear As New BO_ELeaveYear
        Dim boELeavePolicy As New BO_ELeavePolicy
        Dim boLeave As New BO_Leave
        Dim boLeaveDed As New BO_LeaveDeduction


        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = FromDateServer


            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim strFilter As String = ""
            If chklate.Checked = True And chkEarly.Checked = True Then
                strFilter = "LateTime<>'0:00' And EarlyTime<>'0:00'"
            ElseIf chklate.Checked = True And chkEarly.Checked = False Then
                strFilter = "LateTime<>'0:00'"
            ElseIf chklate.Checked = False And chkEarly.Checked = True Then
                strFilter = "EarlyTime<>'0:00'"
            End If

            If chkAbsent.Checked = False Then
                If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
                strFilter = strFilter & " AttendanceStatus<>'A'"
            End If
            If ddlShift.SelectedValue <> 0 Then
                If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
                strFilter = strFilter & " ShiftID=" & ddlShift.SelectedValue
            End If


            Dim IsManualEntry As Boolean = False
            If chkManual.Checked = True Then
                IsManualEntry = True
            End If

            Dim ds As DataSet
            If RptType.DailyAttendanceInOrOut = hdRptType.Value Then
                ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, FromDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, False, strFilter, False, True, IsManualEntry, True)
            Else
                ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, FromDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, False, strFilter, False, True, IsManualEntry, False, False, 0, 0, False, False, IsAttendanceFlag)
            End If

            If IsNothing(ds.Tables(1)) = False AndAlso ds.Tables(1).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                Dim dtLeaveSummary As New DataTable, SL As SortedList
                Dim intCount As Int32, LeaveID As Int32, LeavePrefix As String, EmployeeID As Int64
                Dim boLeaveType As New BO_Leave, dtLeaveType As New DataTable, LeaveHelp As New LeaveHelper
                dtLeaveType = boLeaveType.tblLeave.GetDatabyCompany_Active(CompanyID)

                dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("EmployeeID", GetType(System.Int64))
                dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("LeavePrefix")
                dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("Balance", GetType(Double))

                ds.Tables(1).Columns.Add("LeavePrefix1")
                ds.Tables(1).Columns.Add("LeavePrefix2")
                ds.Tables(1).Columns.Add("LeavePrefix3")
                ds.Tables(1).Columns.Add("Balance1", GetType(Double))
                ds.Tables(1).Columns.Add("Balance2", GetType(Double))
                ds.Tables(1).Columns.Add("Balance3", GetType(Double))

                For intCount = 0 To ds.Tables(1).Rows.Count - 1

                    EmployeeID = ds.Tables(1).Rows(intCount).Item("EmployeeID")

                    For i As Integer = 0 To dtLeaveType.Rows.Count - 1

                        LeaveID = dtLeaveType.Rows(i)("LeaveID")
                        LeavePrefix = dtLeaveType.Rows(i)("LeavePrefix")

                        SL = LeaveHelp.GetLeaveStatisticsByEmployeeToDate(EmployeeID, FromDateServer.Year, LeaveID, CompanyID, boELeaveYear, boELeavePolicy, boEmployee, boLeaveDed, FromDateServer.Date)

                        Dim dblEntitled As Double = SL("Entitled")
                        Dim dblTakenLeaveCount As Double = SL("Taken")
                        Dim dblCarriedForward As Double = SL("CarriedForward")
                        Dim dblLeaveExpired As Double = SL("Expired")
                        Dim dblVoid As Double = SL("Void")
                        Dim dtLeaveExpiry As Date = SL("CarriedForwardExpiryDate")
                        Dim dblDeduction As Double = SL("Deduction")
                        Dim dblEncash As Double = SL("LeaveEncash")
                        Dim dblNetBalance As Double
                        dblNetBalance = ((dblCarriedForward + dblEntitled) - (dblTakenLeaveCount + dblDeduction + dblLeaveExpired + dblVoid + dblEncash))
                        dtLeaveSummary.Rows.Add(EmployeeID, LeavePrefix, dblNetBalance)
                    Next

                    ds.Tables(1).Rows(intCount)("LeavePrefix1") = dtLeaveSummary.Rows(0)("LeavePrefix")
                    ds.Tables(1).Rows(intCount)("LeavePrefix2") = dtLeaveSummary.Rows(1)("LeavePrefix")
                    ds.Tables(1).Rows(intCount)("LeavePrefix3") = dtLeaveSummary.Rows(2)("LeavePrefix")
                    ds.Tables(1).Rows(intCount)("Balance1") = dtLeaveSummary.Rows(0)("Balance")
                    ds.Tables(1).Rows(intCount)("Balance2") = dtLeaveSummary.Rows(1)("Balance")
                    ds.Tables(1).Rows(intCount)("Balance3") = dtLeaveSummary.Rows(2)("Balance")
                    ds.Tables(1).Rows(intCount)("ScheduleInDate") = FromDateServer.Date
                    dtLeaveSummary.Clear()

                Next
            End If

            If IsNothing(ds.Tables(1)) = False AndAlso ds.Tables(1).Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptDailyAttendanceReportCustomized.rpt")

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(ds)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)

                If ddlShift.SelectedValue > 0 Then
                    hdRptTitle.Value = hdRptTitle.Value & "(" & ddlShift.SelectedItem.Text & ")"
                Else
                    hdRptTitle.Value = hdRptTitle.Value
                End If
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "As On Date : " & strFromDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                'Report Specific Params
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strBeforeGrace1", "Within")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strBeforeGrace2", "Grace")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("AfterGrace1", "After")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strAfterGrace2", "Grace")

                SetParameterForDailyAttendanceInOrOut()

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetAttendanceSummaryEmployee_Customized() As Boolean
        Try

            GetAttendanceSummaryEmployee_Customized = False

            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee, strLateFlag As String, strHalfFlag As String
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable, boTransaction As New BO_Transaction
            Dim intTotalDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)

            dtAttendance.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim ds As DataSet
            If IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then
                'If intTotalDays > 31 Then
                '    Throw New Exception("Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 31 days")
                'End If
                If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , , True)
                Else
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , , , , , , , , , , True)
                End If
            Else
                If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , )
                Else
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag)
                End If
            End If

            If IsNothing(ds.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso ds.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                Dim dtAttendanceFlagLate As New DataTable, dtAttendanceFlagHalf As New DataTable
                Dim dc As New DataColumn, dc1 As New DataColumn, dc2 As New DataColumn
                Dim boAttendanceFlag As New BO_AttendanceFlag, dtAttendanceFlagType As New DataTable

                dtAttendanceFlagType = boAttendanceFlag.tblAttendanceFlag.GetDatabyCompany(CompanyID)

                If dtAttendanceFlagType.Rows.Count >= 2 Then

                    strLateFlag = dtAttendanceFlagType.Rows(0)("FlagTitle")
                    strHalfFlag = dtAttendanceFlagType.Rows(1)("FlagTitle")

                    dtAttendanceFlagLate = boTransaction.tblTransaction.GetCountEmployeeFlags(strEmployees, FromDateServer.Date, ToDateServer.Date, dtAttendanceFlagType.Rows(1)("FlagID"), CompanyID)
                    ds.Tables.Add(dtAttendanceFlagLate)
                    ds.Relations.Add("LateFlag_Relation", ds.Tables(0).Columns("EmployeeID"), dtAttendanceFlagLate.Columns("EmployeeID"), False)

                    ds.Tables(0).Columns.Add("LateFlagIn", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(LateFlag_Relation).InFlagCount),0)")
                    ds.Tables(0).Columns.Add("LateFlagOut", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(LateFlag_Relation).OutFlagCount),0)")
                    ds.Tables(0).Columns.Add("LateFlagTotal", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(LateFlag_Relation).TotalFlagCount),0)")

                    dtAttendanceFlagHalf = boTransaction.tblTransaction.GetCountEmployeeFlags(strEmployees, FromDateServer.Date, ToDateServer.Date, dtAttendanceFlagType.Rows(0)("FlagID"), CompanyID)
                    ds.Tables.Add(dtAttendanceFlagHalf)
                    ds.Relations.Add("HalfFlag_Relation", ds.Tables(0).Columns("EmployeeID"), dtAttendanceFlagHalf.Columns("EmployeeID"), False)

                    ds.Tables(0).Columns.Add("HalfFlagIn", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(HalfFlag_Relation).InFlagCount),0)")
                    ds.Tables(0).Columns.Add("HalfFlagOut", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(HalfFlag_Relation).OutFlagCount),0)")
                    ds.Tables(0).Columns.Add("HalfFlagTotal", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(HalfFlag_Relation).TotalFlagCount),0)")

                    dtAttendanceFlagHalf.Dispose()
                    dtAttendanceFlagLate.Dispose()
                    dtAttendanceFlagType.Dispose()

                End If
            End If

            dtAttendance = ds.Tables(0)
            ds.Dispose()

            If dtAttendance.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                dtAttendance.DefaultView.Sort = "DepartmentRefNo, EmployeeRefNo Asc"
                dtAttendance.DefaultView.Sort = "DepartmentID,EmployeeID Asc"
                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceSummaryCustomized.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendance.DefaultView.ToTable)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", rptValues)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strFlagLate", strLateFlag)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strFlagHalf", strHalfFlag)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True
            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try



    End Function

            '            dtTemp = ReportHelper.GetEmployeePerformanceReport(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, ddlReportType.SelectedValue, dtCompany.Rows(i)("CompanyID"), ddlOperator.SelectedValue, txtValue.Text, ddlCalculate.SelectedValue, False)
            '            If IsNothing(dtData) = True Then dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeePerformanceReport(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, ddlReportType.SelectedValue, dtCompany.Rows(i)("CompanyID"), ddlOperator.SelectedValue, txtValue.Text, ddlCalculate.SelectedValue, False)

            If DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer) + 1 < txtValue.Text Then Throw New Exception("Invalid Criteria")

            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeePerformanceReport(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, ddlReportType.SelectedValue, CompanyID, ddlOperator.SelectedValue, txtValue.Text, ddlCalculate.SelectedValue, False)

            strDateCriteria = "Date Range From " & txtFromDate.Text & " To " & txtToDate.Text
            If ddlReportType.SelectedValue = 1 Then
                strSubReportTitle = "List of Employee(s) Who are Absent " & ddlOperator.SelectedValue & " " & txtValue.Text & " Days"
            ElseIf ddlReportType.SelectedValue = 2 Then
                strSubReportTitle = "List of Employee(s) Who are Late Incoming After Grace " & ddlOperator.SelectedValue & " " & txtValue.Text & " Days"
            ElseIf ddlReportType.SelectedValue = 3 Then
                strSubReportTitle = "List of Employee(s) Who are Late Incoming Without Grace " & ddlOperator.SelectedValue & " " & txtValue.Text & " Days"
            ElseIf ddlReportType.SelectedValue = 4 Then
                strSubReportTitle = "List of Employee(s) Who are Early Go " & ddlOperator.SelectedValue & " " & txtValue.Text & " Days"
            End If

            If ddlCalculate.SelectedValue = "C" Then
                strSubReportTitle = strSubReportTitle & " in Continuation."
            Else
                strSubReportTitle = strSubReportTitle & " in Randomly."
            End If

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptEmployeePerformance.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", strDateCriteria)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", strSubReportTitle)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientdateformat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True

            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    Private Function GetEmployeeWarning() As Boolean
        GetEmployeeWarning = False
        Try

            Dim dtData As DataTable = Nothing, strSubReportTitle As String = "", strDateCriteria As String = "", _
                dtCompany As New DataTable, boCompany As New BO_Company, dtTemp As New DataTable


            CreateEmployeeFilter(True)
            dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeWarning(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)
            strDateCriteria = "Date Range From " & txtFromDate.Text & " To " & txtToDate.Text

            If ddlCriteria.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "all" Then
                strSubReportTitle = "Criteria : All Employee(s)"
            Else
                strSubReportTitle = "Criteria : " & ddlCriteria.SelectedValue & ": From " & ddlFrom.SelectedItem.Value & " To " & ddlTo.SelectedItem.Value
            End If
            If ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower <> "all" Then
                strSubReportTitle = strSubReportTitle & vbCrLf & "With Sub Criteria of " & ddlCriteria.SelectedValue & ": From " & ddlFrom.SelectedItem.Value & " To " & ddlTo.SelectedItem.Value
            End If
            strSubReportTitle = strSubReportTitle & " Report."

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWarning.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", strDateCriteria)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", strSubReportTitle)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientdateformat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function

    Private Function GetEmployeeEOBI_DetailReport() As Boolean
        GetEmployeeEOBI_DetailReport = False
        Try

            Dim dtData As DataTable = Nothing, strSubReportTitle As String = "", strDateCriteria As String = "", _
                dtCompany As New DataTable, boCompany As New BO_Company, dtTemp As New DataTable

            dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeSESSI_EOBIReport(strEmployees, CompanyID)
            strDateCriteria = "Date Range From " & txtFromDate.Text & " To " & txtToDate.Text

            If ddlCriteria.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "all" Then
                strSubReportTitle = "All Employee(s)"
            Else
                strSubReportTitle = ddlCriteria.SelectedValue & ": From " & ddlFrom.SelectedItem.Value & " To " & ddlTo.SelectedItem.Value
            End If

            If ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower <> "all" Then

            End If


            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", strDateCriteria)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", strSubReportTitle)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientdateformat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True

    Private Function GetWorkerDetail() As Boolean
        GetWorkerDetail = False
        Try

            Dim dtData As DataTable = Nothing, strSubReportTitle As String = "", strDateCriteria As String = "", _
                dtEmployeeImage As New DataTable, dtImage As New DataTable, i As Integer = 0


            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            dtData = ReportHelper.GetWorkerDetail(strEmployees, CompanyID)

            '--------Employee Images-------------
            dtEmployeeImage = ReportHelper.GetWorkerImage(strEmployees, CompanyID, "./")

            dtImage.Columns.Add("EmployeeImage", System.Type.GetType("System.Byte[]"))
            Dim DCol1 = New DataColumn("EmployeeID", GetType(System.Int64))
            dtImage.Columns.Add(DCol1)

            For i = 0 To dtEmployeeImage.Rows.Count - 1
                dtImage.Merge(EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.CreateEmployeeImageTable(dtEmployeeImage.Rows(i)("EmployeeID"), Server.MapPath(dtEmployeeImage.Rows(i)("EmployeeImage")), "dtEmployeeImage", "EmployeeImage"))
            Next

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeDetailReport.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("dtEmployeeImage").SetDataSource(dtImage)
                'rptDoc.Subreports("rptEmployeeImage.rpt").SetDataSource(dtImage)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientdateformat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function

    Private Function GetMonthlyDataReport() As Boolean
        GetMonthlyDataReport = False
        Try

            Dim ds As New DataSet
            Dim dtGender As New DataTable, dtAge As New DataTable, dtSeniority As New DataTable, _
            dtQualificationLevel As New DataTable, dtData As New DataTable, dtWorkForce As New DataTable

            CreateEmployeeFilter(True)

            ds = ReportHelper.GetMonthlyDemographicsReportDataset(strEmployees, ddlMonth.SelectedValue, ddlyear.SelectedValue, CompanyID, "")
            dtWorkForce = ReportHelper.GetMonthlyWorkforceReport(strEmployees, ddlMonth.SelectedValue, ddlyear.SelectedValue, CompanyID)


            'Report Params
            Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
            Dim rptValues As String = " "

            If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
            ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
            ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
            End If

            dtWorkForce.DefaultView.RowFilter = "(ActiveCount > 0 Or InVoluntary > 0 Or Voluntary > 0 OR UnderOneYear > 0)"


            If IsNothing(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable) = False AndAlso ds.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptMonthlyDemographics.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())

                rptDoc.Database.Tables("DemographicsSeniority").SetDataSource(ds.Tables(2).DefaultView.ToTable)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("DemographicsQualificationLevel").SetDataSource(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("DemographicsGender").SetDataSource(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("DemographicsAge").SetDataSource(ds.Tables(3).DefaultView.ToTable)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("WorkForceTurnOver").SetDataSource(dtWorkForce.DefaultView.ToTable)

                rptDoc.Subreports("rptMonthlyDemographicsGender.rpt").SetDataSource(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                rptDoc.Subreports("rptMonthlyDemographicsAging.rpt").SetDataSource(ds.Tables(3).DefaultView.ToTable)
                rptDoc.Subreports("rptMonthlyDemographicsSeniority.rpt").SetDataSource(ds.Tables(2).DefaultView.ToTable)
                rptDoc.Subreports("rptMonthlyDemographicsQualificationLevel.rpt").SetDataSource(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                rptDoc.Subreports("rptMonthlyWorkForceTurnOver.rpt").SetDataSource(dtWorkForce.DefaultView.ToTable)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", "Monthly Demographics Report")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", "For The Month Of " & MonthName(ddlMonth.SelectedValue) & " - " & ddlyear.SelectedValue)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientdateformat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Criteria", rptValues)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetMonthlyHeadCountDetailReport() As Boolean
        GetMonthlyHeadCountDetailReport = False

        Try

            CreateEmployeeFilter(True)

            dtActualCurrent = ReportHelper.GetMonthlyHCDetailAC(strEmployees, ddlyear.SelectedValue, CompanyID)
