    Private Function GetTimeDurationWiseReport() As Boolean
        GetTimeDurationWiseReport = False
        Try

            If txtFromTime.Text.Trim = "" Or txtToTime.Text.Trim = "" Then Throw New Exception("From Or To Time not defined!")

            Dim dtData As New DataTable, strSubReportTitle As String = "", strReportType As Boolean = True
            Dim strFromTime As String, strToTime As String

            strFromTime = txtFromTime.Text.Trim & ":00"
            strToTime = txtToTime.Text.Trim & ":00"
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                If ddlReportFiltration.SelectedValue = 1 Then
                    dtData.Merge(ReportHelper.GetTimeDurationWiseData(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, strFromTime, strToTime, True))
                    strSubReportTitle = "In Coming Detail Between " & strFromTime & " And " & strToTime & "."
                    strReportType = True
                ElseIf ddlReportFiltration.SelectedValue = 2 Then
                    dtData.Merge(ReportHelper.GetTimeDurationWiseData(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, strFromTime, strToTime, False))
                    strSubReportTitle = "Out Going Detail Between " & strFromTime & " And " & strToTime & "."
                    strReportType = False
                End If
            Next

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                    SetReportPath("rptInOutDurationWiseReport_ar.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptInOutDurationWiseReport.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", "For The Period Of " & txtFromDate.Text & " To " & txtToDate.Text)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", strSubReportTitle)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientdateformat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("InOutFilter", strReportType)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function

    Private Function GetEmployeeAttendance_Customized() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeAttendance_Customized = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""


        Try
            Dim dtFromDate As Date = FromDateServer, dtToDate As Date = ToDateServer

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtFinalData As New DataTable, dtTempData As New DataTable
            Dim dsTempData As New DataSet

            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblMain")
            dsTempData.Tables.Add("tblDetail")

            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeAttendanceStatusByDateCompany(dtFromDate, dtToDate, CompanyID, strEmployees)
            dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").Merge(dtTempData.DefaultView.ToTable)
            dtTempData.Dispose()

            dtFinalData = dsTempData.Tables("tblMain").DefaultView.ToTable

            If dtFinalData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeAttendanceReport.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtFinalData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", strRptName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportDate", Format(FromDateServer, strClientDateFormat))


                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function


    Private Function GetAttendanceAnalysis_MTD_Customized() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceAnalysis_MTD_Customized = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = "", strLeaveType As String = ""


        Try

            Dim datFromDate As Date
            Dim datToDate As Date

            Dim intTotDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)  '0 Based

            If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.AttendanceAnalysis_YTD Then
                If intTotDays > 365 Then
                    Dim strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_FromAndToDateDiffMsg")
                    Throw New Exception(IIf(strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg = "", "Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 365 days", strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg))
                Else
                    datFromDate = FromDateServer
                    datToDate = ToDateServer
                End If
            Else
                If intTotDays > 31 Then
                    Dim strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_FromAndToDateDiffMsg")
                    Throw New Exception(IIf(strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg = "", "Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 31 days", strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg))
                Else
                    datFromDate = FromDateServer
                    datToDate = ToDateServer
                End If
            End If

            If datToDate > Today.Date Then datToDate = Today.Date
            If datFromDate > datToDate Then Throw New Exception("From date should be less or equal to today's date.")


            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtData As New DataTable
            dtData = ReportHelper.GetAnalysisReport(CompanyID, strEmployees, datFromDate, datToDate, strLeaveType)

            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                Dim strLeaves() As String = strLeaveType.Split(",")

                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceAnalysis.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", "Employee Attendance Analysis")

                Dim strReportPeriod As String = ""

                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.AttendanceAnalysis_YTD Then

                    If datFromDate.Year <> datToDate.Year Then
                        strReportPeriod = "For The Year Of " & datFromDate.Year & "/" & datToDate.Year & " (" & datFromDate.Day & " " & MonthName(datFromDate.Month, True) & " " & datFromDate.Year & " to " & datToDate.Day & " " & MonthName(datToDate.Month, True) & " " & datToDate.Year & ")"
                    ElseIf datFromDate.Year = datToDate.Year Then
                        strReportPeriod = "For The Year Of " & datFromDate.Year & " (" & datFromDate.Day & " " & MonthName(datFromDate.Month, True) & " to " & datToDate.Day & " " & MonthName(datToDate.Month, True) & ")"
                    End If

                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportDate", strReportPeriod)
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strRportCaption", "YEAR TO DATE")
                Else

                    If datFromDate.Month <> datToDate.Month AndAlso datFromDate.Year <> datToDate.Year Then
                        strReportPeriod = "For The Month Of " & MonthName(datFromDate.Month) & "/" & MonthName(datToDate.Month) & " (" & datFromDate.Day & " " & MonthName(datFromDate.Month, True) & " " & datFromDate.Year & " to " & datToDate.Day & " " & MonthName(datToDate.Month, True) & " " & datToDate.Year & ")"
                    ElseIf datFromDate.Month <> datToDate.Month AndAlso datFromDate.Year = datToDate.Year Then
                        strReportPeriod = "For The Month Of " & MonthName(datFromDate.Month) & "/" & MonthName(datToDate.Month) & " " & datFromDate.Year & " (" & datFromDate.Day & " " & MonthName(datFromDate.Month, True) & " to " & datToDate.Day & " " & MonthName(datToDate.Month, True) & ")"
                    ElseIf datFromDate.Month = datToDate.Month Then
                        strReportPeriod = "For The Month Of " & MonthName(datFromDate.Month) & " " & datFromDate.Year & " (" & datFromDate.Day & " " & MonthName(datFromDate.Month, True) & " to " & datToDate.Day & " " & MonthName(datToDate.Month, True) & ")"
                    End If

                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportDate", strReportPeriod)
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strRportCaption", "MONTH TO DATE")
                End If
                


                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetAttendancePlentySummary() As Boolean

        GetAttendancePlentySummary = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = "", strLeaveType As String = ""


        Try

            Dim SLDate As SortedList = GetFromAndToDate(ddlyear.SelectedValue, ddlMonth.SelectedValue)
            FromDateServer = SLDate("FromDate").ToString
            ToDateServer = SLDate("ToDate").ToString

            Dim AttendanceLateRulePolicyDate As Date = boCompanyFlag.tblCompanyFlag.GetFlagValue_String("AttendanceLateRulePolicyDate", "01/01/1900", CompanyID)
            If FromDateServer < AttendanceLateRulePolicyDate Then FromDateServer = AttendanceLateRulePolicyDate

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim ds As DataSet
            If IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then
                If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , , True)
                Else
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , , , , , , , , , , True)
                End If
            Else
                If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , )
                Else
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, )
                End If
            End If

            Dim dtData As DataTable
            dtData = ds.Tables(0)
            dtData.DefaultView.RowFilter = "TotalScheduleDays <> 0"
            dtData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable

            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptAttendancePlentySummary.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "w.e.f " & MonthName(FromDateServer.Month) & " " & FromDateServer.Day & ", " & FromDateServer.Year & " to " & MonthName(ToDateServer.Month) & " " & ToDateServer.Day & ", " & ToDateServer.Year)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetAttendanceRegisterMusterRoll() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceRegisterMusterRoll = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New dsAttendance.ShiftScheduleRegDataTable
            Dim strServerDateFormat As String = ConfigurationManager.AppSettings("ServerDateFormat")

            Dim intTotDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)  '0 Based

            If intTotDays > 31 Then
                Dim strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_FromAndToDateDiffMsg")
                Throw New Exception(IIf(strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg = "", "Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 31 days", strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg))
            End If

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")


            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                dt1 = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceRegisterMusterRoll(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)

                If dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = dt1
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(dt1)
                End If
            Next

            Dim strShiftTitle As String = "", strStartingTime As String = "", strEndingTime As String = ""

            If dt2.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If ddlShift.SelectedValue > 0 Then
                    dt2.DefaultView.RowFilter = "ShiftID = " & ddlShift.SelectedValue & ""
                    dt2 = dt2.DefaultView.ToTable
                    If dt2.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                        strShiftTitle = dt2.Compute("Max(ShiftTitle)", "ShiftID = " & ddlShift.SelectedValue & "")
                        strStartingTime = dt2.Compute("Max(StartingTime)", "ShiftID = " & ddlShift.SelectedValue & "")
                        strEndingTime = dt2.Compute("Max(EndingTime)", "ShiftID = " & ddlShift.SelectedValue & "")
                    End If
                Else
                    strShiftTitle = "All"
                End If
            End If

            If dt2.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                If RptType.DailyAttendanceRegister = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptDailyAttendanceCustomized_PBC.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptAttendanceRegisterMusterRoll.rpt")
                End If

                'Report Params
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dt2)
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptValue", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptFromDate", txtFromDate.Text)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptToDate", txtToDate.Text)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptDates", MonthName(ToDateServer.Month) & " " & ToDateServer.Year)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportDays", DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer) + 1)

                If RptType.DailyAttendanceRegister = hdRptType.Value Then
                    rptDoc.SetParameterValue("rptTitle", "D A I L Y        A T T E N D A N C E       F R O M      " & txtFromDate.Text & "      T O    " & txtToDate.Text)
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ShiftTitle", strShiftTitle)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("StartingTime", strStartingTime)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("EndingTime", strEndingTime)

                'Report Params
                Dim datTemp As Date = FromDateServer
                For i As Integer = 1 To 31

                    Try

                        Dim dayName1 As String = datTemp.DayOfWeek.ToString

                        If RptType.DailyAttendanceRegister = hdRptType.Value Then
                            If dayName1.ToLower = "sunday" Then   'Indicate Sundays
                                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, datTemp)
                            Else
                                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, datTemp)
                            End If
                        Else
                            If dayName1.ToLower = "sunday" Then   'Indicate Sundays
                                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, "*" & datTemp.Day.ToString)
                            Else
                                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, datTemp.Day.ToString)
                            End If
                        End If

                    Catch ex As Exception  'Month does not have this Date
                        rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, " ")
                    End Try

                    datTemp = datTemp.AddDays(1)

                Next

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetOvertimeYearlySummary() As Boolean

        GetOvertimeYearlySummary = False

        Try

            Dim dtData As New DataTable, strGroupField As String = ""
            CreateEmployeeFilter()


            If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.OvertimeYearlySummaryBranch Then
                strGroupField = "Branch"
            ElseIf CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.OvertimeYearlySummaryCategory Then
                strGroupField = "EmployeeCategory"
            ElseIf CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.OvertimeYearlySummaryDepartment Then
                strGroupField = "Department"
            ElseIf CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.OvertimeYearlySummaryReason Then
                strGroupField = "OvertimeReason"
            Else
                Throw New Exception("No Records found")
            End If

            dtData = ReportHelper.GetOverTimeYearlySummary(strEmployees, ddlyear.SelectedValue, CompanyID, strGroupField)

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptOverTimeYearlySummary.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim strReportTitle() As String
                strReportTitle = hdRptTitle.Value.ToString.Split("(")

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", strReportTitle(0))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", "For The Period Of " & ddlyear.SelectedValue)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientdateformat", strClientDateFormat)

                If strGroupField.Trim = "EmployeeCategory" Then
                    strGroupField = "Category"
                ElseIf strGroupField.Trim = "OvertimeReason" Then
                    strGroupField = "Reason"
                End If
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", strGroupField & " Wise")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ID", strGroupField & " ID")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Title", strGroupField & " Title")

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetOvertimeApproval() As Boolean

        GetOvertimeApproval = False

        Try

            Dim intTotDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)  '0 Based

            If intTotDays > 31 Then
                Dim strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_FromAndToDateDiffMsg")
                Throw New Exception(IIf(strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg = "", "Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 31 days", strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg))
            End If

            If FromDateServer > ToDateServer Then Throw New Exception("Fromdate should be less or equal to todate.")

            Dim dtData As New DataTable, strGroupField As String = ""
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            dtData = ReportHelper.GetOvertimeApprovalData(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, 0, CompanyID)

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptOverTimeApproval.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim strReportPeriod As String = ""

                If FromDateServer.Month <> ToDateServer.Month AndAlso FromDateServer.Year <> ToDateServer.Year Then
                    strReportPeriod = "For The Month Of " & MonthName(FromDateServer.Month) & "/" & MonthName(ToDateServer.Month) & " (" & FromDateServer.Day & " " & MonthName(FromDateServer.Month, True) & " " & FromDateServer.Year & " to " & ToDateServer.Day & " " & MonthName(ToDateServer.Month, True) & " " & ToDateServer.Year & ")"
                ElseIf FromDateServer.Month <> ToDateServer.Month AndAlso FromDateServer.Year = ToDateServer.Year Then
                    strReportPeriod = "For The Month Of " & MonthName(FromDateServer.Month) & "/" & MonthName(ToDateServer.Month) & " " & FromDateServer.Year & " (" & FromDateServer.Day & " " & MonthName(FromDateServer.Month, True) & " to " & ToDateServer.Day & " " & MonthName(ToDateServer.Month, True) & ")"
                ElseIf FromDateServer.Month = ToDateServer.Month Then
                    strReportPeriod = "For The Month Of " & MonthName(FromDateServer.Month) & " " & FromDateServer.Year & " (" & FromDateServer.Day & " " & MonthName(FromDateServer.Month, True) & " to " & ToDateServer.Day & " " & MonthName(ToDateServer.Month, True) & ")"
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", "APPROVAL FOR ALLOWING TO STAY FOR OVER TIME / INCONVENIENCE / OFF-DAY")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", "This form should be submitted in (Time office) atleast 4 hours prior to starting of overtime")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", strReportPeriod)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientdateformat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function

    Private Function GetSalarySlabProcess() As Boolean

        GetSalarySlabProcess = False

        Dim strReportPath As String = ""

        Try
            Dim BOFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim BOTransaction As New BO_Transaction
            Dim dtSalarySlabProcessData As New DataTable
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtSalarySlabProcessData)
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
            Dim dtData As DataTable
            Dim itemp As Integer = 0
            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeSalarySlabProcessByDateCompany(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, strEmployees)

            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1
                    Dim empid As String = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeID")
                    Dim sDate As String = dtData.Rows(i)("ScheduleDate")
                    Dim dt As DataTable = BOFinalSchedule.tblFinalSchedule.GetDataByEmployeeScheduleDate(empid, Date.Parse(sDate))
                    Dim dt2 As DataTable = BOTransaction.tblTransaction.GetDataByEmployeeScheduleDate(empid, Date.Parse(sDate))

                    For j As Integer = 0 To dt.Columns.Count - 1
                        If itemp = 0 Then
                            Dim dc As DataColumn = New DataColumn(dt.Columns(j).ColumnName, dt.Columns(j).DataType)
                            dtData.Columns.Add(dc)
                        End If
                        dtData.Rows(i)(dt.Columns(j).ColumnName) = dt.Rows(0)(dt.Columns(j).ColumnName)
                    Next
                    For k As Integer = 0 To dt2.Columns.Count - 1
                        If itemp = 0 Then
                            Dim dc As DataColumn = New DataColumn(dt2.Columns(k).ColumnName, dt2.Columns(k).DataType)
                            dtData.Columns.Add(dc)
                        End If
                        dtData.Rows(i)(dt2.Columns(k).ColumnName) = dt2.Rows(0)(dt2.Columns(k).ColumnName)
                    Next
                    itemp = 1
                Next

            End If
            Dim dsNew As New DataSet

            dsNew.Tables.Add(dtData)
            Dim rptValues As String = " "

            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False And dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hdRptType.Value = RptType.SalarySlabProcessData Then
                    SetReportPath("crSalarySlabProcessDetail.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("crSalarySlabProcessSummary.rpt")
                End If
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(CompanyID)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportCaption", hdRptTitle.Value)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception

        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetEmployeePortfolio() As Boolean
        GetEmployeePortfolio = False
        Try

            Dim dtData As DataTable = Nothing, strSubReportTitle As String = "", strDateCriteria As String = "", _
                dtEmployeeImage As New DataTable, dtImage As New DataTable, i As Integer = 0, _
                dtLeave As New DataTable, IsELeave As Boolean = False, IsPayroll As Boolean = False, _
                dtExperience As New DataTable, dtEmployeeQualification As New DataTable, _
                IsHR As Boolean = False, IsAdditionalHR As Boolean = False, dtEmployeeDependant As New DataTable, _
                dtEmployeeAssets As New DataTable, dtEmployeeMedical As New DataTable, dtEmployeeTeam As New DataTable

            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeePortfolio(strEmployees, intCompanyID, dtEmployeeQualification, dtExperience, dtEmployeeDependant, dtEmployeeTeam, dtEmployeeAssets, dtEmployeeMedical)

            If clsCookie.EnableELeave.ToString.ToLower = "y" Then IsELeave = True
            If clsCookie.EnablePayroll.ToString.ToLower = "y" Then IsPayroll = True
            If clsCookie.EnableHR.ToString.ToLower = "y" Then IsHR = True
            If IsHR = True And CBool(boCompanyFlag.tblCompanyFlag.GetFlagValue_String("IsAdditionalDetailRequired", "False", intCompanyID)) = True Then IsAdditionalHR = True

            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 And IsELeave = True Then
                '--------Leave Statics---------------
                For i = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1
                    If i = 0 Then
                        dtLeave = GetLeaveSummary(dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeID"), dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo"), intCompanyID)
                    Else
                        dtLeave.Merge(GetLeaveSummary(dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeID"), dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo"), intCompanyID))
                    End If
                Next
            End If

            '--------Employee Images-------------
            dtEmployeeImage = ReportHelper.GetWorkerImage(strEmployees, CompanyID, "./")

            dtImage.Columns.Add("EmployeeImage", System.Type.GetType("System.Byte[]"))
            Dim DCol1 = New DataColumn("EmployeeID", GetType(System.Int64))
            dtImage.Columns.Add(DCol1)

            For i = 0 To dtEmployeeImage.Rows.Count - 1
                dtImage.Merge(EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.CreateEmployeeImageTable(dtEmployeeImage.Rows(i)("EmployeeID"), Server.MapPath(dtEmployeeImage.Rows(i)("EmployeeImage")), "dtEmployeeImage", "EmployeeImage"))
            Next

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptEmployeePortfolioReport.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("dtEmployeeImage").SetDataSource(dtImage)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("LeaveSummary").SetDataSource(dtLeave)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("EmployeeExperience").SetDataSource(dtExperience)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("EmployeeQualification").SetDataSource(dtEmployeeQualification)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("EmployeeDependant").SetDataSource(dtEmployeeDependant)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("EmployeeTeam").SetDataSource(dtEmployeeTeam)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("EmployeeMedical").SetDataSource(dtEmployeeMedical)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("EmployeeAssets").SetDataSource(dtEmployeeAssets)


                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Company Name", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Caption", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Date", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Report Title", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientdateformat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("IsELeave", IsELeave)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("IsPayroll", IsPayroll)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("IsHR", IsHR)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("IsAdditionalHR", IsAdditionalHR)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetLeaveSummary(ByVal pEmployeeID As Int64, ByVal pEmployeeRefNo As String, ByVal pCompanyID As Int32) As DataTable


        Dim dtEmployee As DataTable = Nothing
        Dim LeaveHelp As New LeaveHelper
        Dim objWseLeave As New WSeLeave
        Dim dtLeaveType As New DataTable
        Dim intLeaveType As Int32, FromDateServer As Date, ToDateServer As Date
        Dim boELeavePolicy As New BO_ELeavePolicy, dtEmployeeLeave As New DataTable
        Dim boLeaveYear As New BO_ELeaveYear, dtLeaveYear As New DataTable, _
            datLeaveYearStart As Date, datLeaveYearEnd As Date, pLeaveYear As Integer
        Dim dtLeaveSummary As New DataTable

        Try

            Dim LeaveYear As System.Int64 = EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.GetOneValue("tbleLeaveYear", "LeaveYear", "WHERE CompanyID = " & pCompanyID & " And IsCurrentLeaveYear = 1 ")
            If IsNothing(LeaveYear) = True Then LeaveYear = Today.Year

            dtLeaveYear = boLeaveYear.tblELeaveYear.GetDatabyYear(LeaveYear, pCompanyID)
            datLeaveYearStart = dtLeaveYear.Rows(0)("LeaveYearStart")
            datLeaveYearEnd = dtLeaveYear.Rows(0)("LeaveYearEnd")

            '------Leave Summary----------------
            Dim intCount As Int32, LeaveID As Int32, LeaveTitle As String, LeaveRefNo As String
            dtLeaveType = objWseLeave.GetAuthenticLeaveTypes(pCompanyID, LeaveYear, pEmployeeID)

            dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("EmployeeID", GetType(System.Int64))
            dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("EmployeeRefNo")
            dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("LeaveRefNo")
            dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("LeaveTitle")
            dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("CarriedForward", GetType(Double))
            dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("YearEntitled", GetType(Double))
            dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("YearAvailed", GetType(Double))
            dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("Expired", GetType(Double))
            dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("Void", GetType(Double))
            dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("Deduction", GetType(Double))
            dtLeaveSummary.Columns.Add("YearBalance", GetType(Double))

            For intCount = 0 To dtLeaveType.Rows.Count - 1
                LeaveID = dtLeaveType.Rows(intCount).Item("LeaveID")
                LeaveTitle = dtLeaveType.Rows(intCount).Item("LeaveTitle")
                LeaveRefNo = dtLeaveType.Rows(intCount).Item("LeaveRefNo")
                LeaveYear = LeaveYear
                dtEmployeeLeave = boELeavePolicy.tblELeavePolicy.GetELeavePolicy_Employeewise(intLeaveType, pCompanyID, LeaveYear, pEmployeeID, datLeaveYearEnd.Date)

                If dtEmployeeLeave.Rows.Count > 0 AndAlso dtEmployeeLeave.Rows(0).Item("IsPeriodic") = True Then
                    FromDateServer = dtEmployeeLeave.Rows(0).Item("FromDate")
                    ToDateServer = dtEmployeeLeave.Rows(0).Item("ToDate")
                    pLeaveYear = dtEmployeeLeave.Rows(0).Item("AppliedForYear")
                Else
                    FromDateServer = datLeaveYearStart
                    ToDateServer = datLeaveYearEnd
                    pLeaveYear = LeaveYear
                End If

                If Today.Date < ToDateServer.Date Then
                    datLeaveYearEnd = Today.Date
                Else
                    datLeaveYearEnd = ToDateServer.Date
                End If

                Dim boELeaveYear As New BO_ELeaveYear, boLeaveDed As New BO_LeaveDeduction

                Dim SL As SortedList
                SL = LeaveHelp.GetLeaveStatisticsByEmployeeToDate(pEmployeeID, pLeaveYear, LeaveID, pCompanyID, boELeaveYear, boELeavePolicy, boEmployee, boLeaveDed, datLeaveYearEnd.Date)

                Dim dblEntitled As Double = SL("Entitled")
                Dim dblTakenLeaveCount As Double = SL("Taken")
                Dim dblCarriedForward As Double = SL("CarriedForward")
                Dim dblLeaveExpired As Double = SL("Expired")
                Dim dblVoid As Double = SL("Void")
                Dim dtLeaveExpiry As Date = SL("CarriedForwardExpiryDate")
                Dim dblDeduction As Double = SL("Deduction")
                Dim dblEncash As Double = SL("LeaveEncash")
                Dim dblNetBalance As Double
                Dim LeaveApplicationLimit As Double = SL("LeaveApplicationLimit")
                Dim AvailLeaveApplicationLimit As Double = SL("AvailLeaveApplicationLimit")

                dblNetBalance = ((dblCarriedForward + dblEntitled) - (dblTakenLeaveCount + dblDeduction + dblLeaveExpired + dblVoid + dblEncash))

                If (dblCarriedForward + dblEntitled) > 0 Then
                    'If dblNetBalance > 0 Then
                    dtLeaveSummary.Rows.Add(pEmployeeID, pEmployeeRefNo, LeaveRefNo, LeaveTitle, dblCarriedForward, dblEntitled, dblTakenLeaveCount, dblLeaveExpired, dblVoid, dblDeduction, dblNetBalance)
                    'End If
                Else
                    dtLeaveSummary.Rows.Add(pEmployeeID, pEmployeeRefNo, LeaveRefNo, LeaveTitle, dblCarriedForward, dblEntitled, dblTakenLeaveCount, dblLeaveExpired, dblVoid, dblDeduction, dblNetBalance)
                End If
            Next


        Catch ex As Exception
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
        End Try

        Return dtLeaveSummary

    End Function

    Private Function GetAttendanceSummaryData() As Boolean
        Try

            GetAttendanceSummaryData = False

            Dim intTotDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)  '0 Based

            If intTotDays > 31 Then
                Dim strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_FromAndToDateDiffMsg")
                Throw New Exception(IIf(strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg = "", "Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 31 days", strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg))
            End If

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dtData As New DataTable, dtLeave As New DataTable
            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                If dtData.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dtData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSummaryData(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, dtLeave)
                Else
                    dtData.Merge(ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSummaryData(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, dtLeave))
                End If
            Next

            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                Dim RowCount As Integer = dtLeave.Rows.Count + 1
                If RowCount < 11 Then
                    For a As Integer = RowCount To 10
                        dtData.Columns.Add("Leave" & a, GetType(System.Double))
                        dtData.Columns("Leave" & a).DefaultValue = 0
                    Next
                End If

                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceSummaryData.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", "Attendance Sheet For The Month of " & MonthName(ToDateServer.Month) & ", " & ToDateServer.Year)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Office of the Registrar")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "w.e.f " & MonthName(FromDateServer.Month) & " " & FromDateServer.Day & ", " & FromDateServer.Year & " to " & MonthName(ToDateServer.Month) & " " & ToDateServer.Day & ", " & ToDateServer.Year)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("L1", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("L2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("L3", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("L4", "")

                For i As Integer = 0 To dtLeave.Rows.Count - 1
                    If i < 4 Then
                        rptDoc.SetParameterValue("L" & (i + 1), dtLeave.Rows(i)("LeavePrefix"))
                    End If
                Next

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True
            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try



    End Function
    Private Function GetEmployeeExcessiveShortHoursAndOverTimeCalculation() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeExcessiveShortHoursAndOverTimeCalculation = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")
            Dim ds As DataSet

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                If IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then
                    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , , True)
                    Else
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , , , , , , , , , , True)
                    End If
                Else
                    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , )
                    Else
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, )
                    End If
                End If

                If dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt1 = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt1.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

                If dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

            Next

            If dt1.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                dt1.DefaultView.RowFilter = "TotalScheduleDays > 0"
                dt1 = dt1.DefaultView.ToTable
            End If

            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt1.DefaultView.ToTable)

            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeOverTimeCalculation Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeOverTimeCalculation.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeExcessiveShortHours.rpt")
                End If


                Dim str As String = ""

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strFromDate", strFromDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strToDate", strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function

                    End If

                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , )

            If dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeLateInSpecifiedPeriod.rpt")

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", FromDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat) & _
                                " To " & ToDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("FromDate", txtFromDate.Text)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ToDate", txtToDate.Text)

                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    Private Function GetEmployeeExcessiveShortHoursAndOverTimeCalculation_Actual() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeExcessiveShortHoursAndOverTimeCalculation_Actual = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")
            Dim ds As DataSet

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                If IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then
                    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , , True)
                    Else
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , , , , , , , , , , True)
                    End If
                Else
                    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , True, , , , , , , , )
                    Else
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, )
                    End If
                End If

                If dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt1 = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt1.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

                If dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

            Next

            Dim dblTotalOutFlagTime As Double = 0
            Dim dblOffDayOT As Double = 0
            dt1.Columns.Add("OutFlagTimeDifference", GetType(System.String))

            If dt1.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                dt1.DefaultView.RowFilter = "TotalScheduleDays > 0"
                dt1 = dt1.DefaultView.ToTable
            End If

            For Each dr As DataRow In dt1.Rows

                If IsDBNull(dt2.Compute("Sum(spentHours)", "spentHours > 0 And ShiftID = 0 And EmployeeID = " & dr("EmployeeID"))) = False Then
                    dblOffDayOT = dt2.Compute("Sum(spentHours)", "spentHours > 0 And ShiftID = 0 And EmployeeID = " & dr("EmployeeID"))
                Else
                    dblOffDayOT = 0
                End If

                If IsDBNull(dt2.Compute("Sum(OutFlagTimeDifference)", "TotalScheduleDays > 0 And EmployeeID = " & dr("EmployeeID"))) = False Then
                    dblTotalOutFlagTime = dt2.Compute("Sum(OutFlagTimeDifference)", "TotalScheduleDays > 0 And EmployeeID = " & dr("EmployeeID"))
                Else
                    dblTotalOutFlagTime = 0
                End If

                Dim strTotalOutFlagTime As String = MinutesToTime(CType(dblTotalOutFlagTime, Long))
                dblOffDayOT = dblOffDayOT * 60
                Dim strTotalOFFDayTime As String = MinutesToTime(CType(dblOffDayOT, Long))

                Dim strTimeArray() As String = strTotalOutFlagTime.Split(":")
                Dim rHours As Integer = CInt(strTimeArray(0))
                Dim rMinutes As Integer = CInt(strTimeArray(1))
                Dim rSeconds As Integer = CInt(strTimeArray(2))
                Dim intReturnMinutes As Integer = ReportHelper.GetOutTimeFlageMinutes(CInt(rHours), CInt(rMinutes), CompanyID)

                If intReturnMinutes = 60 Then
                    rHours += 1
                    rMinutes = 0
                    rSeconds = 0
                Else
                    rMinutes = intReturnMinutes
                End If

                Dim strTimeArray1() As String = strTotalOFFDayTime.Split(":")
                rHours = rHours + CInt(strTimeArray1(0))
                rMinutes = rMinutes + CInt(strTimeArray1(1))
                rSeconds = rSeconds + CInt(strTimeArray1(2))

                If rSeconds = 60 Then
                    rMinutes = rMinutes + 1
                    rSeconds = 0
                End If

                If rMinutes = 60 Then
                    rHours = rHours + 1
                    rMinutes = 0
                End If

                Dim strFinalTotalOutFlagTime As String = TimeFormat(rHours) & ":" & TimeFormat(rMinutes) & ":" & TimeFormat(rSeconds)

                dr("OutFlagTimeDifference") = strFinalTotalOutFlagTime

            Next

            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt1.DefaultView.ToTable)

            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeOverTimeCalculation_Actual.rpt")

                Dim str As String = ""

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strFromDate", strFromDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strToDate", strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_Customized() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_Customized = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
            Dim intTotalDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")
            Dim ds As DataSet

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                If IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then
                    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                                CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                                , , , , True, , , , , , , , , True, True, True)
                    Else
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                                CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                                , , , , , , , , , , , , , True, True, True)
                    End If
                Else
                    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                                CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                                , , , , True, , , , , , , , , , True, True)
                    Else
                        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                                CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                                , , , , , , , , , , , , , , True, True)
                    End If
                End If

                If dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt1 = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt1.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

                If dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

            Next


            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeAttendanceOverTimeSummary Then
                dsNew.Tables.Add(dt1.DefaultView.ToTable)
            Else
                dsNew.Tables.Add(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)
            End If

            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeAttendanceOverTimeSummary Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceSummary_Customized.rpt")
                    rptDoc.SetDataSource(dt1)
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_Customized.rpt")
                    rptDoc.SetDataSource(dt2)
                End If

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strVersion", "SAMCO")

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
