            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeAttendanceOverTimeSummary_SRMCO Then
                dsNew.Tables.Add(dt1.DefaultView.ToTable)
            Else
                dsNew.Tables.Add(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)
            End If


            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.EmployeeAttendanceOverTimeSummary_SRMCO Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceSummary_Customized.rpt")
                    rptDoc.SetDataSource(dt1)
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_Customized.rpt")
                    rptDoc.SetDataSource(dt2)
                End If

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strVersion", "SRMCO")

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetEmployeeAttendanceOverTime() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeAttendanceOverTime = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = "", dtData As New DataTable
        Dim dtEmployees As DataTable, dtTempData As New DataTable
        Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer, dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
        Dim intCount As Integer, intEmployeeID As Int64, intInFlagID As Integer, _
        intOutFlagID As Integer, intOffFlagID As Integer

        Try


            If clsCookie.EnablePayroll.ToString.ToUpper = "Y" Then
                IsPayroll = ValidateMonthClose(ToDateServer.Month, ToDateServer.Year, "Payroll", CompanyID)
            End If

            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            dtEmployees = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeListByCompanyOverTimeSetup_New(CompanyID, strEmployees)

            If dtEmployees.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                For intCount = 0 To dtEmployees.Rows.Count - 1

                    intEmployeeID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("EmployeeID")
                    intInFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("InComingFlagID")
                    intOutFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("OutGoingFlagID")
                    intOffFlagID = dtEmployees.Rows(intCount)("OffDayFlagID")

                    If IsPayroll = True Then
                        If intInFlagID > 0 Or intOutFlagID > 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeAttendanceAndOverTimeDetail(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeAttendanceAndOverTimeDetail(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        ElseIf intInFlagID = -1 And intOutFlagID = -1 And intOffFlagID = 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeAttendanceAndOverTimeDetail(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeAttendanceAndOverTimeDetail(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        End If
                    Else
                        If intInFlagID > 0 Or intOutFlagID > 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeAttendanceAndOverTimeDetail(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeAttendanceAndOverTimeDetail(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        ElseIf intInFlagID = -1 And intOutFlagID = -1 And intOffFlagID = 0 Then
                            dtTempData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeAttendanceAndOverTimeDetail(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                            If intCount = 0 Then
                                dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeAttendanceAndOverTimeDetail(dtFrom, dtTo, CompanyID, intEmployeeID, intInFlagID, intOutFlagID, intOffFlagID)
                                dtData.Clear()
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                    If dtTempData.Rows.Count > 0 Then dtData.Merge(dtTempData)
                Next
            End If

            dtTempData.Clear()
            dtTempData.Dispose()
            dtTempData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable

            If dtTempData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If hidLanguageDirection.Value.ToString.ToLower = "rtl" Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeOverTime_ar.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeAttendanceOverTime.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtTempData)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message & "-" & intEmployeeID)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_TimelyShift_Customized_1() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_TimelyShift_Customized_1 = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
            Dim intTotalDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")
            Dim ds As DataSet

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                'If IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then
                '    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                '        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                '                CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                '                , , , , True, , , , , , , , , True, False, True)
                '    Else
                '        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                '                CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                '                , , , , , , , , , , , , , True, False, True)
                '    End If
                'Else
                '    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                '        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                '                CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                '                , , , , True, , , , , , , , , , False, True)
                '    Else
                '        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                '                CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                '                , , , , , , , , , , , , , , False, True)
                '    End If
                'End If

                ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                    CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                    , , , , True, , , , , , , , , , False, True)

                If dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

            Next

            dt2.Columns.Remove("TotalLateTime")

            Dim DCol1 As DataColumn, DCol2 As DataColumn, DCol3 As DataColumn, _
                DCol4 As DataColumn, DCol5 As DataColumn, DCol6 As DataColumn, DCol7 As DataColumn, DCol8 As DataColumn, DCol9 As DataColumn, _
                dtRule As DataTable
            Dim BOTransactionDetail As New BO_TransactionDetail
            Dim boEmployeeRule As New BO_EmployeeRule
            Dim obj As New ReportHelper
            DCol1 = New DataColumn("FirstShift", GetType(System.String))
            DCol2 = New DataColumn("SecondShift", GetType(System.String))
            DCol3 = New DataColumn("ThirdShift", GetType(System.String))
            DCol4 = New DataColumn("TotalShift", GetType(System.String))
            DCol5 = New DataColumn("DeductionShift", GetType(System.String))
            DCol6 = New DataColumn("NetExcess", GetType(System.String))
            DCol7 = New DataColumn("OT", GetType(System.String))
            DCol8 = New DataColumn("TotalExtraTime", GetType(System.String))
            DCol9 = New DataColumn("TotalLateTime", GetType(System.String))

            DCol1.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol2.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol3.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol4.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol5.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol6.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol7.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol8.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol9.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"

            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol1)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol2)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol3)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol4)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol5)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol6)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol7)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol8)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol9)

            Dim dblTemp As Double = 0
            Dim dblLateTime As Double = 0
            Dim dblShortTime As Double = 0
            Dim dblExcessTime As Double = 0
            Dim dblTotalShiftDeduction As Double = 0
            Dim strSpendTime As String = ""
            Dim strScheduleTime As String = ""
            Dim dblTotalHours As Double = 0
            Dim intEmployeeID As Integer = 0
            Dim ShiftID As Integer = 0
            Dim schdInDate As Date
            Dim dblStartingTime As Double = 0
            Dim dblInTime As Double = 0
            Dim dblOutTime As Double = 0
            Dim dblBeforeTime As Double = 0
            Dim dblLateTimeBetweenGrace As Double = 0
            Dim tsLate As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan
            Dim tsTotalLate As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan
            Dim strLateTime As String = ""

            For j As Integer = 0 To dt2.Rows.Count - 1

                intEmployeeID = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("EmployeeID")) = True, 0, dt2.Rows(j)("EmployeeID"))
                ShiftID = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ShiftID")) = True, 0, dt2.Rows(j)("ShiftID"))
                schdInDate = CDate(dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleDate"))

                dblLateTimeBetweenGrace = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace")))
                If dblLateTimeBetweenGrace > 0 And Left(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace").ToString, 1) <> "-" Then
                    dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime") = dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace")
                End If

                strLateTime = dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")
                Dim intHr As Integer, intMin As Integer, intSec As Integer = 0
                Dim strLatearr() As String = strLateTime.Split(":")
                intHr = strLatearr(0)
                intMin = strLatearr(1)

                strLateTime = String.Format("{0:00}", intHr) & ":" & String.Format("{0:00}", intMin) & ":" & String.Format("{0:00}", intSec)

                dblLateTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")))
                dblShortTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime")))
                dblExcessTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")))
                dblStartingTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")))
                dblInTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")))
                dblOutTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")))

                tsLate = New TimeSpan(strLateTime.Substring(0, 2), strLateTime.Substring(3, 2), "00")
                tsTotalLate = tsTotalLate.Add(tsLate)

                strSpendTime = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("SpendTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("SpendTime"))
                strScheduleTime = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleTime"))
                dblTotalHours = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(strSpendTime) - obj.ConvertTimeToHours(strScheduleTime)
                dblTotalHours = Math.Round(dblTotalHours, 2)
                dblBeforeTime = dblStartingTime - dblInTime
                If dblBeforeTime < 0 Then
                    dblBeforeTime = 0
                End If
                If dblInTime = 0 And dblOutTime = 0 Then
                    dblTotalHours = 0
                    dblBeforeTime = 0
                End If
                dblBeforeTime = Math.Round(dblBeforeTime, 2)

                dtRule = boEmployeeRule.tblEmployeeRule.GetDatabyEmployeeRule(intEmployeeID, "ExemptOTDeduction", CompanyID)
                If Not dtRule.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                    If ShiftID > 0 Then
                        Dim dtShiftTrack As DataTable = boShift.tblShiftTrackDetail.GetDetailCompanyShift(ShiftID, CompanyID)
                        If dtShiftTrack.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                            Dim tsFirstShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsSecondShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsThirdShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsFirstStart As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsFirstEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim dblFirstDeductedMinutes As Double = 0
                            Dim tsSecondStart As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsSecondEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim dblSecondDeductedMinutes As Double = 0
                            Dim tsThirdStart As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsThirdEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim dblThirdDeductedMinutes As Double = 0

                            For iShiftTrack As Integer = 1 To dtShiftTrack.Rows.Count
                                If iShiftTrack = 1 Then
                                    tsFirstStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                    tsFirstEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                    dblFirstDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                                ElseIf iShiftTrack = 2 Then
                                    tsSecondStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                    tsSecondEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                    dblSecondDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                                ElseIf iShiftTrack = 3 Then
                                    tsThirdStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                    tsThirdEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                    dblThirdDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                                End If
                            Next

                            Dim dtTimelyShift As DataTable = BOTransactionDetail.tblTransactionDetail.GetDatabyActivityDateAndEmployeeID(schdInDate, intEmployeeID)
                            If dtTimelyShift.Rows.Count > 2 Then

                                Dim tsTemporary1 As TimeSpan
                                Dim tsTemporary2 As TimeSpan

                                Dim rowCount As Integer = dtTimelyShift.Rows.Count
                                Dim bolDiff As Boolean = False, intShift As Integer = 0
                                For k As Integer = 1 To rowCount - 2

                                    Try
                                        tsTemporary1 = TimeSpan.Parse(dtTimelyShift.Rows(k)("ActivityTime").ToString)
                                    Catch ex As Exception
                                        Continue For
                                    End Try

                                    If (tsTemporary1 >= tsFirstStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsFirstEnd) Then
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                            bolDiff = True
                                            If tsTemporary2 = tsFirstEnd Then
                                                intShift = 2
                                            Else
                                                intShift = 1
                                            End If
                                            If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 1 Then
                                                tsFirstShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                            Else
                                                If intShift = 2 Then
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsThirdStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 3

                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        End If

                                    ElseIf (tsTemporary1 >= tsSecondStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsSecondEnd) Then
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                            bolDiff = True
                                            If tsTemporary2 = tsSecondEnd Then
                                                intShift = 3
                                            Else
                                                intShift = 2
                                            End If
                                            If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 2 Then
                                                tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                            Else
                                                If intShift = 1 Then
                                                    tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 2

                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        End If

                                    ElseIf (tsTemporary1 >= tsThirdStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsThirdEnd) Then
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                            bolDiff = True
                                            intShift = 3
                                            If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 3 Then
                                                tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                            Else
                                                If intShift = 1 Then
                                                    tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 2

                                                ElseIf intShift = 2 Then
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsThirdStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 3

                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            If tsTemporary1 < tsFirstStart Then
                                                tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsFirstStart.Ticks)
                                                bolDiff = True
                                                intShift = 1
                                                If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                    GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        Else
ShiftTrackPrinciple:
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 1 Then
                                                tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                                tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsThirdStart)

                                            ElseIf intShift = 2 Then
                                                tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsThirdStart)

                                            ElseIf intShift = 3 Then
                                                tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)

                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                Next

                                If dblThirdDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                    dblThirdDeductedMinutes = (dblThirdDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                    If tsThirdShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblThirdDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                        tsThirdShift = tsThirdShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblThirdDeductedMinutes)))
                                    End If
                                End If
                                If dblSecondDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                    dblSecondDeductedMinutes = (dblSecondDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                    If tsSecondShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblSecondDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                        tsSecondShift = tsSecondShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblSecondDeductedMinutes)))
                                    End If
                                End If
                                If dblFirstDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                    dblFirstDeductedMinutes = (dblFirstDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                    If tsFirstShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblFirstDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                        tsFirstShift = tsFirstShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblFirstDeductedMinutes)))
                                    End If
                                End If

                                dt2(j)("FirstShift") = tsFirstShift.ToString
                                dt2(j)("SecondShift") = tsSecondShift.ToString
                                dt2(j)("ThirdShift") = tsThirdShift.ToString

                                dblTotalShiftDeduction = tsFirstShift.TotalHours + tsSecondShift.TotalHours + tsThirdShift.TotalHours
                                dblTotalShiftDeduction = Math.Round(dblTotalShiftDeduction, 2)
                                dt2(j)("TotalShift") = obj.convertTotime(dblTotalShiftDeduction)
                                dt2(j)("DeductionShift") = obj.convertTotime(Math.Round((tsFirstShift.TotalHours + tsThirdShift.TotalHours), 2))
                                'dblTemp = ((dblLateTime + dblShortTime + tsFirstShift.TotalHours + tsThirdShift.TotalHours) - dblExcessTime)
                                'dblTemp = (((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime) - dblShortTime) - dblTotalShiftDeduction)
                                dblTemp = (((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime)) - dblTotalShiftDeduction)
                                dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                                If dblTemp < 0 Then
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                                Else
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                                End If
                                dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                            Else
                                'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime) - dblShortTime)
                                dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime))
                                dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                                If dblTemp < 0 Then
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                                Else
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                                End If
                                dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                            End If
                        Else
                            'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime) - dblShortTime)
                            dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime))
                            dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                            If dblTemp < 0 Then
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                            Else
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                            End If
                            dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                        End If
                    Else
                        'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime) - dblShortTime)
                        dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime))
                        If dblTemp * 60 < 30 Then
                            dt2(j)("OT") = "00:00:00"
                        Else
                            If dblTemp > 5 Then
                                dblTemp = dblTemp - 0.75
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                            Else
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                            End If
                            dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)
                        End If
                    End If
                Else
                    'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime) - dblShortTime)
                    dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime))
                    dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                    If dblTemp < 0 Then
                        dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                    Else
                        dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                    End If
                    dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                End If
                dt2(j)("NetExcess") = obj.convertTotime(dblExcessTime - (dblLateTime + dblShortTime))
                dt2(j)("TotalExtraTime") = obj.convertTotime(dblTotalHours)
                dt2(j)("TotalLateTime") = obj.GetFormattedTimeSpanValue(tsTotalLate)

            Next

            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)

            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                'SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_Customized.rpt")
                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_TimelyShift_Customized.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew.Tables(0))

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strVersion", "SAMCO")

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_TimelyShift_Customized() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_TimelyShift_Customized = False
        'Dim inttest As Integer = 0
        'Dim strTest As String = ""
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable



            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
            Dim intTotalDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")
            Dim ds As DataSet

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                'If IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then
                '    If blnIsExcludeAfterGraceInTime = True Then
                '        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _

                '    End If
                'Else

                '        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                '                CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                '                , , , , True, , , , , , , , , , False, True)
                '    Else
                '        ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                '                CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                '                , , , , , , , , , , , , , , False, True)
                '    End If
                'End If

                ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                    CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , _
                    , , , , True, , , , , , , , , , False, True)

                If dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

            Next

            dt2.Columns.Remove("TotalLateTime")

            Dim DCol1 As DataColumn, DCol2 As DataColumn, DCol3 As DataColumn, _
                DCol4 As DataColumn, DCol5 As DataColumn, DCol6 As DataColumn, DCol7 As DataColumn, DCol8 As DataColumn, DCol9 As DataColumn, _
                dtRule As DataTable
            Dim BOTransactionDetail As New BO_TransactionDetail
            Dim boEmployeeRule As New BO_EmployeeRule
            Dim obj As New ReportHelper
            DCol1 = New DataColumn("FirstShift", GetType(System.String))
            DCol2 = New DataColumn("SecondShift", GetType(System.String))
            DCol3 = New DataColumn("ThirdShift", GetType(System.String))
            DCol4 = New DataColumn("TotalShift", GetType(System.String))
            DCol5 = New DataColumn("DeductionShift", GetType(System.String))
            DCol6 = New DataColumn("NetExcess", GetType(System.String))
            DCol7 = New DataColumn("OT", GetType(System.String))
            DCol8 = New DataColumn("TotalExtraTime", GetType(System.String))
            DCol9 = New DataColumn("TotalLateTime", GetType(System.String))
            DCol1.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol2.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol3.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol4.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol5.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol6.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol7.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol8.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol9.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol1)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol2)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol3)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol4)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol5)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol6)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol7)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol8)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol9)

            Dim dblTemp As Double = 0
            Dim dblLateTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsLateTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblShortTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsShortTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblExcessTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsExcessTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblTotalShiftDeduction As Double = 0
            Dim tsTotalShiftDeduction As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim strSpendTime As String = ""
            Dim strScheduleTime As String = ""
            Dim dblTotalHours As Double = 0
            Dim intEmployeeID As Integer = 0
            Dim ShiftID As Integer = 0
            Dim schdInDate As Date
            Dim dblStartingTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsStartingTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblInTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsInTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblOutTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsOutTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblBeforeTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsBeforeTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblLateTimeBetweenGrace As Double = 0
            Dim tsLate As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan
            Dim tsTotalLate As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan
            Dim strLateTime As String = ""



            For j As Integer = 0 To dt2.Rows.Count - 1

                'inttest = j
                'strTest = dt2.Rows(j)("EmployeeID")

                intEmployeeID = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("EmployeeID")) = True, 0, dt2.Rows(j)("EmployeeID"))
                ShiftID = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ShiftID")) = True, 0, dt2.Rows(j)("ShiftID"))
                schdInDate = CDate(dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleDate"))

                dblLateTimeBetweenGrace = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace")))
                If dblLateTimeBetweenGrace > 0 And Left(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace").ToString, 1) <> "-" Then
                    dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime") = dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace")
                End If

                strLateTime = dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")
                Dim intHr As Integer, intMin As Integer, intSec As Integer = 0
                Dim strLatearr() As String = strLateTime.Split(":")
                intHr = strLatearr(0)
                intMin = strLatearr(1)

                strLateTime = String.Format("{0:00}", intHr) & ":" & String.Format("{0:00}", intMin) & ":" & String.Format("{0:00}", intSec)

                dblLateTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")))
                dblShortTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime")))
                dblExcessTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")))
                dblStartingTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")))
                dblInTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")))
                dblOutTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")))

                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                '==================================================================================
                tsLateTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime"))).ToString())
                tsShortTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime"))).ToString())
                tsExcessTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime"))).ToString())
                tsStartingTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")).ToString())
                tsInTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")).ToString())
                tsOutTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")).ToString())


                tsLate = New TimeSpan(strLateTime.Substring(0, 2), strLateTime.Substring(3, 2), "00")
                tsTotalLate = tsTotalLate.Add(tsLate)
                strSpendTime = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("SpendTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("SpendTime")))


                strScheduleTime = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleTime")))
                'Difference Change from double to TimeSpane Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                '===================================================================================
                'dblTotalHours = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(strSpendTime) - obj.ConvertTimeToHours(strScheduleTime)

                'Dim arhour() As String = strSpendTime.Split(":")

                'Dim intHour As Integer = arhour(0)

                'If intHour >= 24 Then
                '    Dim day As Integer = CInt(intHour / 24)
                '    Dim hrs As Integer = (intHour - (intHour / 24) * 24)
                '    strSpendTime = day & "." & hrs & ":" & arhour(1)
                'End If



                dblTotalHours = ConvertTimeToHours_New((TimeSpan.Parse(strSpendTime) - TimeSpan.Parse(strScheduleTime)).ToString())

                If TimeSpan.Parse(strSpendTime) < TimeSpan.Parse(strScheduleTime) Then
                    dblTotalHours = dblTotalHours * -1
                End If

                dblTotalHours = Math.Round(dblTotalHours, 2)

                'Difference Change from double to TimeSpane Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                '==================================================================================
                'dblBeforeTime = dblStartingTime - dblInTime

                If tsInTime < tsStartingTime Then
                    tsBeforeTime = tsStartingTime - tsInTime
                    dblBeforeTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(tsBeforeTime.ToString)
                Else
                    dblBeforeTime = 0
                End If

                'If dblBeforeTime < 0 Then
                '    dblBeforeTime = 0
                'End If

                If dblInTime = 0 And dblOutTime = 0 Then
                    dblTotalHours = 0
                    dblBeforeTime = 0
                End If
                dblBeforeTime = Math.Round(dblBeforeTime, 2)

                dtRule = boEmployeeRule.tblEmployeeRule.GetDatabyEmployeeRule(intEmployeeID, "ExemptOTDeduction", CompanyID)
                If Not dtRule.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                    If ShiftID > 0 Then
                        Dim dtShiftTrack As DataTable = boShift.tblShiftTrackDetail.GetDetailCompanyShift(ShiftID, CompanyID)
                        If dtShiftTrack.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                            Dim tsFirstShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsSecondShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsThirdShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsFirstStart As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsFirstEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim dblFirstDeductedMinutes As Double = 0
                            Dim tsSecondStart As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsSecondEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim dblSecondDeductedMinutes As Double = 0
                            Dim tsThirdStart As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsThirdEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim dblThirdDeductedMinutes As Double = 0



                            For iShiftTrack As Integer = 1 To dtShiftTrack.Rows.Count
                                If iShiftTrack = 1 Then
                                    tsFirstStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                    tsFirstEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                    dblFirstDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                                ElseIf iShiftTrack = 2 Then
                                    tsSecondStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                    tsSecondEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                    dblSecondDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                                ElseIf iShiftTrack = 3 Then
                                    tsThirdStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                    tsThirdEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                    dblThirdDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                                End If
                            Next

                            Dim dtTimelyShift As DataTable = BOTransactionDetail.tblTransactionDetail.GetDatabyActivityDateAndEmployeeID(schdInDate, intEmployeeID)
                            If dtTimelyShift.Rows.Count > 2 Then

                                Dim tsTemporary1 As TimeSpan
                                Dim tsTemporary2 As TimeSpan

                                Dim rowCount As Integer = dtTimelyShift.Rows.Count
                                Dim bolDiff As Boolean = False, intShift As Integer = 0
                                For k As Integer = 1 To rowCount - 2

                                    Try
                                        tsTemporary1 = TimeSpan.Parse(dtTimelyShift.Rows(k)("ActivityTime").ToString)
                                    Catch ex As Exception
                                        Continue For
                                    End Try

                                    If (tsTemporary1 >= tsFirstStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsFirstEnd) Then
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                            bolDiff = True
                                            If tsTemporary2 = tsFirstEnd Then
                                                intShift = 2
                                            Else
                                                intShift = 1
                                            End If
                                            If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 1 Then
                                                tsFirstShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                            Else
                                                If intShift = 2 Then
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsThirdStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 3

                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        End If

                                    ElseIf (tsTemporary1 >= tsSecondStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsSecondEnd) Then
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                            bolDiff = True
                                            If tsTemporary2 = tsSecondEnd Then
                                                intShift = 3
                                            Else
                                                intShift = 2
                                            End If
                                            If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 2 Then
                                                tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                            Else
                                                If intShift = 1 Then
                                                    tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 2

                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        End If

                                    ElseIf (tsTemporary1 >= tsThirdStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsThirdEnd) Then
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                            bolDiff = True
                                            intShift = 3
                                            If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 3 Then
                                                tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                            Else
                                                If intShift = 1 Then
                                                    tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 2

                                                ElseIf intShift = 2 Then
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsThirdStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 3

                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            If tsTemporary1 < tsFirstStart Then
                                                tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsFirstStart.Ticks)
                                                bolDiff = True
                                                intShift = 1
                                                If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                    GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        Else
ShiftTrackPrinciple:
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 1 Then
                                                tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                                tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsThirdStart)

                                            ElseIf intShift = 2 Then
                                                tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsThirdStart)

                                            ElseIf intShift = 3 Then
                                                tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)

                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                Next

                                If dblThirdDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                    dblThirdDeductedMinutes = (dblThirdDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                    If tsThirdShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblThirdDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                        tsThirdShift = tsThirdShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblThirdDeductedMinutes)))
                                    End If
                                End If
                                If dblSecondDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                    dblSecondDeductedMinutes = (dblSecondDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                    If tsSecondShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblSecondDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                        tsSecondShift = tsSecondShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblSecondDeductedMinutes)))
                                    End If
                                End If
                                If dblFirstDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                    dblFirstDeductedMinutes = (dblFirstDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                    If tsFirstShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblFirstDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                        tsFirstShift = tsFirstShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblFirstDeductedMinutes)))
                                    End If
                                End If

                                dt2(j)("FirstShift") = tsFirstShift.ToString
                                dt2(j)("SecondShift") = tsSecondShift.ToString
                                dt2(j)("ThirdShift") = tsThirdShift.ToString

                                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U 
                                '===========================================
                                'dblTotalShiftDeduction = tsFirstShift.TotalHours + tsSecondShift.TotalHours + tsThirdShift.TotalHours
                                dblTotalShiftDeduction = obj.ConvertTimeToHours((tsFirstShift + tsSecondShift + tsThirdShift).ToString())
                                tsTotalShiftDeduction = tsFirstShift + tsSecondShift + tsThirdShift

                                dblTotalShiftDeduction = Math.Round(dblTotalShiftDeduction, 2)

                                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                                '===========================================
                                'dt2(j)("TotalShift") = obj.convertTotime(dblTotalShiftDeduction)
                                dt2(j)("TotalShift") = tsTotalShiftDeduction.ToString()


                                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                                '===========================================
                                'dt2(j)("DeductionShift") = obj.convertTotime(Math.Round((tsFirstShift.TotalHours + tsThirdShift.TotalHours), 2))
                                dt2(j)("DeductionShift") = (tsFirstShift.TotalHours + tsThirdShift.TotalHours).ToString()


                                'dblTemp = ((dblLateTime + dblShortTime + tsFirstShift.TotalHours + tsThirdShift.TotalHours) - dblExcessTime)
                                'dblTemp = (((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime) - dblShortTime) - dblTotalShiftDeduction)
                                'dblTemp = (((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime)) - dblTotalShiftDeduction) 'Last Sikandar
                                dblTemp = (((dblTotalHours)) - dblTotalShiftDeduction) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)
                                dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                                If dblTemp < 0 Then
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                                Else
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                                End If
                                dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                            Else
                                'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime) - dblShortTime)
                                'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime)) 'Last Sikandar
                                dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)

                                dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                                If dblTemp < 0 Then
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                                Else
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                                End If
                                dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                            End If
                        Else
                            'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime) - dblShortTime)
                            'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime)) 'Last Sikandar
                            dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)
                            dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                            If dblTemp < 0 Then
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                            Else
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                            End If
                            dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                        End If
                    Else
                        'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime) - dblShortTime)
                        'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime)) 'Last Sikandar
                        dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)

                        If dblTemp * 60 < 30 Then
                            dt2(j)("OT") = "00:00:00"
                        Else
                            If Math.Round(dblTemp, 2) >= 5.75 Then
                                dblTemp = dblTemp - 0.75
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                            Else
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                            End If
                            dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)
                        End If
                    End If
                Else
                    'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime) - dblShortTime)
                    'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours - dblLateTime)) 'Last Sikandar
                    dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)

                    dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                    If dblTemp < 0 Then
                        dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                    Else
                        dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                    End If
                    dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                End If

                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                '==================================================================================
                'dt2(j)("NetExcess") = obj.convertTotime(dblExcessTime - (dblLateTime + dblShortTime))
                dt2(j)("NetExcess") = tsExcessTime - (tsLateTime + tsShortTime)

                dt2(j)("TotalExtraTime") = obj.convertTotime(dblTotalHours)
                dt2(j)("TotalLateTime") = obj.GetFormattedTimeSpanValue(tsTotalLate)

            Next

            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)

            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                'SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_Customized.rpt")
                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_TimelyShift_Customized.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew.Tables(0))

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strVersion", "SAMCO")

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetTimeSpanceByValue(ByVal strTimeSpane As String) As String

        Dim arhour() As String = strTimeSpane.Split(":")

        Dim intHour As Integer = arhour(0)

        If intHour >= 24 Then
            Dim day As Integer = CInt(intHour / 24)
            Dim hrs As Integer = (intHour - (intHour / 24) * 24)
            strTimeSpane = day & "." & hrs & ":" & arhour(1)
        End If

        Return strTimeSpane

    End Function
    Private Function ConvertTimeToHours_New(ByVal pTimeStr As String) As Double
        Dim ArrTime() As String = pTimeStr.Split(":"), IsNegetive As Boolean = False
        Dim dblhours As Double, dblMin As Double, dblTotalHours As Double
        dblhours = Val(ArrTime(0))
        dblMin = Val(ArrTime(1))

        If dblhours < 0 Then
            dblhours = dblhours * -1
            IsNegetive = True
        End If

        If dblMin < 0 Then
            dblMin = dblMin * -1
            IsNegetive = True
        End If

        dblTotalHours = dblhours + dblMin / 60
        dblTotalHours = Math.Round(dblTotalHours, 2)

        'If IsNegetive = True Then dblTotalHours = dblTotalHours * -1

        Return dblTotalHours

    End Function

    Private Function GetEmployeeDataCard() As Boolean
        GetEmployeeDataCard = False
        Try

            Dim dtData As DataTable = Nothing, strSubReportTitle As String = "", strDateCriteria As String = "", _
                dtEmployeeImage As New DataTable, dtImage As New DataTable, i As Integer = 0, _
                dtLeave As New DataTable, IsELeave As Boolean = False, IsPayroll As Boolean = False, _
                dtExperience As New DataTable, dtEmployeeQualification As New DataTable, _
                IsHR As Boolean = False, IsAdditionalHR As Boolean = False, dtEmployeeDependant As New DataTable, _
                dtEmployeeAssets As New DataTable, dtEmployeeMedical As New DataTable, dtEmployeeTeam As New DataTable

            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeePortfolio(strEmployees, intCompanyID, dtEmployeeQualification, dtExperience, dtEmployeeDependant, dtEmployeeTeam, dtEmployeeAssets, dtEmployeeMedical)


            If clsCookie.EnableELeave.ToString.ToLower = "y" Then IsELeave = True
            If clsCookie.EnablePayroll.ToString.ToLower = "y" Then IsPayroll = True
            If clsCookie.EnableHR.ToString.ToLower = "y" Then IsHR = True
            If IsHR = True And CBool(boCompanyFlag.tblCompanyFlag.GetFlagValue_String("IsAdditionalDetailRequired", "False", intCompanyID)) = True Then IsAdditionalHR = True


            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 And IsELeave = True Then
                '--------Leave Statics---------------
                For i = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1
                    If i = 0 Then
                        dtLeave = GetLeaveSummary(dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeID"), dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo"), intCompanyID)
                    Else
                        dtLeave.Merge(GetLeaveSummary(dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeID"), dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo"), intCompanyID))
                    End If
                Next
            End If

            '--------Employee Images-------------
            dtEmployeeImage = ReportHelper.GetWorkerImage(strEmployees, CompanyID, "./")

            dtImage.Columns.Add("EmployeeImage", System.Type.GetType("System.Byte[]"))
            Dim DCol1 = New DataColumn("EmployeeID", GetType(System.Int64))
            dtImage.Columns.Add(DCol1)


            For i = 0 To dtEmployeeImage.Rows.Count - 1
                dtImage.Merge(EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.CreateEmployeeImageTable(dtEmployeeImage.Rows(i)("EmployeeID"), Server.MapPath(dtEmployeeImage.Rows(i)("EmployeeImage")), "dtEmployeeImage", "EmployeeImage"))
            Next


            Dim ds As New dsAttendanceFull
            Dim dt As DataTable

            'Add Column for fixed assets As instructed 
            '====================================================
            'Dim column As DataColumn = New DataColumn("Transport", GetType(System.Boolean))
            'ds.EmployeePortFolio.Columns.Add(column)
            'column = New DataColumn("Colony", GetType(System.Boolean))
            'ds.EmployeePortFolio.Columns.Add(column)
            'column = New DataColumn("Canteen", GetType(System.Boolean))
            'ds.EmployeePortFolio.Columns.Add(column)
            'ds.Tables("EmployeePortFolio").Columns.Add("Transport", GetType(System.Boolean))
            'ds.Tables("EmployeePortFolio").Columns.Add("Colony", GetType(System.Boolean))
            'ds.Tables("EmployeePortFolio").Columns.Add("Canteen", GetType(System.Boolean))


            Dim count As Integer, Increase As Integer

            Increase = 1
            For x As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1
                Dim row As DataRow
                If count = 4 Then
                    Increase += 1
                    count = 1
                Else
                    count += 1
                End If
                row = ds.Tables("EmployeePortFolio").NewRow
                row("GroupField") = Increase
                row("EmployeeID") = dtData.Rows(x)("EmployeeRefNo")
                row("EmployeeName") = dtData.Rows(x)("EmployeeName")
                row("Designation") = dtData.Rows(x)("DesignationTitle")
                row("Department") = dtData.Rows(x)("DepartmentTitle")
                row("BloodGroup") = dtData.Rows(x)("BloodGroup")
                row("MobileNO") = dtData.Rows(x)("MobileNumber")
                row("Address") = dtData.Rows(x)("Address")
                row("JoiningDate") = Format(dtData.Rows(x)("AppointmentDate"), "dd/MM/yyyy")
                row("Division") = dtData.Rows(x)("DivisionTitle")
                row("EmployeeCategory") = dtData.Rows(x)("EmployeeCategoryTitle")
                row("CompanyAddress") = dtData.Rows(x)("CompanyAddress")
                row("CNICNo") = dtData.Rows(x)("CNICNo")
                If dtImage.Select("EmployeeID = " & dtData.Rows(x)("EmployeeID")).Length <> 0 Then
                    dt = dtImage.Select("EmployeeID = " & dtData.Rows(x)("EmployeeID")).CopyToDataTable
                    row("EmployeeImage") = dt.Rows(0)("EmployeeImage")
                Else
                    Dim fs As System.IO.FileStream = Nothing
                    Dim br As System.IO.BinaryReader = Nothing
                    If System.IO.File.Exists(Server.MapPath("~\UsersPicture\nopicture.jpg")) = True Then
                        fs = New System.IO.FileStream(Server.MapPath("~\UsersPicture\nopicture.jpg"), System.IO.FileMode.Open)
                        br = New System.IO.BinaryReader(fs)
                        Dim byteArr() As Byte = br.ReadBytes(br.BaseStream.Length)
                        row("EmployeeImage") = byteArr
                    End If

                    br = Nothing
                    If IsNothing(fs) = False Then fs.Close()
                    fs = Nothing
                End If

                row("Transport") = dtEmployeeAssets.Select("EmployeeID  = " & dtData.Rows(x)("EmployeeID") & " And AssetsTitle = 'Transport' ").Length > 0
                row("Colony") = dtEmployeeAssets.Select("EmployeeID  = " & dtData.Rows(x)("EmployeeID") & " And AssetsTitle = 'Colony' ").Length > 0
                row("Canteen") = dtEmployeeAssets.Select("EmployeeID  = " & dtData.Rows(x)("EmployeeID") & " And AssetsTitle = 'Canteen' ").Length > 0

                ds.EmployeePortFolio.Rows.Add(row)
            Next

            ds.AcceptChanges()


            ''--------Company Logo-------------
            Dim dtImageLogo As New DataTable
            Dim boCompany As New BO_Company
            Dim dtCompany As DataTable = boCompany.tblCompany.GetDatabyKey(CompanyID)
            If dtCompany.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Dim strCompanyLogoFilePath As String = dtCompany.Rows(0)("CompanyLogo").ToString
                Dim strPath As String = "./CompanyLogos/"
                strCompanyLogoFilePath = strPath & strCompanyLogoFilePath
                dtImageLogo = EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.CreateImageTable( _
                                    Server.MapPath(strCompanyLogoFilePath), "dtCompanyLogo", "CompanyLogo")
            End If
            '-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(CompanyID)
            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeSlipMain.rpt")

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(ds.Tables("EmployeePortFolio").DefaultView.ToTable)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("dtCompanyLogo").SetDataSource(dtImageLogo)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName1", strCompanyName)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
