    Private Function TotalOutFlagTimeCalculation(ByVal outTimeFlage As Double) As Double

        Dim result As Double = 0
        result = Math.Round(1.5, 0, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
        Return result

    End Function
    Private Function TimeFormat(ByVal p As Integer) As String
        Dim rString As String = "00"
        If p > 9 Then
            Return p.ToString
        Else
            Return "0" & p
        End If
    End Function
    Public Function MinutesToTime(ByVal mMinutes As Long) As String
        'On Error GoTo ErrHandler

        '**********************************************************************
        'Function   :   SecondToTime
        'Purpose    :   This function takes total time in seconds and return
        '               back results "hours:minutes:seconds".
        '**********************************************************************

        Dim mH As Long, mM As Long, mS As Long, mTemp As Double

        mH = Fix((mMinutes / 60))
        mTemp = ((mMinutes / 60) - mH)
        mTemp = (mTemp * 60)
        mM = Fix(mTemp)
        mTemp = mTemp - mM
        mS = mTemp * 60

        '** New Change **
        If mH = 0 And mM = 0 And mS = 0 Then
            MinutesToTime = "00:00:00"
        ElseIf mH = 0 And mM = 0 Then
            MinutesToTime = "00" & ":00:" & mS
        ElseIf mH = 0 Then
            MinutesToTime = "00" & ":" & mM & ":" & mS
        ElseIf mM = 0 Then
            MinutesToTime = mH & ":00:" & mS
        ElseIf mS = 0 Then
            MinutesToTime = mH & ":" & mM & ":00"
        Else
            '** OLD **
            MinutesToTime = mH & ":" & mM & ":" & mS
        End If

    End Function

    Private Sub FillWeek()

        Dim BOMonthWeek As New BO_MonthWeek
        Dim dt As DataTable = BOMonthWeek.tblMonthWeek.GetDatabyMonthYearCompany(ddlMonth.SelectedValue, ddlyear.SelectedValue, CompanyID)
        ddlweek.Items.Clear()
        If dt.Rows.Count > 0 Then

            ddlweek.DataTextField = "Week"
            ddlweek.DataValueField = "Week"
            ddlweek.DataSource = dt
            ddlweek.DataBind()

            ddlweek.Items.Insert(0, New ListItem("- All -", "All"))
            ddlweek_SelectedIndexChanged(New Object, EventArgs.Empty)
        Else
            ddlweek.Items.Insert(0, New ListItem("- Select -", "-1"))
        End If

    End Sub

    Private Function Get10DaysAbsents() As Boolean

        Get10DaysAbsents = False

        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New dsAttendance.ShiftScheduleRegDataTable
            Dim strServerDateFormat As String = ConfigurationManager.AppSettings("ServerDateFormat")
            Dim intTotDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)  '0 Based
            Dim AbsentDays As Integer = 10

            If intTotDays > 31 Then
                Dim strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_FromAndToDateDiffMsg")
                Throw New Exception(IIf(strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg = "", "Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 31 days", strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg))
            End If

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            Dim dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable, ArrEmployeeID() As String = strEmployees.Split(",")
            
            If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.Employee10DaysAbsents1 Then AbsentDays = txtValue1.Text

            'Dim ds As New DataSet
            For i As Integer = 0 To ArrEmployeeID.Length - 1

                dt2 = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceByDateWithLeave(ArrEmployeeID(i), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)

                Dim isRule As Boolean = False
                Dim intAbs As Integer = 0
                Dim TotalAbsCount As Double = 0

                For Each dr As DataRow In dt2.Rows
                    If dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.ToLower = "a" Or (dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.ToLower = "h" And intAbs >= 1) Then
                        intAbs += 1
                        TotalAbsCount += 1
                        dt2.Rows(dt2.Rows.IndexOf(dr)).Item("TotalAbsent") = 1
                        If dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.ToLower = "h" Then
                            dt2.Rows(dt2.Rows.IndexOf(dr)).Item("AttendanceStatus") = "A"

                        End If
                    Else
                        intAbs = 0
                    End If
                    dt2.AcceptChanges()
                    If intAbs >= AbsentDays And isRule = False Then isRule = True
                Next


                If isRule = True Then
                    If dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                        dt1 = dt2
                    Else
                        dt1.Merge(dt2)
                    End If
                End If
                isRule = False
                TotalAbsCount = 0
                dt2 = Nothing

            Next


            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt1)

            If dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.Employee10DaysAbsents1 Then
                    SetReportPath("rpt10DaysContinousAbsent1.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rpt10DaysContinousAbsent.rpt")
                End If


                'Report Params
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                'rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptValue", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptFromDate", txtFromDate.Text)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptToDate", ToDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat.ToString()))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptDates", "Dates From : " & FromDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat) & _
                                " To " & ToDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportDays", DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer) + 1)


                'Report Params
                Dim datTemp As Date = FromDateServer


                For i As Integer = 1 To 31
                    Try

                        'Dim dayName1 As String = datTemp.Day.ToString("dddd")
                        Dim dayName1 As String = datTemp.DayOfWeek.ToString

                        If dayName1.ToLower = "sunday" Then   'Indicate Sundays
                            rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, "*" & datTemp.Day.ToString)
                        Else
                            rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, datTemp.Day.ToString)
                        End If


                    Catch ex As Exception  'Month does not have this Date
                        rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, " ")
                    End Try

                    datTemp = datTemp.AddDays(1)

                Next

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetEmployeeHRReport() As Boolean
        GetEmployeeHRReport = False
        Try

            Dim dtData As DataTable = Nothing, dtImage As New DataTable, i As Integer = 0
            Dim strWhere As String = "", strRptType As String = ""
            Dim ConfirmationDueDays As Integer = 0, dtDependent As New DataTable

            ConfirmationDueDays = boCompanyFlag.tblCompanyFlag.GetFlagValue_Numeric("ConfirmationDueDays", 0, CompanyID)

            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            Dim ds As New dsMaster.WorkerDetailDataTable


            If RptType.EmployeeHRReport_BirthDate = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = " And tblEmployee.BirthDate Between ? And ? "
                strRptType = "Birth"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Confirmation = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = " And (tblEmployee.HasConfirmed = 1 And  tblEmployee.ConfirmationDate Between ? And ? ) OR tblEmployee.ConfirmationDueDate Between ? And ? "
                strRptType = "Confirmation"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Appointment = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = " And tblEmployee.AppointmentDate Between ? And ? "
                strRptType = "Appointment"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Retirement = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = " And tblEmployee.RetirementDate Between ? And ? "
                strRptType = "Retirement"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_LengthOfService = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = ""
                strRptType = "LengthOfService"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Designation = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = ""
                strRptType = "Designation"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Designation = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = ""
                strRptType = "Department"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Designation = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = ""
                strRptType = "AllEmployee"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Designation = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = ""
                strRptType = "Grade"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_AgeWise = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = ""
                strRptType = "AgeWise"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_LocationWise = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = ""
                strRptType = "LocationWise"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Probationary = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = " And tblEmployee.ConfirmationDueDate Between ? And ? "
                strRptType = "Probationary"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_FunctionWise = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = ""
                strRptType = "FunctionWise"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Dependent = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = ""
                strRptType = "Dependent"
            ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_ProbationExtensionPeriod = hdRptType.Value Then
                strWhere = ""
                strRptType = "ProbationExtensionPeriod"
            End If

            dtData = ds
            ReportHelper.GetEmployeeInformation_Customized(strEmployees, intCompanyID, strWhere, strRptType, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, dtData, ConfirmationDueDays)
            If RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Dependent = hdRptType.Value Then
                dtDependent = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeInformation_Dependent(strEmployees, intCompanyID, strWhere)
            End If

            If RptType.EmployeeHRReport_LengthOfService = hdRptType.Value Then
                dtData.DefaultView.RowFilter = "ServiceDuration >=" & CDbl(txtValue1.Text) & " And ServiceDuration <= " & CDbl(txtValue.Text) & ""
                dtData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable
            End If

            '--------Company Logo-------------
            Dim dtCompany As DataTable = boCompany.tblCompany.GetDatabyKey(CompanyID)
            If dtCompany.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Dim strCompanyLogoFilePath As String = dtCompany.Rows(0)("CompanyLogo").ToString
                Dim strPath As String = "~/CompanyLogos/"
                strCompanyLogoFilePath = strPath & strCompanyLogoFilePath
                dtImage = EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.CreateImageTable( _
                                    Server.MapPath(strCompanyLogoFilePath), "dtCompanyLogo", "CompanyLogo")
            End If
            '-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(intCompanyID)
            Dim strDateCriteria As String = ""


            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If RptType.EmployeeHRReport_BirthDate = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_BirthWise.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "Birth Date Between " & strFromDateClient & " And " & strToDateClient & " "
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Confirmation = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_ConfirmationDateWsie.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "For Confirmation Date Between " & strFromDateClient & " And " & strToDateClient & " "
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Appointment = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_AppointmentDateWise.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "Appointment Date Between " & strFromDateClient & " And " & strToDateClient & " "
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Retirement = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_RetirementDateWise.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "Retirement Date Between " & strFromDateClient & " And " & strToDateClient & " "
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_LengthOfService = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_LengthofService.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "Between " & CDbl(txtValue1.Text) & " And " & CDbl(txtValue.Text) & " Year Service"
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Designation = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_DesignationWise.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "For All Desigantion"
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Department = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_DepartmentWise.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = ""
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_AllEmployee = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_All.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "Sort Order : Employee Name"
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Grade = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_GradeWise.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = ""
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_AgeWise = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_AgeWise.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "Between " & CDbl(txtValue1.Text) & " And " & CDbl(txtValue.Text) & " Year"
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_LocationWise = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_LocationWise.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "CSAPL Geo"
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Probationary = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_ProbationaryDateWise.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "From " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient & " "
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_FunctionWise = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_FunctionWise.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "Function Wise"
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_Dependent = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_Dependents.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = ""
                ElseIf RptType.EmployeeHRReport_ProbationExtensionPeriod = hdRptType.Value Then
                    SetReportPath("rptEmployeeInfo_ProbationExtension.rpt")
                    strDateCriteria = "Extension Information"
                    dtData.DefaultView.RowFilter = "ConfirmationDueDate <> '1/1/1900' And LogicalConfirmationDueDate < ConfirmationDueDate "
                    dtData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("dtCompanyLogo").SetDataSource(dtImage)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria", strDateCriteria)
                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function

    Private Function GetDailyAttendance_FlagWise() As Boolean

        GetDailyAttendance_FlagWise = False
        Dim strReportPath As String = ""

        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim strFilter As String = ""
            If ddlShift.SelectedValue <> 0 Then
                If strFilter <> "" Then strFilter = strFilter & " And "
                strFilter = strFilter & " ShiftID=" & ddlShift.SelectedValue
            End If

            Dim ds As DataSet

            ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(strEmployees, blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, _
                    boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, False, strFilter, False, True, False, False, False, 0, 0, False, False, IsAttendanceFlag)

            Dim dt As DataTable
            dt = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
            Dim dt2 As DataTable
            dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable

            For Each dr As DataRow In dt2.Rows
                If dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("InTimeIsValid")) = True And CBool(dr("OutTimeIsValid")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = "Present"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("InTimeIsValid")) = True And CBool(dr("OutTimeIsValid")) = False Then
                    dr("Remarks") = "Present & OutTime Missing"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("InTimeIsValid")) = False And CBool(dr("OutTimeIsValid")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = "Present & InTime Missing"
                End If

                If dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (" & dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & "/" & dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & ")"
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim = "" Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (" & dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & ")"
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim = "" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (" & dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & ")"
                End If

                If dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("IsManualEntry")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (M.E)"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("IsTourEntry")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (V.E)"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(IIf(IsDBNull(dr("IsTravelEntry")) = True, False, dr("IsTravelEntry"))) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (Travel)"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(IIf(IsDBNull(dr("IsTrainingEntry")) = True, False, dr("IsTrainingEntry"))) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (Training)"
                End If
                dt2.AcceptChanges()
            Next


            If chklate.Checked = True And chkEarly.Checked = True And chkAbsent.Checked = False Then
                strFilter = "(InFlagCategory IN ('Late','Early') OR OutFlagCategory IN ('Late','Early')) And (InFlagCategory NOT IN ('Absent') OR OutFlagCategory NOT IN ('Absent') OR AttendanceStatus<>'A')"
            ElseIf chklate.Checked = True And chkEarly.Checked = False And chkAbsent.Checked = False Then
                strFilter = "(InFlagCategory IN ('Late') OR OutFlagCategory IN ('Late')) And (InFlagCategory NOT IN ('Absent') OR OutFlagCategory NOT IN ('Absent') OR AttendanceStatus<>'A')"
            ElseIf chklate.Checked = False And chkEarly.Checked = True And chkAbsent.Checked = False Then
                strFilter = "(InFlagCategory IN ('Early') OR OutFlagCategory IN ('Early')) And (InFlagCategory NOT IN ('Absent') OR OutFlagCategory NOT IN ('Absent') OR AttendanceStatus<>'A')"
            ElseIf chklate.Checked = False And chkEarly.Checked = False And chkAbsent.Checked = False Then
                strFilter = "(AttendanceStatus<>'A')"
            ElseIf chklate.Checked = True And chkEarly.Checked = True And chkAbsent.Checked = True Then
                strFilter = "(InFlagCategory IN ('Late','Early','Absent') OR OutFlagCategory IN ('Late','Early','Absent') OR AttendanceStatus = 'A')"
            ElseIf chklate.Checked = True And chkEarly.Checked = False And chkAbsent.Checked = True Then
                strFilter = "(InFlagCategory IN ('Late','Absent') OR OutFlagCategory IN ('Late','Absent') OR AttendanceStatus = 'A')"
            ElseIf chklate.Checked = False And chkEarly.Checked = True And chkAbsent.Checked = True Then
                strFilter = "(InFlagCategory IN ('Early','Absent') OR OutFlagCategory IN ('Early','Absent') OR AttendanceStatus = 'A')"
            End If

            If chkManual.Checked = True And chkAbsent.Checked = True Then
                If strFilter.Trim <> "" Then strFilter = " And "
                strFilter = " (IsManualEntry = 1 OR AttendanceStatus = 'A') "
            ElseIf chkManual.Checked = True And chkAbsent.Checked = False Then
                If strFilter.Trim <> "" Then strFilter = " And "
                strFilter = " IsManualEntry = 1 "
            End If

            dt2.DefaultView.RowFilter = strFilter
            dt2 = dt2.DefaultView.ToTable

            If dt2.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptDailyAttendanceDepartmentFlag1.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                If rdoInOnly.Checked = True Then
                    hdRptTitle.Value = hdRptTitle.Value & " (In Time)"
                Else
                    hdRptTitle.Value = hdRptTitle.Value & " (Out Time)"
                End If
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                'Report Specific Params
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strBeforeGrace1", "Within")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strBeforeGrace2", "Grace")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("AfterGrace1", "After")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strAfterGrace2", "Grace")

                SetParameterForDailyAttendanceInOrOut()

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_FlagWise() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_FlagWise = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
            Dim intTotalDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")
            Dim ds As DataSet

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1

                ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , , , , , , , True)

                If IsNothing(dt1) = False And dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt1 = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt1.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

                If IsNothing(dt2) = False And dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

            Next

            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt2)       'Detail
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt1)       'Master

            dsNew.Relations.Add("Master_Detail", New DataColumn() {dt2.Columns("EmployeeID")}, New DataColumn() {dt1.Columns("EmployeeID")}, False)
            dt2.Columns.Add("TotalAbsentWithFlag", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(Master_Detail).TotalAbsentWithFlag),0)")
            dt2.Columns.Add("TotalAbsentFlagWise", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(Master_Detail).TotalAbsentFlagWise),0)")
            dt2.Columns.Add("TotalLateFlagWise", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(Master_Detail).TotalLateFlagWise),0)")
            dt2.Columns.Add("TotalEarlyFlagWise", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(Master_Detail).TotalEarlyFlagWise),0)")
            dt2.Columns.Add("TotalHalfDayFlagWise", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(Master_Detail).TotalHalfDayFlagWise),0)")

            dt2.Columns.Add("LateFlagMinutes", GetType(System.Double))
            dt2.Columns.Add("EarlyFlagMinutes", GetType(System.Double))
            dt2.Columns.Add("OverTimeFlagMinutes", GetType(System.Double))
            dt2.Columns.Add("HalfdayFlagMinutes", GetType(System.Double))
            'dt2.Columns.Add("AbsentFlagMinutes", GetType(System.Double))
            'dt2.Columns.Add("OthersFlagMinutes", GetType(System.Double))

            dt2.Columns.Add("LateFlagTime", GetType(System.String))
            dt2.Columns.Add("EarlyFlagTime", GetType(System.String))
            dt2.Columns.Add("OverTimeFlagTime", GetType(System.String))
            dt2.Columns.Add("HalfdayFlagTime", GetType(System.String))

            For Each dr As DataRow In dt2.Rows
                If dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("InTimeIsValid")) = True And CBool(dr("OutTimeIsValid")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = "Present"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("InTimeIsValid")) = True And CBool(dr("OutTimeIsValid")) = False Then
                    dr("Remarks") = "Present & OutTime Missing"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("InTimeIsValid")) = False And CBool(dr("OutTimeIsValid")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = "Present & InTime Missing"
                End If

                If dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (" & dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & "/" & dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & ")"
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim = "" Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (" & dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & ")"
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim = "" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (" & dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & ")"
                End If

                If dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("IsManualEntry")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (M.E)"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("IsTourEntry")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (V.E)"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(IIf(IsDBNull(dr("IsTravelEntry")) = True, False, dr("IsTravelEntry"))) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (Travel)"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(IIf(IsDBNull(dr("IsTrainingEntry")) = True, False, dr("IsTrainingEntry"))) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (Training)"
                End If


                If dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "late" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "late" Then
                    dr("LateFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes")) + CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "late" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "late" Then
                    dr("LateFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "late" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "late" Then
                    dr("LateFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "late" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "late" Then
                    dr("LateFlagMinutes") = 0
                End If

                If dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "early" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "early" Then
                    dr("EarlyFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes")) + CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "early" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "early" Then
                    dr("EarlyFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "early" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "early" Then
                    dr("EarlyFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "early" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "early" Then
                    dr("EarlyFlagMinutes") = 0
                End If

                If dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "overtime" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "overtime" Then
                    dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes")) + CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "overtime" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "overtime" Then
                    dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "overtime" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "overtime" Then
                    dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "overtime" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "overtime" Then
                    dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes") = 0
                End If

                If dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "halfday" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "halfday" Then
                    dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes")) + CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "halfday" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "halfday" Then
                    dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "halfday" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "halfday" Then
                    dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "halfday" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "halfday" Then
                    dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes") = 0
                End If

                If CDbl(dr("LateFlagMinutes")) > 0 Then
                    dr("LateFlagTime") = ReportHelper.convertTotime((CDbl(dr("LateFlagMinutes")) / 60))
                Else
                    dr("LateFlagTime") = "00:00:00"
                End If

                If CDbl(dr("EarlyFlagMinutes")) > 0 Then
                    dr("EarlyFlagTime") = ReportHelper.convertTotime((CDbl(dr("EarlyFlagMinutes")) / 60))
                Else
                    dr("EarlyFlagTime") = "00:00:00"
                End If

                If CDbl(dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes")) > 0 Then
                    dr("OverTimeFlagTime") = ReportHelper.convertTotime((CDbl(dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes")) / 60))
                Else
                    dr("OverTimeFlagTime") = "00:00:00"
                End If

                If CDbl(dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes")) > 0 Then
                    dr("HalfdayFlagTime") = ReportHelper.convertTotime((CDbl(dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes")) / 60))
                Else
                    dr("HalfdayFlagTime") = "00:00:00"
                End If

                dt2.AcceptChanges()
            Next

            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceDetail_FlagWise.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Protected Sub readdatatable(ByRef pdt As DataTable)
        pdt = pdt
    End Sub
    Private Function GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceSummary_FlagWise() As Boolean

        GetEmployeeWiseAttendanceSummary_FlagWise = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable

            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
            Dim intTotalDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable
            Dim ds As DataSet, EmployeeListBreakup As String = ""
            EmployeeListBreakup = boCompanyFlag.tblCompanyFlag.GetFlagValue_String("EmployeeListBreakup", "Department", CompanyID)

            If EmployeeListBreakup.ToLower <> "employee" Then
                dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, EmployeeListBreakup)
                For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1

                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , , , , , , , True)

                    If IsNothing(dt1) = False And dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                        dt1 = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                    Else
                        dt1.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                    End If

                    If IsNothing(dt2) = False And dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                        dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                    Else
                        dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                    End If

                Next
            Else

                Dim ArrEmployee() As String = strEmployees.Split(",")
                For i As Integer = 0 To ArrEmployee.Length - 1

                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(ArrEmployee(i), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag, , , , , , , , , , , , True)

                    If IsNothing(dt1) = False And dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                        dt1 = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                    Else
                        dt1.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                    End If

                    If IsNothing(dt2) = False And dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                        dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                    Else
                        dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                    End If

                Next
            End If

            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt2)       'Detail
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt1)       'Master

            dsNew.Relations.Add("Master_Detail", New DataColumn() {dt2.Columns("EmployeeID")}, New DataColumn() {dt1.Columns("EmployeeID")}, False)
            dt2.Columns.Add("TotalAbsentWithFlag", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(Master_Detail).TotalAbsentWithFlag),0)")
            dt2.Columns.Add("TotalAbsentFlagWise", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(Master_Detail).TotalAbsentFlagWise),0)")
            dt2.Columns.Add("TotalLateFlagWise", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(Master_Detail).TotalLateFlagWise),0)")
            dt2.Columns.Add("TotalEarlyFlagWise", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(Master_Detail).TotalEarlyFlagWise),0)")
            dt2.Columns.Add("TotalHalfDayFlagWise", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(Master_Detail).TotalHalfDayFlagWise),0)")

            dt2.Columns.Add("LateFlagMinutes", GetType(System.Double))
            dt2.Columns.Add("EarlyFlagMinutes", GetType(System.Double))
            dt2.Columns.Add("OverTimeFlagMinutes", GetType(System.Double))
            dt2.Columns.Add("HalfdayFlagMinutes", GetType(System.Double))

            dt2.Columns.Add("LateFlagTime", GetType(System.String))
            dt2.Columns.Add("EarlyFlagTime", GetType(System.String))
            dt2.Columns.Add("OverTimeFlagTime", GetType(System.String))
            dt2.Columns.Add("HalfdayFlagTime", GetType(System.String))

            For Each dr As DataRow In dt2.Rows
                If dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("InTimeIsValid")) = True And CBool(dr("OutTimeIsValid")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = "Present"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("InTimeIsValid")) = True And CBool(dr("OutTimeIsValid")) = False Then
                    dr("Remarks") = "Present & OutTime Missing"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("InTimeIsValid")) = False And CBool(dr("OutTimeIsValid")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = "Present & InTime Missing"
                End If

                If dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (" & dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & "/" & dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & ")"
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim = "" Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (" & dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & ")"
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim = "" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim <> "" Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (" & dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim & ")"
                End If

                If dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("IsManualEntry")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (M.E)"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(dr("IsTourEntry")) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (V.E)"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(IIf(IsDBNull(dr("IsTravelEntry")) = True, False, dr("IsTravelEntry"))) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (Travel)"
                ElseIf dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.Trim.ToUpper = "P" And CBool(IIf(IsDBNull(dr("IsTrainingEntry")) = True, False, dr("IsTrainingEntry"))) = True Then
                    dr("Remarks") = dr("Remarks") & " (Training)"
                End If


                If dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "late" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "late" Then
                    dr("LateFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes")) + CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "late" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "late" Then
                    dr("LateFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "late" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "late" Then
                    dr("LateFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "late" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "late" Then
                    dr("LateFlagMinutes") = 0
                End If

                If dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "early" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "early" Then
                    dr("EarlyFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes")) + CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "early" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "early" Then
                    dr("EarlyFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "early" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "early" Then
                    dr("EarlyFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "early" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "early" Then
                    dr("EarlyFlagMinutes") = 0
                End If

                If dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "overtime" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "overtime" Then
                    dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes")) + CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "overtime" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "overtime" Then
                    dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "overtime" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "overtime" Then
                    dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "overtime" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "overtime" Then
                    dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes") = 0
                End If

                If dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "halfday" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "halfday" Then
                    dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes")) + CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "halfday" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "halfday" Then
                    dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("InFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "halfday" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower = "halfday" Then
                    dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes") = CDbl(dr("OutFlagMinutes"))
                ElseIf dr("InFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "halfday" And dr("OutFlagCategory").ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "halfday" Then
                    dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes") = 0
                End If

                If CDbl(dr("LateFlagMinutes")) > 0 Then
                    dr("LateFlagTime") = ReportHelper.convertTotime((CDbl(dr("LateFlagMinutes")) / 60))
                Else
                    dr("LateFlagTime") = "00:00:00"
                End If

                If CDbl(dr("EarlyFlagMinutes")) > 0 Then
                    dr("EarlyFlagTime") = ReportHelper.convertTotime((CDbl(dr("EarlyFlagMinutes")) / 60))
                Else
                    dr("EarlyFlagTime") = "00:00:00"
                End If

                If CDbl(dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes")) > 0 Then
                    dr("OverTimeFlagTime") = ReportHelper.convertTotime((CDbl(dr("OverTimeFlagMinutes")) / 60))
                Else
                    dr("OverTimeFlagTime") = "00:00:00"
                End If

                If CDbl(dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes")) > 0 Then
                    dr("HalfdayFlagTime") = ReportHelper.convertTotime((CDbl(dr("HalfdayFlagMinutes")) / 60))
                Else
                    dr("HalfdayFlagTime") = "00:00:00"
                End If

                dt2.AcceptChanges()
            Next

            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeWiseAttendanceSummary_FlagWise.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetAttendanceSummary_FlagWise_Customized() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceSummary_FlagWise_Customized = False

        Try

            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer
            Dim intTotalDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable
            Dim ds As DataSet, EmployeeListBreakup As String = ""
            EmployeeListBreakup = boCompanyFlag.tblCompanyFlag.GetFlagValue_String("EmployeeListBreakup", "Department", CompanyID)

            If EmployeeListBreakup.ToLower <> "employee" Then
                dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, EmployeeListBreakup)
                For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1

                    'ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSummary_Customized(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)
                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFlagSummary(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID, boCompany, boAttendRule, boFlag)

                    If IsNothing(dt1) = False And dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                        dt1 = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                    Else
                        dt1.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                    End If

                Next
            Else

                Dim ArrEmployee() As String = strEmployees.Split(",")
                For i As Integer = 0 To ArrEmployee.Length - 1

                    ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSummary_Customized(ArrEmployee(i), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)

                    If IsNothing(dt1) = False And dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                        dt1 = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                    Else
                        dt1.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                    End If

                Next
            End If

            
            If IsNothing(dt1) = False AndAlso dt1.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceSummary_FlagWise_Customized.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dt1.DefaultView.ToTable)

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetPerformanceReviewSummary() As Boolean

        GetPerformanceReviewSummary = False

        Try

            Dim dtPerformanceReview As New DataTable
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtPerformanceReview)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                dt1 = ReportHelper.GetPerformanceReviewSummary(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), ddlShift.SelectedValue, CompanyID)
                If dtPerformanceReview.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                    dtPerformanceReview.Merge(dt1)
                Else
                    dtPerformanceReview = dt1
                End If
            Next

            If IsNothing(dtPerformanceReview) = False AndAlso dtPerformanceReview.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptPerformanceReviewSummary.rpt")

                '--------Company Logo-------------
                Dim dtImage As New DataTable
                Dim dtCompany As DataTable = boCompany.tblCompany.GetDatabyKey(CompanyID)
                If dtCompany.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                    Dim strCompanyLogoFilePath As String = dtCompany.Rows(0)("CompanyLogo").ToString
                    Dim strPath As String = "~/CompanyLogos/"
                    strCompanyLogoFilePath = strPath & strCompanyLogoFilePath
                    dtImage = EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.CreateImageTable( _
                                        Server.MapPath(strCompanyLogoFilePath), "dtCompanyLogo", "CompanyLogo")
                End If
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtPerformanceReview.DefaultView.ToTable)
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("dtCompanyLogo").SetDataSource(dtImage)
                '-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompanyTitle", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReviewType", ddlShift.SelectedItem.Text)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetEmployeePresentInDuration() As Boolean

        GetEmployeePresentInDuration = False

        Try

            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable
            Dim datFromDateTime As System.DateTime = FromDateServer & " " & txtFromTime.Text & ":00.000"
            Dim datToDateTime As System.DateTime = FromDateServer & " " & txtToTime.Text & ":00.000"

            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                dt1 = ReportHelper.GetDataForEmployeesBetweenDuration(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), CompanyID, datFromDateTime, datToDateTime, FromDateServer)
                If dtAttendance.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                    dtAttendance.Merge(dt1)
                Else
                    dtAttendance = dt1
                End If
            Next

            If IsNothing(dtAttendance) = False AndAlso dtAttendance.Rows.Count > 0 Then


                SetReportPath("rptEmployeePresentInDurationDrildown.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendance.DefaultView.ToTable)
                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria", "Date : " & txtFromDate.Text & " Time Between " & txtFromTime.Text & " And " & txtToTime.Text & "")

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetEmployeePresentInOffDays() As Boolean

        GetEmployeePresentInOffDays = False

        Try

            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                dt1 = ReportHelper.GetDataForEmployeesPresentInOffDays(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), CompanyID, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)
                If dtAttendance.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                    dtAttendance.Merge(dt1)
                Else
                    dtAttendance = dt1
                End If
            Next

            If IsNothing(dtAttendance) = False AndAlso dtAttendance.Rows.Count > 0 Then


                SetReportPath("rptEmployeesPresentInOffDays.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendance.DefaultView.ToTable)
                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("Criteria", "Date From " & txtFromDate.Text.ToString & " To " & txtToDate.Text.ToString)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetLaborOverTimeReport_Customized() As Boolean

        GetLaborOverTimeReport_Customized = False

        Try

            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer

                If dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(ds.Tables(1).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

            Next

            If dt2.Columns.Contains("TotalLateTime") Then dt2.Columns.Remove("TotalLateTime")
            If dt2.Columns.Contains("TotalEarlyTime") Then dt2.Columns.Remove("TotalEarlyTime")

            Dim DCol1 As DataColumn, DCol2 As DataColumn, DCol3 As DataColumn, DCol4 As DataColumn, DCol5 As DataColumn, _

            Dim obj As New ReportHelper

            DCol4.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"

            DCol9.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol11.DefaultValue = "00:00"
            DCol10.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol12.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"
            DCol13.DefaultValue = "00:00:00"

            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol1)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol2)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol3)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol4)

            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol7)

            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol10)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol11)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol12)
            dt2.Columns.Add(DCol13)

            Dim tsTotalGrossOT As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim tsTotalOT As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim tsOutOfCompany As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim tsTotalOutOfCompany As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim tsLateEarlyTime1 As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim tsLateEarlyTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblTemp As Double = 0
            Dim dblLateTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsLateTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim tsEarlyTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblShortTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsShortTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblExcessTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsExcessTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblTotalShiftDeduction As Double = 0
            Dim tsTotalShiftDeduction As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim strSpendTime As String = ""
            Dim strScheduleTime As String = ""
            Dim dblTotalHours As Double = 0
            Dim intEmployeeID As Integer = 0
            Dim ShiftID As Integer = 0
            Dim schdInDate As Date
            Dim dblStartingTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsStartingTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblInTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsInTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblOutTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsOutTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
            Dim dblBeforeTime As Double = 0
            Dim tsBeforeTime As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)

            Dim tsLate As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan
            Dim tsTotalLate As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan
            Dim tsTotalEarly As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan

            For j As Integer = 0 To dt2.Rows.Count - 1

                'inttest = j

                If intEmployeeID <> IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("EmployeeID")) = True, 0, dt2.Rows(j)("EmployeeID")) Then
                    tsTotalGrossOT = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                    tsTotalLate = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                    tsTotalEarly = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                    tsTotalOutOfCompany = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                    tsLateEarlyTime = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                    tsTotalShiftDeduction = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                End If

                intEmployeeID = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("EmployeeID")) = True, 0, dt2.Rows(j)("EmployeeID"))
                ShiftID = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ShiftID")) = True, 0, dt2.Rows(j)("ShiftID"))
                schdInDate = CDate(dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleDate"))

                dblLateTimeBetweenGrace = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace")))
                If dblLateTimeBetweenGrace > 0 And Left(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace").ToString, 1) <> "-" Then
                    dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime") = dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace")
                End If

                strLateTime = dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")
                strEarlyTime = dt2.Rows(j)("EarlyTime")

                'Late Time
                Dim intHr As Integer, intMin As Integer, intSec As Integer = 0
                Dim strLatearr() As String = strLateTime.Split(":")
                intHr = strLatearr(0)
                intMin = strLatearr(1)
                strLateTime = String.Format("{0:00}", intHr) & ":" & String.Format("{0:00}", intMin) & ":" & String.Format("{0:00}", intSec)

                'Early Time
                strLatearr = strEarlyTime.Split(":")
                intHr = strLatearr(0)
