                dblLateTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")))
                dblShortTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime")))
                dblExcessTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")))
                dblStartingTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")))
                dblInTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")))
                dblOutTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")))

                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                '==================================================================================
                tsLateTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime"))).ToString())
                tsEarlyTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("EarlyTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("EarlyTime"))).ToString())
                tsShortTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime"))).ToString())
                tsExcessTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime"))).ToString())
                tsStartingTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")).ToString())
                tsInTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")).ToString())
                tsOutTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")).ToString())


                tsLate = New TimeSpan(strLateTime.Substring(0, 2), strLateTime.Substring(3, 2), "00")
                tsTotalLate = tsTotalLate.Add(tsLate)

                tsEarlyTime = New TimeSpan(strEarlyTime.Substring(0, 2), strEarlyTime.Substring(3, 2), "00")
                tsTotalEarly = tsTotalEarly.Add(tsEarlyTime)

                strSpendTime = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("SpendTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("SpendTime")))
                strScheduleTime = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleTime")))

                'By Umair
                dblTotalHours = ConvertTimeToHours_New(GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")).ToString())
                'If TimeSpan.Parse(strSpendTime) < TimeSpan.Parse(strScheduleTime) Then
                '    dblTotalHours = dblTotalHours * -1
                'End If

                dblTotalHours = Math.Round(dblTotalHours, 2)

                'Difference Change from double to TimeSpane Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                '==================================================================================
                If tsInTime < tsStartingTime Then
                    tsBeforeTime = tsStartingTime - tsInTime
                    dblBeforeTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(tsBeforeTime.ToString)
                Else
                    dblBeforeTime = 0
                End If

                If dblInTime = 0 And dblOutTime = 0 Then
                    dblTotalHours = 0
                    dblBeforeTime = 0
                End If
                dblBeforeTime = Math.Round(dblBeforeTime, 2)

                'closed by kashan on 07-05-2015 as per instruct by shahzaib no need of it.
                'dtRule = boEmployeeRule.tblEmployeeRule.GetDatabyEmployeeRule(intEmployeeID, "ExemptOTDeduction", CompanyID)
                'If Not dtRule.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                '-------------------------------------------------------------------------

                If ShiftID > 0 Then
                    Dim dtShiftTrack As DataTable = boShift.tblShiftTrackDetail.GetDetailCompanyShift(ShiftID, CompanyID)
                    If dtShiftTrack.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                        Dim tsFirstShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                        Dim tsSecondShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                        Dim tsThirdShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                        Dim tsFirstStart As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                        Dim tsFirstEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                        Dim dblFirstDeductedMinutes As Double = 0
                        Dim tsSecondStart As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                        Dim tsSecondEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                        Dim dblSecondDeductedMinutes As Double = 0
                        Dim tsThirdStart As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                        Dim tsThirdEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                        Dim dblThirdDeductedMinutes As Double = 0

                        For iShiftTrack As Integer = 1 To dtShiftTrack.Rows.Count
                            If iShiftTrack = 1 Then
                                tsFirstStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                tsFirstEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                dblFirstDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                            ElseIf iShiftTrack = 2 Then
                                tsSecondStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                tsSecondEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                dblSecondDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                            ElseIf iShiftTrack = 3 Then
                                tsThirdStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                tsThirdEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                dblThirdDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                            End If
                        Next

                        Dim dtTimelyShift As DataTable = BOTransactionDetail.tblTransactionDetail.GetDatabyActivityDateAndEmployeeID(schdInDate, intEmployeeID)
                        If dtTimelyShift.Rows.Count > 2 Then

                            Dim tsTemporary1 As TimeSpan
                            Dim tsTemporary2 As TimeSpan

                            Dim rowCount As Integer = dtTimelyShift.Rows.Count
                            Dim bolDiff As Boolean = False, intShift As Integer = 0
                            For k As Integer = 1 To rowCount - 2

                                Try
                                    tsTemporary1 = TimeSpan.Parse(dtTimelyShift.Rows(k)("ActivityTime").ToString)
                                Catch ex As Exception
                                    Continue For
                                End Try

                                If (tsTemporary1 >= tsFirstStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsFirstEnd) Then
                                    If bolDiff = False Then
                                        tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                        bolDiff = True
                                        If tsTemporary2 = tsFirstEnd Then
                                            intShift = 2
                                        Else
                                            intShift = 1
                                        End If
                                        If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                            GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        bolDiff = False
                                        If intShift = 1 Then
                                            tsFirstShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                        Else
                                            If intShift = 2 Then
                                                tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsThirdStart)
                                                tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                bolDiff = True
                                                intShift = 3

                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                ElseIf (tsTemporary1 >= tsSecondStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsSecondEnd) Then
                                    If bolDiff = False Then
                                        tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                        bolDiff = True
                                        If tsTemporary2 = tsSecondEnd Then
                                            intShift = 3
                                        Else
                                            intShift = 2
                                        End If
                                        If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                            GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        bolDiff = False
                                        If intShift = 2 Then
                                            tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                        Else
                                            If intShift = 1 Then
                                                tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                                tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                bolDiff = True
                                                intShift = 2

                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                ElseIf (tsTemporary1 >= tsThirdStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsThirdEnd) Then
                                    If bolDiff = False Then
                                        tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                        bolDiff = True
                                        intShift = 3
                                        If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                            GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        bolDiff = False
                                        If intShift = 3 Then
                                            tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                        Else
                                            If intShift = 1 Then
                                                tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                                tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                bolDiff = True
                                                intShift = 2

                                            ElseIf intShift = 2 Then
                                                tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsThirdStart)
                                                tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                bolDiff = True
                                                intShift = 3

                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                Else
                                    If bolDiff = False Then
                                        If tsTemporary1 < tsFirstStart Then
                                            tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsFirstStart.Ticks)
                                            bolDiff = True
                                            intShift = 1
                                            If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    Else
ShiftTrackPrinciple:
                                        bolDiff = False
                                        If intShift = 1 Then
                                            tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                            tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                            tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsThirdStart)

                                        ElseIf intShift = 2 Then
                                            tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                            tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsThirdStart)

                                        ElseIf intShift = 3 Then
                                            tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)

                                        End If
                                    End If
                                End If

                            Next

                            If dblThirdDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                dblThirdDeductedMinutes = (dblThirdDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                If tsThirdShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblThirdDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                    tsThirdShift = tsThirdShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblThirdDeductedMinutes)))
                                End If
                            End If
                            If dblSecondDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                dblSecondDeductedMinutes = (dblSecondDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                If tsSecondShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblSecondDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                    tsSecondShift = tsSecondShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblSecondDeductedMinutes)))
                                End If
                            End If
                            If dblFirstDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                dblFirstDeductedMinutes = (dblFirstDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                If tsFirstShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblFirstDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                    tsFirstShift = tsFirstShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblFirstDeductedMinutes)))
                                End If
                            End If

                            dt2(j)("FirstShift") = tsFirstShift.ToString
                            dt2(j)("SecondShift") = tsSecondShift.ToString
                            dt2(j)("ThirdShift") = tsThirdShift.ToString

                            'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U 
                            '===========================================
                            dblTotalShiftDeduction = obj.ConvertTimeToHours((tsFirstShift + tsSecondShift + tsThirdShift).ToString())
                            tsTotalShiftDeduction = tsFirstShift + tsSecondShift + tsThirdShift
                            dblTotalShiftDeduction = Math.Round(dblTotalShiftDeduction, 2)

                            'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                            '===========================================
                            dt2(j)("TotalShift") = tsTotalShiftDeduction.ToString()


                            'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                            '===========================================
                            dt2(j)("DeductionShift") = (tsFirstShift.TotalHours + tsThirdShift.TotalHours).ToString()

                            'Closed by Kashan on 27/04/2015 due to OT Calculation revised as per instruction by Shahzaib
                            'dblTemp = (((dblTotalHours)) - dblTotalShiftDeduction) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)
                            'dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                            dblTemp = dblTotalHours
                            '---------------------------------


                            If dblTemp < 0 Then
                                'dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1) change by Ammar for Overtime set as Zero in case of Negative
                                dblTemp = 0
                            Else
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                            End If
                            dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                        Else

                            dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)

                            'Closed by Kashan on 27/04/2015 due to OT Calculation revised as per instruction by Shahzaib
                            'dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                            '---------------------------------

                            If dblTemp < 0 Then
                                'dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1) change by Ammar for Overtime set as Zero in case of Negative
                                dblTemp = 0
                            Else
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                            End If
                            dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                        End If
                    Else

                        dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)

                        'Closed by Kashan on 27/04/2015 due to OT Calculation revised as per instruction by Shahzaib
                        'dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                        '---------------------------------

                        If dblTemp < 0 Then
                            'dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1) change by Ammar for Overtime set as Zero in case of Negative
                            dblTemp = 0
                        Else
                            dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                        End If
                        dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                    End If
                Else

                    dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)

                    If dblTemp * 60 < 30 Then
                        dt2(j)("OT") = "00:00:00"
                    Else
                        If Math.Round(dblTemp, 2) >= 5.75 Then
                            'By Umair
                            'dblTemp = dblTemp - 0.75
                            dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                        Else
                            dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                        End If
                        dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)
                    End If
                End If

                'closed by kashan on 07-05-2015 as per instruct by shahzaib no need of it.
                'Else

                'dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)

                ''Closed by Kashan on 27/04/2015 due to OT Calculation revised as per instruction by Shahzaib
                ''dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                ''---------------------------------

                'If dblTemp < 0 Then
                '    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                'Else
                '    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                'End If
                'dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                'End If
                '--------------------------------------------------------------------------


                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                '==================================================================================
                dt2(j)("NetExcess") = tsExcessTime - (tsLateTime + tsShortTime)


                dt2(j)("TotalShift") = dt2(j)("TotalShift").ToString().Substring(0, 5)

                tsLate = New TimeSpan(strLateTime.Substring(0, 2), strLateTime.Substring(3, 2), "00")

                tsTotalOT = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("OT")) = True, "00:00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("OT"))).ToString())
                dt2(j)("OT") = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("OT")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("OT"))).ToString()).ToString.Substring(0, 5)

                tsTotalGrossOT = tsTotalGrossOT.Add(tsTotalOT)
                dt2(j)("TotalOverTime1") = obj.GetFormattedTimeSpanValue(tsTotalGrossOT)
                tsOutOfCompany = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalShift")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalShift"))).ToString())
                tsTotalOutOfCompany = tsTotalOutOfCompany.Add(tsOutOfCompany)

                dt2(j)("TotalExtraTime") = obj.convertTotime(dblTotalHours)
                dt2(j)("TotalLateTime") = obj.GetFormattedTimeSpanValue(tsTotalLate)
                dt2(j)("TotalOutOfCompany") = obj.GetFormattedTimeSpanValue(tsTotalOutOfCompany)
                dt2.Rows(j)("TotalEarlyTime") = obj.GetFormattedTimeSpanValue(tsTotalEarly)

                tsLateEarlyTime1 = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalLateTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalLateTime"))).ToString())
                tsLateEarlyTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalEarlyTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalEarlyTime"))).ToString())
                dt2(j)("TotalLateAndEarly") = tsLateEarlyTime.Add(tsLateEarlyTime1)

            Next

            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)

            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptLaborOverTimeReport_Customized.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew.Tables(0))

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strVersion", "SAMCO")

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetLaborOverTimeReport_Customized_Old() As Boolean

        GetLaborOverTimeReport_Customized_Old = False

            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")
            Dim ds As DataSet

                'If IsIncludeAutoLeaveDeductionColumn = True Then

                ShiftID = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ShiftID")) = True, 0, dt2.Rows(j)("ShiftID"))
                schdInDate = CDate(dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleDate"))

                    dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime") = dt2.Rows(j)("LateTimeBetweenGrace")
                End If

                strLateTime = dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")
                Dim intHr As Integer, intMin As Integer, intSec As Integer = 0
                Dim strLatearr() As String = strLateTime.Split(":")
                intHr = strLatearr(0)
                intMin = strLatearr(1)

                strLateTime = String.Format("{0:00}", intHr) & ":" & String.Format("{0:00}", intMin) & ":" & String.Format("{0:00}", intSec)

                dblLateTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")))
                dblShortTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime")))
                dblExcessTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")))
                dblStartingTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")))
                dblInTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")))
                dblOutTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")))

                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                '==================================================================================
                tsLateTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("LateTime"))).ToString())
                tsEarlyTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("EarlyTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("EarlyTime"))).ToString())
                tsShortTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("ShortTime"))).ToString())
                tsExcessTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("ExcessTime"))).ToString())
                tsStartingTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("StartingTime")).ToString())
                tsInTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("InTime")).ToString())
                tsOutTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")) = True, "00:00", dt2.Rows(j)("OutTime")).ToString())

                tsTotalLate = tsTotalLate.Add(tsLate)
                strSpendTime = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("SpendTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("SpendTime")))


                strScheduleTime = IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("ScheduleTime")))
                dblTotalHours = ConvertTimeToHours_New((TimeSpan.Parse(strSpendTime) - TimeSpan.Parse(strScheduleTime)).ToString())

                If TimeSpan.Parse(strSpendTime) < TimeSpan.Parse(strScheduleTime) Then
                    dblTotalHours = dblTotalHours * -1
                End If

                dblTotalHours = Math.Round(dblTotalHours, 2)

                'Difference Change from double to TimeSpane Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                '==================================================================================
                If tsInTime < tsStartingTime Then
                    tsBeforeTime = tsStartingTime - tsInTime
                    dblBeforeTime = obj.ConvertTimeToHours(tsBeforeTime.ToString)
                Else
                    dblBeforeTime = 0
                End If

                If dblInTime = 0 And dblOutTime = 0 Then
                    dblTotalHours = 0
                    dblBeforeTime = 0
                End If
                dblBeforeTime = Math.Round(dblBeforeTime, 2)

                If Not dtRule.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                    If ShiftID > 0 Then
                        Dim dtShiftTrack As DataTable = boShift.tblShiftTrackDetail.GetDetailCompanyShift(ShiftID, CompanyID)
                        If dtShiftTrack.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                            Dim tsFirstShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsSecondShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsThirdShift As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsFirstStart As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsFirstEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim dblFirstDeductedMinutes As Double = 0

                            Dim tsSecondEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim dblSecondDeductedMinutes As Double = 0
                            Dim tsThirdStart As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim tsThirdEnd As TimeSpan = New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                            Dim dblThirdDeductedMinutes As Double = 0

                            For iShiftTrack As Integer = 1 To dtShiftTrack.Rows.Count
                                If iShiftTrack = 1 Then
                                    tsFirstStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                    tsFirstEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                    dblFirstDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                                ElseIf iShiftTrack = 2 Then
                                    tsSecondStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                    tsSecondEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                    dblSecondDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                                ElseIf iShiftTrack = 3 Then
                                    tsThirdStart = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("FromTime")))
                                    tsThirdEnd = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")) = True, "00:00:00", dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("ToTime")))
                                    dblThirdDeductedMinutes = IIf(IsDBNull(dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes")) = True, 0, dtShiftTrack.Rows(iShiftTrack - 1)("DeductedMinutes"))
                                End If
                            Next

                            Dim dtTimelyShift As DataTable = BOTransactionDetail.tblTransactionDetail.GetDatabyActivityDateAndEmployeeID(schdInDate, intEmployeeID)
                            If dtTimelyShift.Rows.Count > 2 Then

                                Dim tsTemporary1 As TimeSpan
                                Dim tsTemporary2 As TimeSpan

                                Dim rowCount As Integer = dtTimelyShift.Rows.Count
                                Dim bolDiff As Boolean = False, intShift As Integer = 0
                                For k As Integer = 1 To rowCount - 2

                                    Try
                                        tsTemporary1 = TimeSpan.Parse(dtTimelyShift.Rows(k)("ActivityTime").ToString)
                                    Catch ex As Exception
                                        Continue For
                                    End Try

                                    If (tsTemporary1 >= tsFirstStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsFirstEnd) Then
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                            bolDiff = True
                                            If tsTemporary2 = tsFirstEnd Then
                                                intShift = 2
                                            Else
                                                intShift = 1
                                            End If
                                            If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 1 Then
                                                tsFirstShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                            Else
                                                If intShift = 2 Then
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsThirdStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 3

                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        End If

                                    ElseIf (tsTemporary1 >= tsSecondStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsSecondEnd) Then
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                            bolDiff = True
                                            If tsTemporary2 = tsSecondEnd Then
                                                intShift = 3
                                            Else
                                                intShift = 2
                                            End If
                                            If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 2 Then
                                                tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                            Else
                                                If intShift = 1 Then
                                                    tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 2

                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        End If

                                    ElseIf (tsTemporary1 >= tsThirdStart And tsTemporary1 <= tsThirdEnd) Then
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                            bolDiff = True
                                            intShift = 3
                                            If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 3 Then
                                                tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                            Else
                                                If intShift = 1 Then
                                                    tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 2

                                                ElseIf intShift = 2 Then
                                                    tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                    tsThirdShift += tsTemporary1.Subtract(tsThirdStart)
                                                    tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsTemporary1.Ticks)
                                                    bolDiff = True
                                                    intShift = 3

                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        If bolDiff = False Then
                                            If tsTemporary1 < tsFirstStart Then
                                                tsTemporary2 = New TimeSpan(tsFirstStart.Ticks)
                                                bolDiff = True
                                                intShift = 1
                                                If k = rowCount - 2 Then
                                                    GoTo ShiftTrackPrinciple
                                                End If
                                            End If
                                        Else
ShiftTrackPrinciple:
                                            bolDiff = False
                                            If intShift = 1 Then
                                                tsFirstShift += tsFirstEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsSecondStart)
                                                tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsThirdStart)

                                            ElseIf intShift = 2 Then
                                                tsSecondShift += tsSecondEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)
                                                tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsThirdStart)

                                            ElseIf intShift = 3 Then
                                                tsThirdShift += tsThirdEnd.Subtract(tsTemporary2)

                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                Next

                                If dblThirdDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                    dblThirdDeductedMinutes = (dblThirdDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                    If tsThirdShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblThirdDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                        tsThirdShift = tsThirdShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblThirdDeductedMinutes)))
                                    End If
                                End If
                                If dblSecondDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                    dblSecondDeductedMinutes = (dblSecondDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                    If tsSecondShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblSecondDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                        tsSecondShift = tsSecondShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblSecondDeductedMinutes)))
                                    End If
                                End If
                                If dblFirstDeductedMinutes > 0 Then
                                    dblFirstDeductedMinutes = (dblFirstDeductedMinutes / 60)
                                    If tsFirstShift >= TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblFirstDeductedMinutes)) Then
                                        tsFirstShift = tsFirstShift.Subtract(TimeSpan.Parse(obj.convertTotime(dblFirstDeductedMinutes)))
                                    End If
                                End If

                                dt2(j)("FirstShift") = tsFirstShift.ToString
                                dt2(j)("SecondShift") = tsSecondShift.ToString
                                dt2(j)("ThirdShift") = tsThirdShift.ToString

                                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U 
                                '===========================================
                                dblTotalShiftDeduction = obj.ConvertTimeToHours((tsFirstShift + tsSecondShift + tsThirdShift).ToString())
                                tsTotalShiftDeduction = tsFirstShift + tsSecondShift + tsThirdShift
                                dblTotalShiftDeduction = Math.Round(dblTotalShiftDeduction, 2)

                                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                                '===========================================
                                dt2(j)("TotalShift") = tsTotalShiftDeduction.ToString()


                                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                                '===========================================
                                dt2(j)("DeductionShift") = (tsFirstShift.TotalHours + tsThirdShift.TotalHours).ToString()

                                'Closed by Kashan on 27/04/2015 due to OT Calculation revised as per instruction by Shahzaib
                                'dblTemp = (((dblTotalHours)) - dblTotalShiftDeduction) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)
                                'dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                                dblTemp = dblTotalHours
                                '---------------------------------


                                If dblTemp < 0 Then
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                                Else
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                                End If
                                dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                            Else

                                dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)

                                'Closed by Kashan on 27/04/2015 due to OT Calculation revised as per instruction by Shahzaib
                                'dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                                '---------------------------------

                                If dblTemp < 0 Then
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                                Else
                                    dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                                End If
                                dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                            End If
                        Else

                            dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)

                            'Closed by Kashan on 27/04/2015 due to OT Calculation revised as per instruction by Shahzaib
                            'dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                            '---------------------------------

                            If dblTemp < 0 Then
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                            Else
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                            End If
                            dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                        End If
                    Else

                        dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)

                        If dblTemp * 60 < 30 Then
                            dt2(j)("OT") = "00:00:00"
                        Else
                            If Math.Round(dblTemp, 2) >= 5.75 Then
                                dblTemp = dblTemp - 0.75
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                            Else
                                dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                            End If
                            dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)
                        End If
                    End If
                Else

                    dblTemp = ((dblTotalHours)) 'Shahzaib Changes(04-12-2014)

                    'Closed by Kashan on 27/04/2015 due to OT Calculation revised as per instruction by Shahzaib
                    'dblTemp = dblTemp - dblBeforeTime
                    '---------------------------------

                    If dblTemp < 0 Then
                        dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp * (-1)) * (-1)
                    Else
                        dblTemp = obj.GetOverTimeRoundedHours(dblTemp)
                    End If
                    dt2(j)("OT") = obj.convertTotime(dblTemp)

                End If

                'Change from double to TimeSpane  By K.U
                '==================================================================================
                dt2(j)("NetExcess") = tsExcessTime - (tsLateTime + tsShortTime)


                tsOutOfCompany = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalShift")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalShift"))).ToString())
                tsTotalOutOfCompany = tsTotalOutOfCompany.Add(tsOutOfCompany)

                dt2(j)("TotalExtraTime") = obj.convertTotime(dblTotalHours)
                dt2(j)("TotalLateTime") = obj.GetFormattedTimeSpanValue(tsTotalLate)
                dt2(j)("TotalOutOfCompany") = obj.GetFormattedTimeSpanValue(tsTotalOutOfCompany)

                tsLateEarlyTime1 = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalLateTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalLateTime"))).ToString())
                tsLateEarlyTime = TimeSpan.Parse(IIf(IsDBNull(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalEarlyTime")) = True, "00:00", GetTimeSpanceByValue(dt2.Rows(j)("TotalEarlyTime"))).ToString())

                dt2(j)("TotalLateAndEarly") = tsLateEarlyTime.Add(tsLateEarlyTime1)
            Next

            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt2.DefaultView.ToTable)

            If IsNothing(dsNew.Tables(0)) = False AndAlso dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptLaborOverTimeReport_Customized.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew.Tables(0))

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCompName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria1", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strCriteria2", "")
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strClientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("strVersion", "SAMCO")

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetAttendanceTrendAnalysis() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceTrendAnalysis = False
        Try
            Dim intTotDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)  '0 Based
            If FromDateServer.Date.Month = ToDateServer.Date.Month Then
                If intTotDays > 30 Then
                    Dim strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_FromAndToDateDiffMsg")
                    Throw New Exception(IIf(strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg = "", "Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 31 days", strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg))
                End If
            Else
                Throw New Exception("Month not matched")
            End If

            Dim dtAttendanceDept As New dsAttendance.AttendanceSummaryDeptDataTable
            Dim boFinalSchedule As New BO_FinalSchedule
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As New DataTable

            dtAttendanceDept.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendanceDept)
            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            dtData = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetEmployeeInfo(strEmployees, CompanyID)
            dtData = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable(True, New String() { _
                    "BranchID", "BranchTitle", "BranchRefNo", "DepartmentID", "DepartmentRefNo", "DepartmentTitle", "DivisionID", "DivisionRefNo", "DivisionTitle"})

            Dim boShift As New BO_Shift
            Dim datTemp As Date = FromDateServer.Date
            Dim datToTemp As Date = ToDateServer.Date
            Dim dtShift As DataTable = boShift.tblShift.GetDatabyCompany(CompanyID)
            Dim strFilter As String = "", ds As DataSet
            Dim intTotal As Integer = dtData.Rows.Count

            ds = ReadXMLFromHidden()
            strFilter = " ShiftID IN " & ShiftFilter(ds)
            If Right(strFilter, 4) <> "(-1)" Then
                dtShift.DefaultView.RowFilter = strFilter
                dtShift = dtShift.DefaultView.ToTable()
            Else
                Throw New Exception("Atleast One Shift Must Be Select.")
            End If

            Dim dtAttend As New DataTable("AttendanceDetail")
            Dim DCol1 = New DataColumn("EmployeeID", GetType(System.Int64))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol1)
            Dim DCol2 = New DataColumn("BranchID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol2)
            Dim DCol3 = New DataColumn("DepartmentID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol3)
            Dim DCol4 = New DataColumn("ShiftID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol4)
            Dim DCol5 = New DataColumn("ScheduleDate", GetType(System.DateTime))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol5)
            Dim DCol6 = New DataColumn("DivisionID", GetType(System.Int32))
            dtAttend.Columns.Add(DCol6)
            Dim PKeysA() As DataColumn = {DCol1, DCol2, DCol3, DCol4, DCol5, DCol6}
            dtAttend.PrimaryKey = PKeysA

            dtAttend.Merge(ReportHelper.GetAttendanceByDateDepartment(strEmployees, datTemp, datToTemp, CompanyID, strRptCriteria1, strRptFromCriteria1, strRptToCriteria1))

            Dim dtLeave As New DataTable
            dtLeave = ReportHelper.GetLeaveByDateDepartment(strEmployees, datTemp, datToTemp, CompanyID, strRptCriteria1, strRptFromCriteria1, strRptToCriteria1)

            dtAttend.Merge(dtLeave)
            If Right(strFilter, 4) <> "(-1)" Then
                dtAttend.DefaultView.RowFilter = strFilter & " OR ShiftID = 0"
                dtAttend = dtAttend.DefaultView.ToTable
            End If

            Dim dtStrength As DataTable

            ' Changes By Kashan On 03/12/2009
            If strRptCriteria1.ToLower = "employee" Then
                dtStrength = ReportHelper.GetDepartmentStrength(CompanyID)
            ElseIf strRptCriteria1.ToLower <> "all" Then
                dtStrength = ReportHelper.GetDepartmentStrength_New(CompanyID, strRptCriteria1, strRptFromCriteria1, strRptToCriteria1, strEmployees)
            Else
                dtStrength = ReportHelper.GetDepartmentStrength(CompanyID)
            End If

            For j As Integer = 0 To dtAttend.Rows.Count - 1
                Dim drDestRows As DataRow = dtAttend.Rows(j)
                Dim schdInDate As Date = CDate(drDestRows("ScheduleDate"))
                Dim schdOutDate As Date
                If IsDBNull(drDestRows("ScheduleOutDate")) = True Then
                    schdOutDate = CDate(drDestRows("ScheduleDate"))
                Else
                    schdOutDate = CDate(drDestRows("ScheduleOutDate"))
                End If

                Dim ShiftID As Int32 = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("ShiftID")) = True, 0, drDestRows("ShiftID"))
                Dim GraceTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("GraceTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("GraceTime").ToString)
                Dim strStartingTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("StartingTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("StartingTime"))
                Dim strEndingTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("EndingTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("EndingTime"))

                Dim strAttendanceStatus As String
                Dim tsGraceTime As TimeSpan
                Dim tsDiff As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsTemp As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsSchdTimeSpan As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsTimeSpent As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                Dim tsLateTime As New TimeSpan(0, 0, 0)
                If GraceTime.Length = 8 Then
                    tsGraceTime = New TimeSpan(GraceTime.Substring(0, 2), GraceTime.Substring(3, 2), GraceTime.Substring(6, 2))
                End If

                strAttendanceStatus = drDestRows("AttendanceStatus").ToString
                If strAttendanceStatus = "P" Then
                    Dim dtTranInDate As Date = CDate(drDestRows("InDate"))
                    Dim dtTranOutDate As Date = CDate(drDestRows("OutDate"))

                    Dim strInTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("InTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("InTime"))
                    Dim strOutTime As String = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("OutTime")) = True, "00:00:00", drDestRows("OutTime"))

                    Dim InTimeIsValid As Boolean = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("InTimeIsValid")) = True, False, drDestRows("InTimeIsValid"))
                    Dim OutTimeIsValid As Boolean = IIf(IsDBNull(drDestRows("OutTimeIsValid")) = True, False, drDestRows("OutTimeIsValid"))

                    'TS - Late Time
                    '-------------------------------------------------------
                    If InTimeIsValid = True Then
                        tsDiff = ReportHelper.GetTimeBetween(schdInDate, strStartingTime, dtTranInDate, strInTime)
                        If tsDiff.CompareTo(tsGraceTime) > 0 And ShiftID > 0 Then
                            dtAttend.Rows(j).Item("IsLate") = 1
                        End If
                    End If

                End If
            Next

            Dim dtdistinctDate As New DataTable
            dtdistinctDate = dtAttend.DefaultView.ToTable(True, "ScheduleDate")

            Dim dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis As DataTable
            dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis = New dsAttendance.AttendanceTrendAnalysisDataTable
            For i As Integer = 0 To dtdistinctDate.Rows.Count - 1

                Dim dr As DataRow
                dr = dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis.NewRow
                Dim Scheduledate As String = dtdistinctDate.Rows(i)("ScheduleDate")

                Dim intStrengthSchedule As Integer
                Dim intPresent As Integer
                Dim intOnLeave As Integer
                Dim intAbsent As Integer
                Dim intStrength As Integer
                Dim intShiftID As Integer, strShiftTitle As String
                Dim intLate As Integer

                intShiftID = dtShift.Rows(0).Item("ShiftID")
                strShiftTitle = dtShift.Rows(0).Item("ShiftTitle")

                intStrength = dtStrength.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "")

                If chkOffDay.Visible = True AndAlso chkOffDay.Checked = False Then
                    intStrengthSchedule = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "ScheduleDate ='" & Scheduledate & "' And AttendanceStatus<>'H'")
                ElseIf chkOffDay.Visible = True AndAlso chkOffDay.Checked = True Then
                    intStrengthSchedule = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "ScheduleDate ='" & Scheduledate & "'")
                End If

                intPresent = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "ScheduleDate ='" & Scheduledate & "' And AttendanceStatus='P'")
                intOnLeave = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "ScheduleDate ='" & Scheduledate & "' And AttendanceStatus='L'")
                intAbsent = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "ScheduleDate ='" & Scheduledate & "' And AttendanceStatus='A'")
                intLate = dtAttend.Compute("Count(EmployeeID)", "ScheduleDate ='" & Scheduledate & "' And IsLate=1")
                dr("ScheduleDate") = Scheduledate
                dr("Strenght") = intStrengthSchedule
                dr("Present") = intPresent
                dr("Absent") = intAbsent
                dr("Leave") = intOnLeave
                dr("Late") = intLate
                dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis.Rows.Add(dr)
            Next

            dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis.DefaultView.Sort = "ScheduleDate ASC"
            'dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis.DefaultView.RowFilter = "Strenght > 0"

            Dim dtTemp As New DataTable
            dtTemp = GenerateColumnToRowTable(dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis.DefaultView.ToTable)
            If dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis.DefaultView.Count > 0 Then
                For i As Integer = 0 To dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis.Rows.Count - 1
                    dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis.Rows(i)("ScheduleDate") = Format(CDate(dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis.Rows(i)("ScheduleDate").ToString), "dd MMM")
                Next
                SetReportPath("rptAttendanceTrendAnalysis_1.rpt")
                rptDoc.Database.Tables("AttendanceTrendAnalysis").SetDataSource(dtAttendanceTrendAnalysis.DefaultView.ToTable())
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", "Attendance Trend - " & Format(datTemp, "MMMM yyyy"))

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True
            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    'Private Sub MakeAttendanceAnalysisChart(ByVal pdt As DataTable)
