    'End Sub

    Private Function GetEmployeeAttendanceSummary_Stylo() As Boolean
        Try

            GetEmployeeAttendanceSummary_Stylo = False

            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee, EmpHelper As New EmployeeHelper
            Dim dtAttendance As New DataTable


            Dim SR As SortedList = GetFromAndToDate(ddlyear.SelectedValue, ddlMonth.SelectedValue)

            FromDateServer = SR("FromDate").ToString
            ToDateServer = SR("ToDate").ToString

            Dim intTotalDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)
            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dtData As New DataTable, dtPivot As New DataTable
            Dim dtLeave As New DataTable, boLeave As New BO_Leave


            dtAttendance.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendance)

            Dim dtFrom As Date = FromDateServer
            Dim dtTo As Date = ToDateServer

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")

            Dim ds As New DataSet

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSummary_Customized(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)
                If dtData.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dtData = ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                Else
                    dtData.Merge(ds.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                End If

                If dtAttendance.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dtAttendance = ReportHelper.GetApprovedLeaveDetail(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer)
                Else
                    dtAttendance.Merge(ReportHelper.GetApprovedLeaveDetail(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer))
                End If
            Next

            Dim dsTemp As New DataSet
            Dim dc As DataColumn, dtTemp As New DataTable

            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                dtTemp = dtData.DefaultView.ToTable(True, "EmployeeID", "EmployeeRefNo")

                If dtAttendance.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                    dtLeave = dtAttendance.DefaultView.ToTable(True, "LeaveID", "LeaveTitle")
                    dtPivot = GetPivotDataTable(dtAttendance, "LeaveTitle", "EmployeeID", "LeaveValue", "0", False, False, True)

                    dtData.TableName = "tblDataTable"
                    dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtTemp)
                    dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtLeave)
                    dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtPivot)
                    dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtData)

                    dsTemp.Relations.Add("MstRelation", dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables(2).Columns("EmployeeID"), False)

                    For Each dr As DataRow In dtLeave.Rows
                        Dim ColName As String = "[" & dr("LeaveTitle") & "]"
                        Dim ColName1 As String = dr("LeaveTitle")
                        dc = New DataColumn(ColName1, GetType(System.Double), "(IsNull(Max(Child(MstRelation)." & ColName & "),0))")
                        dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)
                    Next

                    dsTemp.Relations.Add("MstRelation1", dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables(3).Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                    dc = New DataColumn("Absent", GetType(System.Double), "(IsNull(Max(Child(MstRelation1).TotalAbsentWithFlag),0))")
                    dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                    dc = New DataColumn("Late Minutes", GetType(System.Double), "(IsNull(Max(Child(MstRelation1).TotalLateFlagMin),0))")
                    dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                    dc = New DataColumn("Total No Of Lates", GetType(System.Double), "(IsNull(Max(Child(MstRelation1).TotalLateFlagWise),0))")
                    dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                    dc = New DataColumn("Deductable Late Counts", GetType(System.Double), "(IsNull(Max(Child(MstRelation1).DeductableLateCount),0))")
                    dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                    dc = New DataColumn("Part Timer hours", GetType(System.Double), "(IsNull(Max(Child(MstRelation1).SpentHours),0))")
                    dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                Else

                    dtData.TableName = "tblDataTable"
                    dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtTemp)
                    dsTemp.Tables.Add(dtData)

                    dsTemp.Relations.Add("MstRelation1", dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns("EmployeeID"), dsTemp.Tables(1).Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
                    dc = New DataColumn("Absent", GetType(System.Double), "(IsNull(Max(Child(MstRelation1).TotalAbsentWithFlag),0))")
                    dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                    dc = New DataColumn("Late Minutes", GetType(System.Double), "(IsNull(Max(Child(MstRelation1).TotalLateFlagMin),0))")
                    dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                    dc = New DataColumn("Total No Of Lates", GetType(System.Double), "(IsNull(Max(Child(MstRelation1).TotalLateFlagWise),0))")
                    dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                    dc = New DataColumn("Deductable Late Counts", GetType(System.Double), "(IsNull(Max(Child(MstRelation1).DeductableLateCount),0))")
                    dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                    dc = New DataColumn("Part Timer hours", GetType(System.Double), "(IsNull(Max(Child(MstRelation1).SpentHours),0))")
                    dsTemp.Tables(0).Columns.Add(dc)

                End If

                dsTemp.Relations.Clear()
                dsTemp.Tables.Remove(dtData)

                dtData = dsTemp.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable
                dtData.Columns.Remove("EmployeeID")
                dtData.Columns("EmployeeRefNo").ColumnName = "EmployeeID"
                dtData.AcceptChanges()

            End If

            If dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                hypDownloadTamplet.Visible = True
                hypDownloadTamplet.Text = "Monthly Summary"
                CreateExcelFileForUpload(dtData, "MonthlySummary")
            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try



    End Function
    Private Function GetAttendanceActivityWithIP() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceActivityWithIP = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendanceActivity As New dsAttendance.AttendanceActivityDataTable

            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee
            Dim dtData As New DataTable

            dtAttendanceActivity.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtAttendanceActivity)

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            Dim empCodeLength As Integer = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetRefNoLength(CompanyID)

            'Get Data
            dtData = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceActivityUsingActivityID(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)
            For i As Integer = 0 To dtData.Rows.Count - 1

                Dim dr As dsAttendance.AttendanceActivityRow = dtAttendanceActivity.NewAttendanceActivityRow
                Dim datInTime As System.DateTime, datOutTime As System.DateTime, ts As TimeSpan

                dr.EmployeeID = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeID").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeRefNo = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeRefNo").ToString()
                dr.EmployeeName = dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeName").ToString()

                dr.DepartmentID = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentID").ToString()
                dr.DepartmentTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DepartmentTitle").ToString()
                dr.DesignationID = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationID").ToString()
                dr.DesignationTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("DesignationTitle").ToString()
                dr.WorkCode = dtData.Rows(i)("WorkCode").ToString()
                dr.BranchTitle = dtData.Rows(i)("BranchTitle").ToString()
                dr.ActivityDate = CDate(dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityDate"))
                dr.InTime = dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityTime").ToString()
                dr.InDateTime = dtData.Rows(i)("InDateTime").ToString()
                dr.OutDateTime = dtData.Rows(i)("OutDateTime").ToString()
                dr.ScheduleInDate = dtData.Rows(i)("ScheduleInDate").ToString()

                Try

                    If dtData.Rows(i)("EmployeeID") = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("EmployeeID") Then
                        If dtData.Rows(i)("ScheduleInDate").ToString = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("ScheduleInDate").ToString() _
                        And dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityID").ToString = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("ActivityID").ToString() Then
                            dr.OutTime = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("ActivityTime").ToString()
                            datInTime = dtData.Rows(i)("ActivityDateTime")
                            datOutTime = dtData.Rows(i + 1)("ActivityDateTime")
                            ts = datOutTime.Subtract(datInTime)
                            If ts.TotalHours > 0 Then
                                dr.TimeSpent = ReportHelper.convertTotime(ts.TotalHours)
                            Else
                                dr.TimeSpent = "00:00:00"
                            End If
                            i = i + 1
                        Else
                            dr.OutTime = "00:00:00"
                            dr.TimeSpent = "00:00:00"
                        End If
                    Else
                        dr.OutTime = "00:00:00"
                        dr.TimeSpent = "00:00:00"
                    End If

                Catch ex As Exception
                    dr.OutTime = "00:00:00"
                    dr.TimeSpent = "00:00:00"
                End Try
                dtAttendanceActivity.Rows.Add(dr)
            Next


            If dtAttendanceActivity.Rows.Count > 0 Then

                If CInt(hdRptType.Value) = RptType.AttendanceActivity_Stylo Then
                    SetReportPath("rptAttendanceActivity_Stylo.rpt")
                Else
                    SetReportPath("rptAttendanceActivity_New.rpt")
                End If

                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtAttendanceActivity.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("DateTitle", "Dates From : " & strFromDateClient & _
                    " To " & strToDateClient)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportCriteria", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Function GetContinuousAbsents() As Boolean

        GetContinuousAbsents = False

        Try

            Dim strServerDateFormat As String = ConfigurationManager.AppSettings("ServerDateFormat")
            Dim intTotDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)  '0 Based
            Dim AbsentDays As Integer = 10, dt3 As New DataTable

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            Dim dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable, ArrEmployeeID() As String = strEmployees.Split(",")
            AbsentDays = txtValue1.Text

            dt2 = ReportHelper.GetContinuousAbsentsByDate(strEmployees, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)

            'Dim ds As New DataSet
            For i As Integer = 0 To ArrEmployeeID.Length - 1

                Dim isRule As Boolean = False
                Dim intAbs As Integer = 0
                Dim TotalAbsCount As Double = 0

                dt2.DefaultView.RowFilter = "EmployeeID = " & ArrEmployeeID(i) & " "
                dt3 = dt2.DefaultView.ToTable

                If IsDBNull(dt3.Compute("Count(AttendanceStatus)", "EmployeeID = " & ArrEmployeeID(i) & " And AttendanceStatus = 'A'")) = False Then
                    TotalAbsCount = dt3.Compute("Count(AttendanceStatus)", "EmployeeID = " & ArrEmployeeID(i) & " And AttendanceStatus = 'A'")
                Else
                    TotalAbsCount = 0
                End If

                If TotalAbsCount >= AbsentDays Then
                    TotalAbsCount = 0
                    For Each dr As DataRow In dt3.Rows
                        If dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.ToLower = "a" Or (dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.ToLower = "h" And intAbs >= 1) Then
                            intAbs += 1
                            TotalAbsCount += 1
                        Else
                            If isRule = True And intAbs >= AbsentDays Then GoTo NextEmployee
                            intAbs = 0
                            TotalAbsCount = 0
                        End If

                        If intAbs >= AbsentDays Then
                            isRule = True
                            dt3.Rows(dt3.Rows.IndexOf(dr)).Item("TotalAbsent") = TotalAbsCount
                            dt3.Rows(dt3.Rows.IndexOf(dr)).Item("FromDate") = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, (intAbs * -1), dr("ScheduleDate"))
                            dt3.Rows(dt3.Rows.IndexOf(dr)).Item("FromDate") = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, dt3.Rows(dt3.Rows.IndexOf(dr)).Item("FromDate"))
                            dt3.Rows(dt3.Rows.IndexOf(dr)).Item("ToDate") = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, intAbs, dt3.Rows(dt3.Rows.IndexOf(dr)).Item("FromDate"))

                            If dr("AttendanceStatus").ToString.ToLower = "h" Then
                                dt3.Rows(dt3.Rows.IndexOf(dr)).Item("AttendanceStatus") = "A"
                            End If
                            dt3.AcceptChanges()
                        End If
                    Next
                End If
NextEmployee:
                If isRule = True Then
                    If dt1.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                        dt1 = dt3
                    Else
                        dt1.Merge(dt3)
                    End If
                End If

                isRule = False
                TotalAbsCount = 0
                dt3 = Nothing
            Next


            Dim dsNew As New DataSet
            dsNew.Tables.Add(dt1)

            If dsNew.Tables(0).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("rptContinuousAbsentees.rpt")

                'Report Params
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dsNew.Tables(0).DefaultView.ToTable)
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("DateTitle", "Dates From : " & FromDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat) & _
                                " To " & ToDateServer.ToString(clsCookie.DateFormat))
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", strReportTitle)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try


    End Function
    Private Function GetAttendanceRegister_Stylo() As Boolean

        GetAttendanceRegister_Stylo = False
        Try
            Dim dtAttendance As New dsAttendance.ShiftScheduleRegDataTable
            Dim strServerDateFormat As String = ConfigurationManager.AppSettings("ServerDateFormat")

            Dim intTotDays As Integer = DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer)  '0 Based

            If intTotDays > 31 Then
                Dim strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_FromAndToDateDiffMsg")
                Throw New Exception(IIf(strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg = "", "Difference between From and To date must be less than or equal to 31 days", strFromAndToDateDiffMsgg))
            End If

            'Create Employee List
            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            Dim dtList As New DataTable, dt1 As New DataTable, dt2 As New DataTable, DataFullAtteandance As New DataSet
            dtList = EmpHelper.GetEmployeesListByType(strEmployees, CompanyID, "Department")
            Dim ds As New DataSet

            For i As Integer = 0 To dtList.Rows.Count - 1
                dt1 = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceRegisterMusterRoll(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)
                DataFullAtteandance = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceFull(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), blnIsLateEarlyAccountEnabled, FromDateServer, ToDateServer, _
                        CompanyID, boLeaveDeduction, boCompany, boShift, boovetimeSetup, boAttendRule, boLeaveRule, boFlag)

                ds = ReportHelper.GetAttendanceSummary_Customized(dtList.Rows(i)("Employees"), FromDateServer, ToDateServer, CompanyID)

                If dt2.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    dt2 = dt1
                Else
                    dt2.Merge(dt1)
                End If
            Next

            DataFullAtteandance.Tables.Add(dt2)
            DataFullAtteandance.Relations.Add("Main_Detail", DataFullAtteandance.Tables(0).Columns("EmployeeID"), DataFullAtteandance.Tables(2).Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
            DataFullAtteandance.Tables(2).Columns.Add("TotalPresent", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Parent(Main_Detail).TotalPresent,0)")
            DataFullAtteandance.Tables(2).Columns.Add("TotalLeave", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Parent(Main_Detail).TotalLeave,0)")
            DataFullAtteandance.Tables(2).Columns.Add("TotalLateMinutes", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Parent(Main_Detail).TotalLateMinutes,0)")

            Dim strShiftTitle As String = "", strStartingTime As String = "", strEndingTime As String = ""

            If DataFullAtteandance.Tables(2).Rows.Count > 0 Then
                If ddlShift.SelectedValue > 0 Then
                    dt2.DefaultView.RowFilter = "ShiftID = " & ddlShift.SelectedValue & ""
                    dt2 = dt2.DefaultView.ToTable
                    If dt2.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                        strShiftTitle = dt2.Compute("Max(ShiftTitle)", "ShiftID = " & ddlShift.SelectedValue & "")
                        strStartingTime = dt2.Compute("Max(StartingTime)", "ShiftID = " & ddlShift.SelectedValue & "")
                        strEndingTime = dt2.Compute("Max(EndingTime)", "ShiftID = " & ddlShift.SelectedValue & "")
                    End If
                Else
                    strShiftTitle = "All"
                End If
            End If

            Dim dt As New DataTable
            dt = ds.Tables(0)
            ds.Tables.RemoveAt(0)
            dt.TableName = "DtTemp"
            DataFullAtteandance.Tables.Add(dt)
            DataFullAtteandance.Relations.Add("MstDetail", DataFullAtteandance.Tables(2).Columns("EmployeeID"), DataFullAtteandance.Tables(3).Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
            DataFullAtteandance.Tables(2).Columns.Add("DeductableLateCount", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(MstDetail).DeductableLateCount),0)")

            If IsAttendanceFlag = True Then
                DataFullAtteandance.Tables(2).Columns.Add("TotalAbsent", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Max(Child(MstDetail).TotalAbsentWithFlag),0)")
            Else
                DataFullAtteandance.Tables(2).Columns.Add("TotalAbsent", GetType(System.Double), "IsNull(Parent(Main_Detail).TotalAbsent,0)")
            End If

            If DataFullAtteandance.Tables(2).Rows.Count > 0 Then

                SetReportPath("DailyAttendanceCustomized_Stylo.rpt")

                'Report Params
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(DataFullAtteandance.Tables(2).DefaultView.ToTable)
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptValue", rptValues)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptFromDate", txtFromDate.Text)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptToDate", txtToDate.Text)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("RptDates", MonthName(ToDateServer.Month) & " " & ToDateServer.Year)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportDays", DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, FromDateServer, ToDateServer) + 1)

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("rptTitle", "D A I L Y        A T T E N D A N C E       F R O M      " & txtFromDate.Text & "      T O    " & txtToDate.Text)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ShiftTitle", strShiftTitle)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("StartingTime", strStartingTime)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("EndingTime", strEndingTime)

                'Report Params
                Dim datTemp As Date = FromDateServer
                For i As Integer = 1 To 31

                    Try

                        Dim dayName1 As String = datTemp.DayOfWeek.ToString
                        If dayName1.ToLower = "sunday" Then   'Indicate Sundays
                            rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, datTemp)
                        Else
                            rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, datTemp)
                        End If

                    Catch ex As Exception  'Month does not have this Date
                        rptDoc.SetParameterValue("D" & i.ToString, " ")
                    End Try

                    datTemp = datTemp.AddDays(1)

                Next

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try
    End Function
    Private Function GetEmployeeSalaryStructureStatus() As Boolean
        GetEmployeeSalaryStructureStatus = False
        Try
            Dim dtEmployeeInfo As New dsMaster.EmployeeInformation_UpdateDataTable
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee, boSalaryStructure As New BO_SalaryStructureMapping
            Dim dtData As DataTable = Nothing
            Dim ds As New DataSet
            Dim dtSalary As New DataTable
            dtEmployeeInfo.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtEmployeeInfo)

            CreateEmployeeFilter()

            dtData = boEmployee.tblEmployee.GetEmployeeInfoForExcelFileCreate(CompanyID, strEmployees, 101)

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Dim dv As DataView = dtData.DefaultView
                dv.Sort = "EmployeeID"
                dtData = dv.ToTable
            End If
            dtSalary = boSalaryStructure.tblSalaryStructureMapping.CheckSalaryStructureStatus(CompanyID)
            ds.Tables.Add(dtData)
            ds.Tables.Add(dtSalary)
            ds.Relations.Add("MstDetail", ds.Tables(0).Columns("EmployeeID"), ds.Tables(1).Columns("EmployeeID"), False)
            ds.Tables(0).Columns.Add("Status", GetType(System.String), "IsNull(Max(Child(MstDetail).Status),0)")
            ds.Relations.Remove("MstDetail")

            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptMasterPayrollSummary.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "

                If strRptCriteria = "Employee" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.EmployeeCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Department" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.DepartmentCriteria()
                ElseIf strRptCriteria = "Branch" Then
                    rptValues = objCommonFunctions.BranchCriteria()
                End If

                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("clientDateFormat", strClientDateFormat)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function GetEmployeeLeaveEtitlize() As Boolean
        GetEmployeeLeaveEtitlize = False
        Try
            Dim dtEmployeeInfo As New dsMaster.EmployeeInformation_UpdateDataTable
            Dim boEmployee As New BO_Employee, boSalaryStructure As New BO_SalaryStructureMapping
            Dim dtData As DataTable = Nothing
            Dim ds As New DataSet
            Dim dtSalary As New DataTable
            dtEmployeeInfo.Clear()
            objCommonFunctions.SetTableForFunctions(dtEmployeeInfo)

            CreateEmployeeFilter()
            Dim SR As SortedList = GetFromAndToDate(ddlyear.SelectedValue, ddlMonth.SelectedValue)

            FromDateServer = SR("FromDate").ToString
            ToDateServer = SR("ToDate").ToString

            dtData = ReportHelper.GetEmployeeLeaveDeductionSummary(FromDateServer, ToDateServer, strEmployees, ddlMonth.SelectedValue, ddlyear.SelectedValue, CompanyID)
            dtData.TableName = "EmployeeLeaveEtilze"
            If IsNothing(dtData) = False AndAlso dtData.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                SetReportPath("rptEmployeeLeaveDeductionSummary.rpt")
                rptDoc.SetDataSource(dtData.DefaultView.ToTable())

                'Report Params
                Dim strCompanyName As String = objCommonFunctions.GetCompanyName(Convert.ToInt32(CompanyID))
                Dim rptValues As String = " "


                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("CompanyName", strCompanyName)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("ReportTitle", hdRptTitle.Value)
                rptDoc.SetParameterValue("DateTitle", "For The Month Of " & MonthName(ddlMonth.SelectedValue) & " - " & ddlyear.SelectedValue)

                Dim SO As System.Web.SessionState.HttpSessionState = Me.Page.Session
                objCommonFunctions.SetSessionObject(SO, hdRptTitle.Value, rptDoc)

                Return True

            Else
                lblError.Visible = True
                Dim strRun_NoRecordFound As String = ED.GetFieldText("Run_NoRecordFound")
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, IIf(strRun_NoRecordFound = "", "No Records found", strRun_NoRecordFound))
                Return False
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            lblError.Visible = True
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.MessageBoxBySM(Me.Page, ex.Message)
            Return False
        End Try

    End Function


    Private Function GenerateColumnToRowTable(ByVal inputTable As DataTable) As DataTable
        Dim outputTable As DataTable = New DataTable
        ' Add columns by looping rows
        ' Header row's first column is same as in inputTable
        outputTable.Columns.Add(inputTable.Columns(0).ColumnName.ToString)
        ' Header row's second column onwards, 'inputTable's first column taken
        For Each inRow As DataRow In inputTable.Rows
            Dim newColName As String = Format(CDate(inRow(0).ToString), "dd MMM")
            outputTable.Columns.Add(newColName)
        Next
        ' Add rows by looping columns        
        Dim rCount As Integer = 1
        Do While (rCount _
                    <= (inputTable.Columns.Count - 1))
            Dim newRow As DataRow = outputTable.NewRow
            ' First column is inputTable's Header row's second column
            newRow(0) = inputTable.Columns(rCount).ColumnName.ToString
            Dim cCount As Integer = 0
            Do While (cCount _
                        <= (inputTable.Rows.Count - 1))
                Dim colValue As String = inputTable.Rows(cCount)(rCount).ToString
                newRow((cCount + 1)) = colValue
                cCount = (cCount + 1)
            Loop
            outputTable.Rows.Add(newRow)
            rCount = (rCount + 1)
        Loop
        Return outputTable
    End Function
    Protected Sub ddlweek_SelectedIndexChanged(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As System.EventArgs) Handles ddlweek.SelectedIndexChanged

        Dim BOMonthWeek As New BO_MonthWeek
        Dim dt As DataTable = BOMonthWeek.tblMonthWeek.GetDatabyMonthYearCompany(ddlMonth.SelectedValue, ddlyear.SelectedValue, CompanyID)
        Dim dt2 As DataTable
        If ddlweek.SelectedValue = "All" Then
            If dt.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                dt.DefaultView.Sort = "StartDate ASC"
                txtFromDate.Text = DTHelper.ConvertToClientDateFormat(dt.Rows(0)("StartDate").ToString, strClientDateFormat)
                txtToDate.Text = DTHelper.ConvertToClientDateFormat(dt.Rows(dt.Rows.Count - 1)("EndDate").ToString, strClientDateFormat)
            End If
        Else
            If dt.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                dt.DefaultView.RowFilter = "Week = '" & ddlweek.SelectedValue & "' "
                dt2 = dt.DefaultView.ToTable
                If dt2.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                    txtFromDate.Text = DTHelper.ConvertToClientDateFormat(dt2.Rows(0)("StartDate").ToString, strClientDateFormat)
                    txtToDate.Text = DTHelper.ConvertToClientDateFormat(dt2.Rows(0)("EndDate").ToString, strClientDateFormat)
                End If
            End If
        End If

    End Sub
    Protected Sub ddlMonth_SelectedIndexChanged(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As System.EventArgs) Handles ddlMonth.SelectedIndexChanged

        Dim BOMonthWeek As New BO_MonthWeek
        Dim dt As DataTable = BOMonthWeek.tblMonthWeek.GetDatabyMonthYearCompany(ddlMonth.SelectedValue, ddlyear.SelectedValue, CompanyID)
        ddlweek.Items.Clear()
        If dt.Rows.Count > 0 Then

            ddlweek.DataTextField = "Week"
            ddlweek.DataValueField = "Week"
            ddlweek.DataSource = dt
            ddlweek.DataBind()

            ddlweek.Items.Insert(0, New ListItem("- All -", "All"))
            ddlweek_SelectedIndexChanged(New Object, EventArgs.Empty)
        Else
            ddlweek.Items.Insert(0, New ListItem("- Select -", "-1"))
        End If

    End Sub
    Private Sub CreateExcelFile(ByRef pdt As DataTable, ByVal fileName As String)

        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("EQU1-4YRI-KEYA-HERE")

        Dim ef As New ExcelFile
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = ef.Worksheets.Add("Employees")
        ws.InsertDataTable(pdt, "A1", True)

        Response.Clear()
        Dim attachment As String = "attachment; filename=" & fileName & ".xls"
        Response.ContentType = "application/vnd.ms-excel"
        Response.AddHeader("content-disposition", attachment)
        ef.SaveXls(Response.OutputStream)
        Response.End()

    End Sub

        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("EQU1-4YRI-KEYA-HERE")
        Dim ef As New ExcelFile, TotalExceptionRecord As System.Int64 = 0

        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = ef.Worksheets.Add(fileName)
        ws.InsertDataTable(pdt, "A1", True)

        fileName = Now.Hour & Now.Minute & Now.Second & Now.Millisecond & "_" & fileName & ".xls"

        Dim path As String = Server.MapPath("~/FTPUpload/" & fileName)
        hypDownloadTamplet.NavigateUrl = "FTPUpload/" & fileName
        ef.SaveXls(path)

    End Sub

    Public Sub AddHiddenField()
        ' hidCriteria.Value = ""
        For i As Integer = 0 To gvwPCriteria.Rows.Count - 1

            Dim chkInclude As Boolean = CType(gvwPCriteria.Rows(i).FindControl("chkInclude"), CheckBox).Checked
            If chkInclude = True Then
                Dim IsAlreadyExist As Boolean = False
                Dim hidField As HiddenField = CType(gvwPCriteria.Rows(i).FindControl("hidID"), HiddenField)
                Dim strCriteria() As String = hidCriteria.Value.ToString.Split("And")
                Dim strCriteria1() As String = hidCriteria.Value.ToString.Split("And ")
                If strCriteria.Length > 1 Then
                    For j As Integer = 1 To strCriteria.Length - 1
                        strCriteria(j) = Right(strCriteria(j), Len(strCriteria(j)) - 3)
                        Dim str1Criteria() As String = strCriteria(j).Split(" ")
                        Dim strTemp As String = strCriteria1(j).ToString
                        If str1Criteria(0).ToString = (ddlPCriteria.SelectedValue().ToString & "ID") Then
                            strCriteria1(j) = strTemp.Insert(strTemp.IndexOf("" & ddlPCriteria.SelectedValue.ToString & "ID IN (" & "") + ("" & ddlPCriteria.SelectedValue.ToString & "ID IN (" & "").Length, hidField.Value & ", ")
                            hidCriteria.Value = ""
                            For c As Integer = 1 To strCriteria.Length - 1
                                hidCriteria.Value = hidCriteria.Value & "A" & strCriteria1(c)
                            Next
                            IsAlreadyExist = True
                            GoTo ExitLoop
                        End If
                    Next
                    hidCriteria.Value = hidCriteria.Value & " And " & CStr(ddlPCriteria.SelectedValue) & "ID IN (" & hidField.Value & ")"
                    If IsAlreadyExist = False Then
                        hidNameStatus.Value = hidNameStatus.Value & "," & ddlPCriteria.SelectedItem.ToString & "IDs"
                    End If
ExitLoop:
                Else
                    hidCriteria.Value = hidCriteria.Value & " And " & CStr(ddlPCriteria.SelectedValue) & "ID IN (" & hidField.Value & ")"
                    If hidNameStatus.Value = "" Then
                        hidNameStatus.Value = ddlPCriteria.SelectedItem.ToString & "IDs"
                    Else
                        If IsAlreadyExist = False Then
                            hidNameStatus.Value = hidNameStatus.Value & "," & ddlPCriteria.SelectedItem.ToString & "IDs"
                        End If
                    End If
                End If



            End If

        Next
        gvwPCriteria.DataSource = Nothing
        gvwPCriteria.DataBind()
        lblAdditionalCriteria.Text = hidNameStatus.Value
        ' lblAdditionalCriteria1.Text = hidCriteria.Value
        ddlPCriteria.SelectedValue = 0
        trBtn.Visible = False
        pnlPCriteria.ScrollBars = ScrollBars.None
    End Sub

        ScriptManager.RegisterStartupScript(Me, Me.GetType(), "myClose", "myClose();", True)
        hidNameStatus.Value = ""
    End Sub

    Private Sub CreateEmployeeFilter(Optional ByVal pIsLeavedEmployeeRequired As Boolean = False, _
                                     Optional ByVal pCompanyID As System.Int32 = 0)

        If ddlCriteria.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "left employee" Or ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "left employee" Then
            objCommonFunctions.IsLeftEmployeeOnly = True
        Else
            objCommonFunctions.IsLeftEmployeeOnly = False
        End If

        If ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "left employee" Then
            objCommonFunctions.RptCriteria1 = "Employee"
        Else
            objCommonFunctions.RptCriteria1 = ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue
        End If

        If ddlCriteria.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "left employee" Then
            objCommonFunctions.RptCriteria = "Employee"
        Else
            objCommonFunctions.RptCriteria = ddlCriteria.SelectedValue
        End If

        'If ddlFrom.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower = "all" Then
        If ddlFrom.SelectedIndex = -1 And ddlCriteria.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "all" Then
            objCommonFunctions.RptCriteria = "All"
            ddlCriteria.SelectedValue = "All"
        End If

        If ddlCriteria.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "all" Then
            objCommonFunctions.RptFromCriteria = ""
            objCommonFunctions.RptToCriteria = ""
        Else
            If clsCookie.IsSimpleUser = True And ddlCriteria.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "employee" Then
                objCommonFunctions.RptFromCriteria = clsCookie.EmployeeCode
                objCommonFunctions.RptToCriteria = clsCookie.EmployeeCode
            Else
                objCommonFunctions.RptFromCriteria = ddlFrom.SelectedItem.GetValue("RefNo")
                objCommonFunctions.RptToCriteria = ddlTo.SelectedItem.GetValue("RefNo")
            End If
        End If

        If ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower <> "all" Then
            objCommonFunctions.RptFromCriteria1 = ddlFrom1.SelectedItem.GetValue("RefNo")
            objCommonFunctions.RptToCriteria1 = ddlTo1.SelectedItem.GetValue("RefNo")
        End If

        If pIsLeavedEmployeeRequired = True Then objCommonFunctions.IsLeftEmployeeOnly = True

        If pCompanyID = 0 Then
            If clsCookie.IsLeaveApprover = True AndAlso ddlCriteria.SelectedValue.ToLower = "all" AndAlso CBool(clsCookie.IsAdmin) <> True AndAlso CBool(clsCookie.IsBAdmin) <> True Then
                strEmployees = objCommonFunctions.CreateEmployeeListbyRefNoList(clsCookie.EmployeeID, CompanyID, clsCookie.Searchby)
            ElseIf clsCookie.IsLeaveApprover = True AndAlso ddlCriteria.SelectedValue.ToLower <> "all" AndAlso CBool(clsCookie.IsAdmin) <> True AndAlso CBool(clsCookie.IsBAdmin) <> True Then

                If IsNothing(ddlFrom.SelectedItem) = True Then
                    If ddlFrom.ClientValue.ToString = "1000000" Then
                        objCommonFunctions.RptFromCriteria = ""
                        objCommonFunctions.RptToCriteria = ""
                        objCommonFunctions.RptCriteria = "All"
                        strEmployees = objCommonFunctions.CreateEmployeeListbyRefNoList(clsCookie.EmployeeID, CompanyID, clsCookie.Searchby)
                    Else
                        objCommonFunctions.RptFromCriteria = clsCookie.EmployeeCode
                        objCommonFunctions.RptToCriteria = clsCookie.EmployeeCode
                        strEmployees = objCommonFunctions.CreateEmployeeListbyRefNo(CBool(clsCookie.IsBAdmin), "", , , , , False, , CompanyID)
                    End If
                Else
                    objCommonFunctions.RptFromCriteria = ddlFrom.SelectedItem.GetValue("RefNo")
                    objCommonFunctions.RptToCriteria = ddlFrom.SelectedItem.GetValue("RefNo")
                    strEmployees = objCommonFunctions.CreateEmployeeListbyRefNo(CBool(clsCookie.IsBAdmin), "", , , , , False, , CompanyID)
                End If
            ElseIf ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "all" Then
                strEmployees = objCommonFunctions.CreateEmployeeListbyRefNo(CBool(clsCookie.IsBAdmin), m_strBranchList, m_strGradeList, pIsLeavedEmployeeRequired, m_strDepartmentList, m_strDivisionList, , , CompanyID)
            Else
                strEmployees = objCommonFunctions.CreateEmployeeListbyRefNo2(CBool(clsCookie.IsBAdmin), m_strBranchList, m_strGradeList, pIsLeavedEmployeeRequired, m_strDepartmentList, m_strDivisionList, , , CompanyID)
            End If
        Else
            If ddlCriteria1.SelectedValue.ToString.ToLower = "all" Then
                strEmployees = objCommonFunctions.CreateEmployeeListbyRefNo(CBool(clsCookie.IsBAdmin), m_strBranchList, m_strGradeList, pIsLeavedEmployeeRequired, m_strDepartmentList, m_strDivisionList, , , pCompanyID)
            Else
                strEmployees = objCommonFunctions.CreateEmployeeListbyRefNo2(CBool(clsCookie.IsBAdmin), m_strBranchList, m_strGradeList, pIsLeavedEmployeeRequired, m_strDepartmentList, m_strDivisionList, , , pCompanyID)
            End If
        End If

        ''Additional Criteria
        'If hidCriteria.Value.ToString.Trim <> "" Then
        '    strEmployees = objCommonFunctions.ApplyAdditionalFilter(strEmployees, hidCriteria.Value, CompanyID)
        'End If

    End Sub
    Private Sub SetReportPath(ByVal pReportFileName As String)
        rptDoc = New ReportDocument
        Dim FilePath As String = Server.MapPath("Reports/" & pReportFileName)
        Try
            rptDoc.Load(FilePath)
        Catch ex As Exception
            EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.Update_LogDetail(-4, clsCookie.UserID, Request.UserHostAddress, "Load error: " & FilePath & " ex:" & ex.InnerException.Message, -6, clsCookie.UserName, CompanyID)
            If ex.InnerException.Message.Trim = ReportErrorMsg Then
                ReportCleanup(clsCookie.UserID, clsCookie.UserName, Request.UserHostAddress, CompanyID, 2, "Criteria Form: " & FilePath)
                rptDoc.Load(FilePath)
                EfroTech.clsGeneralFunctions.Update_LogDetail(-4, clsCookie.UserID, Request.UserHostAddress, "Load After Cleanup: " & FilePath, -6, clsCookie.UserName, CompanyID)
            Else
                Throw ex
            End If
        End Try
    End Sub
    Private Sub DisplayShiftDetail()
        Dim dtShift As New DataTable
        Dim boShift As New BO_Shift

        dtShift = boShift.tblShift.GetDataForSelectionCriteria(CompanyID)

        If dtShift.Rows.Count = 1 Then
            dtShift.Rows.Add(0, "None", "000 | None", "000", 0)
            dtShift.DefaultView.Sort = "ShiftID"
            gvwDetail.DataSource = dtShift.DefaultView.ToTable
            gvwDetail.DataBind()
            gvwDetail.Visible = True
            btnViewReport.Enabled = True
        ElseIf dtShift.Rows.Count >= 1 Then
            gvwDetail.DataSource = dtShift
            gvwDetail.DataBind()
            gvwDetail.Visible = True
            btnViewReport.Enabled = True
        Else
            dtShift.Rows.Add(0, "None", "000 | None", "000", 0)
            gvwDetail.DataSource = dtShift
            gvwDetail.DataBind()
            gvwDetail.Visible = True
            btnViewReport.Enabled = False
            Throw New Exception("First enter shift master data.")
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Function ShiftFilter(ByVal dsShift As DataSet) As String
        ShiftFilter = ""
        Dim i As Integer = 1, strFilter As String

        If dsShift.Tables("tblShiftData").Rows.Count > 1 Then
            strFilter = "( "
            For i = 0 To dsShift.Tables(0).Rows.Count - 1
                If dsShift.Tables("tblShiftData").Rows(i)("on") = "1" Then
                    strFilter = strFilter & dsShift.Tables("tblShiftData").Rows(i)("ShiftID") & ","
                End If
            Next
            strFilter = Left(strFilter, Len(strFilter) - 1) & ")"
        End If
        If Trim(strFilter) = "()" Then
            Return "(-1)"
        Else
            Return strFilter
        End If
    End Function

    End Function
    Private Function GetFromAndToDate(ByVal pYear As Int32, ByVal pMonth As Int32) As SortedList
        Dim bocompany As New BO_Company
        Dim dt As DataTable
        dt = bocompany.tblCompany.GetDatabyKey(CompanyID)
        Dim PayrollProcessStartDay As Int32, PayrollProcessEndDay As Int32
        Dim PayrollProcessStartType As String = "C", PayrollProcessEndType As String = "C"

        If dt.Rows.Count > 0 Then
            PayrollProcessStartDay = dt.Rows(0).Item("PayrollProcessStartDay")
            PayrollProcessEndDay = dt.Rows(0).Item("PayrollProcessEndDay")
            PayrollProcessStartType = dt.Rows(0).Item("PayrollProcessStartType")
            PayrollProcessEndType = dt.Rows(0).Item("PayrollProcessEndType")
        End If

        If PayrollProcessStartType <> "C" AndAlso PayrollProcessStartType <> "P" Then
            Throw New Exception("Payroll Cycle not defined in Company Setup")
        End If

        If PayrollProcessStartDay = 0 Or PayrollProcessEndDay = 0 Then
            Throw New Exception("Payroll Cycle not defined in Company Setup")
        End If

        Dim fromDate As Date, todate As Date
        Dim intMonthdays As Int32
        If PayrollProcessStartType = "C" AndAlso PayrollProcessEndType = "C" Then
            intMonthdays = Date.DaysInMonth(pYear, pMonth)
            If PayrollProcessStartDay > intMonthdays Then
                fromDate = New Date(pYear, pMonth, intMonthdays)
            Else
                fromDate = New Date(pYear, pMonth, PayrollProcessStartDay)
            End If

            If PayrollProcessEndDay > intMonthdays Then
                todate = New Date(pYear, pMonth, intMonthdays)
            Else
                todate = New Date(pYear, pMonth, PayrollProcessEndDay)
            End If

        ElseIf PayrollProcessStartType = "P" AndAlso PayrollProcessEndType = "C" Then
            Dim dattemp As New Date(pYear, pMonth, 1)
            dattemp = dattemp.AddMonths(-1)
            intMonthdays = Date.DaysInMonth(dattemp.Year, dattemp.Month)

            If PayrollProcessStartDay > intMonthdays Then
                fromDate = New Date(dattemp.Year, dattemp.Month, intMonthdays)
            Else
                fromDate = New Date(dattemp.Year, dattemp.Month, PayrollProcessStartDay)
            End If

            intMonthdays = Date.DaysInMonth(pYear, pMonth)
            If PayrollProcessEndDay > intMonthdays Then
                todate = New Date(pYear, pMonth, intMonthdays)
            Else
                todate = New Date(pYear, pMonth, PayrollProcessEndDay)
            End If
        End If

        Dim SR As New SortedList
        SR.Add("FromDate", fromDate)
        SR.Add("ToDate", todate)

        Return SR

    End Function
    Private Function GetPivotDataTable(ByVal table As DataTable, ByVal columnX As String, ByVal columnY As String, _
                           ByVal columnZ As String, ByVal nullValue As String, ByVal sumValues As Boolean, _
                           ByVal IsConcatenateValues As Boolean, ByVal IsConcatenateAndlinkValues As Boolean) As DataTable
        'Create a DataTable to Return
        Dim returnTable As New DataTable()

        If columnX = "" Then
            columnX = table.Columns(0).ColumnName
        End If


        'Add a Column at the beginning of the table
        returnTable.Columns.Add(columnY, System.Type.GetType(table.Columns(columnY).DataType.FullName))

        'Read all DISTINCT values from columnX Column in the provided DataTale
        Dim columnXValues As New List(Of String)()

        For Each dr As DataRow In table.Rows

            Dim columnXTemp As String = dr(columnX).ToString()
            If Not columnXValues.Contains(columnXTemp) Then
                'Read each row value, if it's different from others provided, add to the list of values and creates a new Column with its value.
                columnXValues.Add(columnXTemp)
                returnTable.Columns.Add(columnXTemp)
            End If
        Next

        'Verify if Y and Z Axis columns re provided
        If columnY <> "" AndAlso columnZ <> "" Then
            'Read DISTINCT Values for Y Axis Column
            Dim columnYValues As New List(Of String)()

            For Each dr As DataRow In table.Rows
                If Not columnYValues.Contains(dr(columnY).ToString()) Then
                    columnYValues.Add(dr(columnY).ToString())
                End If
            Next

            'Loop all Column Y Distinct Value
            For Each columnYValue As String In columnYValues
                'Creates a new Row
                Dim drReturn As DataRow = returnTable.NewRow()
                drReturn(0) = columnYValue
                'foreach column Y value, The rows are selected distincted
                Dim rows As DataRow() = table.[Select]((columnY & "='") + columnYValue & "'")

                'Read each row to fill the DataTable
                For Each dr As DataRow In rows
                    Dim rowColumnTitle As String = dr(columnX).ToString()

                    'Read each column to fill the DataTable
                    For Each dc As DataColumn In returnTable.Columns
                        If dc.ColumnName = rowColumnTitle Then
                            'If Sum of Values is True it try to perform a Sum
                            'If sum is not possible due to value types, the value displayed is the last one read
                            If sumValues Then
                                Try
                                    drReturn(rowColumnTitle) = Convert.ToDecimal(drReturn(rowColumnTitle)) + Convert.ToDecimal(dr(columnZ))
                                Catch
                                    drReturn(rowColumnTitle) = dr(columnZ)
                                End Try
                            ElseIf IsConcatenateAndlinkValues Then
                                'Dim strLinkText As String = "View" 'dr("DateOfUpload")
                                Dim strHypLink As String = dr(columnZ) '"<a href ="" & dr(columnZ) & "" target =""_blank"" >" & strLinkText & " </a><br/>"
                                drReturn(rowColumnTitle) = drReturn(rowColumnTitle) & strHypLink
                            ElseIf IsConcatenateValues Then
                                drReturn(rowColumnTitle) = drReturn(rowColumnTitle) & dr(columnZ)
                            Else
                                drReturn(rowColumnTitle) = dr(columnZ)
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next
                Next

                returnTable.Rows.Add(drReturn)

            Next
        Else
            Throw New Exception("The columns to perform inversion are not provided")
        End If

        'if a nullValue is provided, fill the datable with it
        If nullValue <> "" Then
            For Each dr As DataRow In returnTable.Rows
                For Each dc As DataColumn In returnTable.Columns
                    If dr(dc.ColumnName).ToString() = "" Then
                        dr(dc.ColumnName) = nullValue
                    End If
                Next
            Next
        End If

        Return returnTable
    End Function

#Region "DevEx DropDown"

    Protected Sub dxddl_OnItemsRequestedByFilterCondition(ByVal source As Object, ByVal e As ListEditItemsRequestedByFilterConditionEventArgs)
        Dim comboBox As ASPxComboBox = CType(source, ASPxComboBox)
        Dim dtTemp As New DataTable

        If ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value <> "All" Then

            If (clsCookie.IsAdmin = False And clsCookie.IsBAdmin = False) Then
                dtTemp = boEmployeeHelper.GetMasterDataForDevExpressCombo(ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value, CompanyID, _
                            CType(e.BeginIndex + 1, Int64), CType(e.EndIndex + 1, Int64), e.Filter, , , , clsCookie.EmployeeID, _
                            clsCookie.IsLeaveApprover, hidEmployeeHirarchy.Value)
            Else
                dtTemp = boEmployeeHelper.GetMasterDataForDevExpressCombo(ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value, CompanyID, _
                            CType(e.BeginIndex + 1, Int64), CType(e.EndIndex + 1, Int64), e.Filter)
            End If

            comboBox.DataSource = dtTemp
            comboBox.DataBind()
        End If
    End Sub
    Protected Sub dxddl_OnItemRequestedByValue(ByVal source As Object, ByVal e As ListEditItemRequestedByValueEventArgs)

        Dim value As Long = 0
        If e.Value Is Nothing OrElse (Not Int64.TryParse(e.Value.ToString(), value)) Then
            Return
        End If

        Dim comboBox As ASPxComboBox = CType(source, ASPxComboBox)
        'If comboBox.Text <> "0" Then value = e.Value

        Dim dtTemp As DataTable
        If ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value <> "All" Then
            If (clsCookie.IsAdmin = False And clsCookie.IsBAdmin = False) Then
                dtTemp = boEmployeeHelper.GetMasterDataForDevExpressComboByValue(ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value, CompanyID, e.Value.ToString, , , , clsCookie.EmployeeID, clsCookie.IsLeaveApprover)
            Else
                dtTemp = boEmployeeHelper.GetMasterDataForDevExpressComboByValue(ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value, CompanyID, e.Value.ToString)
            End If
            comboBox.DataSource = dtTemp
            comboBox.DataBind()

            If IsNothing(comboBox.Items.FindByValue(e.Value)) = False Then comboBox.Items.FindByValue(e.Value).Selected = True
        End If


    End Sub
    Protected Sub dxddlSub_OnItemsRequestedByFilterCondition(ByVal source As Object, ByVal e As ListEditItemsRequestedByFilterConditionEventArgs)

        Dim comboBox As ASPxComboBox = CType(source, ASPxComboBox)
        Dim dtTemp As New DataTable, strWhere As String = "", strFieldName As String = ""

        If ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value <> "All" Then

            strFieldName = ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value

            If (ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "employee" Or _
               ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "left employees") AndAlso _
               (ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower = "employee" Or _
               ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower = "left employees") Then

                If ddlFrom.SelectedIndex <> -1 And ddlTo.SelectedIndex <> -1 Then
                    If ddlFrom.SelectedItem.Value > 0 And ddlTo.SelectedItem.Value > 0 Then
                        strWhere = " And " & strFieldName & "ID >= " & ddlFrom.SelectedItem.Value & " And " & strFieldName & "ID <= " & ddlTo.SelectedItem.Value
                    Else
                        strWhere = ""
                    End If
                End If
            ElseIf (ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower = ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower) Then

                If ddlFrom.SelectedIndex <> -1 And ddlTo.SelectedIndex <> -1 Then
                    If ddlFrom.SelectedItem.Value > 0 And ddlTo.SelectedItem.Value > 0 Then
                        strWhere = " And " & strFieldName & "ID >= " & ddlFrom.SelectedItem.Value & " And " & strFieldName & "ID <= " & ddlTo.SelectedItem.Value
                    Else
                        strWhere = ""
                    End If
                End If
            End If

            If (clsCookie.IsAdmin = False And clsCookie.IsBAdmin = False) Then
                dtTemp = boEmployeeHelper.GetMasterDataForDevExpressCombo(ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value, CompanyID, _
                        CType(e.BeginIndex + 1, Int64), CType(e.EndIndex + 1, Int64), e.Filter, , , strWhere, clsCookie.EmployeeID, _
                        clsCookie.IsLeaveApprover, hidEmployeeHirarchy.Value)
            Else
                dtTemp = boEmployeeHelper.GetMasterDataForDevExpressCombo(ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value, CompanyID, _
                    CType(e.BeginIndex + 1, Int64), CType(e.EndIndex + 1, Int64), e.Filter, , , strWhere)
            End If

            comboBox.DataSource = dtTemp
            comboBox.DataBind()

        End If


    End Sub
    Protected Sub dxddlSub_OnItemRequestedByValue(ByVal source As Object, ByVal e As ListEditItemRequestedByValueEventArgs)

        Dim value As Long = 0, strWhere As String = "", strFieldName As String = ""

        If e.Value Is Nothing OrElse (Not Int64.TryParse(e.Value.ToString(), value)) Then
            Return
        End If
        Dim comboBox As ASPxComboBox = CType(source, ASPxComboBox)
        Dim dtTemp As DataTable
        If ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value <> "All" Then

            strFieldName = ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value

            If (ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "employee" Or _
                ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower <> "left employees") AndAlso _
                (ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower = "employee" Or _
                ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower = "left employees") Then

                If ddlFrom1.SelectedIndex <> -1 And ddlTo1.SelectedIndex <> -1 Then
                    If ddlFrom1.SelectedItem.Value > 0 And ddlTo1.SelectedItem.Value > 0 Then
                        strWhere = " And " & strFieldName & "ID >= " & ddlFrom1.SelectedItem.Value & " And " & strFieldName & "ID <= " & ddlTo1.SelectedItem.Value
                    End If
                End If

            ElseIf (ddlCriteria.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower = ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value.ToString.Trim.ToLower) Then

                If ddlFrom1.SelectedIndex <> -1 And ddlTo1.SelectedIndex <> -1 Then
                    If ddlFrom1.SelectedItem.Value > 0 And ddlTo1.SelectedItem.Value > 0 Then
                        strWhere = " And " & strFieldName & "ID >= " & ddlFrom1.SelectedItem.Value & " And " & strFieldName & "ID <= " & ddlTo1.SelectedItem.Value
                    Else
                        strWhere = ""
                    End If
                End If
            End If

            If (clsCookie.IsAdmin = False And clsCookie.IsBAdmin = False) Then
                dtTemp = boEmployeeHelper.GetMasterDataForDevExpressComboByValue(ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value, CompanyID, e.Value.ToString(), , , strWhere, clsCookie.EmployeeID, clsCookie.IsLeaveApprover)
            Else
                dtTemp = boEmployeeHelper.GetMasterDataForDevExpressComboByValue(ddlCriteria1.SelectedItem.Value, CompanyID, e.Value.ToString(), , , strWhere)
            End If

            comboBox.DataSource = dtTemp
            comboBox.DataBind()
        End If


    End Sub

#End Region

  
End Class
